EvoArena: Tracking Memory Evolution for Robust LLM Agents in Dynamic Environments
Authors: Jundong Xu, Qingchuan Li, Jiaying Wu, Yihuai Lan, Shuyue Stella Li, Huichi Zhou, Bowen Jiang, Lei Wang, Jun Wang, Anh Tuan Luu, Caiming Xiong, Hae Won Park, Bryan Hooi, Zhiyuan Hu
First: 2026-06-11T17:59:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:59:59+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language model (LLM) agents have achieved strong performance on a wide range of benchmarks, yet most evaluations assume static environments. In contrast, real-world deployment is inherently dynamic, requiring agents to continually align their knowledge, skills, and behavior with changing environments and updated task conditions. To address this gap, we introduce EvoArena, a benchmark suite that models environment changes as sequences of progressive updates across terminal, software, and social domains. We further propose EvoMem, a patch-based memory paradigm that records memory evolution as structured update histories, enabling agents to reason about environmental evolution through changes in their memory. Experiments show that current agents struggle on EvoArena, achieving an average accuracy of 39.6% across evolving terminal, software, and social-preference domains. EvoMem consistently improves performance, yielding an average gain of 1.5% on EvoArena and also improving standard benchmarks such as GAIA and LoCoMo by 6.1% and 4.8%. Beyond individual tasks, EvoMem further improves chain-level accuracy by 3.7% on EvoArena, where success requires completing a consecutive sequence of related evolutionary subtasks. Mechanistic analysis shows that EvoMem improves evidence capture in the memory, indicating better preservation of complete evolving environment states. Our results highlight the importance of modeling evolution in both evaluation and memory for reliable agent deployment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：EvoArena：追踪记忆演化以提升大语言模型代理在动态环境中的鲁棒性
大语言模型（LLM）代理已在多种基准测试中表现出色，但大多数评估假设环境静态。相比之下，现实部署本质上是动态的，要求代理持续调整其知识、技能和行为以适应变化的环境和更新的任务条件。为解决这一差距，我们引入EvoArena，一个将环境变化建模为终端、软件和社会领域渐进更新序列的基准测试套件。我们进一步提出EvoMem，一种基于补丁的记忆范式，将记忆演化记录为结构化的更新历史，使代理能够通过记忆变化推理环境演化。实验表明，当前代理在EvoArena上表现不佳，平均准确率仅为39.6%（涵盖终端、软件和社会偏好领域）。EvoMem持续提升性能，在EvoArena上平均提升1.5%，同时在GAIA和LoCoMo等标准基准上分别提升6.1%和4.8%。在链式任务层面，EvoMem在EvoArena上进一步提升准确率3.7%，该任务要求完成一系列连续的演化子任务。机制分析表明，EvoMem增强了记忆中的证据捕获，表明更完整地保留了演化环境状态。我们的结果强调了在评估和记忆中建模演化对于可靠代理部署的重要性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for LLM agents to adapt to dynamic environments, the paper introduces EvoArena, a benchmark that simulates evolving conditions across terminal, software, and social domains through progressive updates. To support this, it proposes EvoMem, a patch-based memory system that logs memory changes as structured histories, enabling agents to reason about environmental evolution. Experiments reveal that baseline agents perform poorly on EvoArena with only 39.6% average accuracy, while EvoMem boosts performance by 1.5% on EvoArena and improves standard benchmarks GAIA and LoCoMo by 6.1% and 4.8% respectively. It also enhances chain-level task accuracy by 3.7% and improves evidence retention in memory, demonstrating the value of modeling environmental evolution for robust agent behavior.
为应对大语言模型代理在动态环境中适应能力不足的问题，作者提出了EvoArena基准测试，模拟终端、软件与社交领域的渐进式环境变化。他们设计了EvoMem记忆机制，以结构化更新历史记录记忆演化，使代理能推理环境变迁。实验表明，基线模型在EvoArena上平均准确率仅为39.6%，而EvoMem提升1.5%，并在GAIA和LoCoMo基准上分别提升6.1%和4.8%。EvoMem还使链式任务准确率提升3.7%，并增强记忆中证据保留能力，证明其在建模演化环境方面的有效性。
Learning to Reason by Analogy via Retrieval-Augmented Reinforcement Fine-Tuning
Authors: Zilin Xiao, Qi Ma, Chun-cheng Jason Chen, Xintao Chen, Avinash Atreya, Hanjie Chen, Vicente Ordonez
First: 2026-06-11T17:59:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:59:52+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) has become a standard mechanism for grounding language models in external knowledge, yet conventional retrieval based on lexical or semantic similarity is poorly suited for complex reasoning tasks: a semantically similar problem may demand an entirely different solution strategy, while a superficially different problem may share the same underlying reasoning pattern. We propose Retrieval-Augmented Reinforcement Fine-Tuning (RA-RFT), a post-training framework that teaches language models to reason by analogy. RA-RFT uses gold-relevance distillation to train a retriever that ranks contexts by expected reasoning benefit rather than semantic overlap, and then fine-tunes the policy model via reinforcement fine-tuning methods with retrieved analogous demonstrations, so the model learns to leverage reasoning traces under verifiable outcome rewards. We further analyze the diversity of retrieved contexts and find that reasoning-aware retrieval surfaces complementary solution strategies that provide distinct reasoning scaffolds for individual problems. Across challenging mathematical reasoning benchmarks, RA-RFT consistently outperforms standard reinforcement fine-tuning methods. For example, it improves AIME 2025 average@32 accuracy by 7.1 and 2.8 points over GRPO for Qwen3-1.7B and Qwen3-4B respectively -- suggesting that reasoning-aware retrieval is a complementary axis of improvement and orthogonal to advances in reward design or training curricula.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过检索增强的强化微调学习类比推理
检索增强生成（RAG）已成为将语言模型与外部知识对齐的标准机制，但基于词典或语义相似性的传统检索方法难以应对复杂推理任务：语义相似的问题可能需要完全不同的解决方案，而表面不同的问题可能共享相同的推理模式。我们提出检索增强强化微调（RA-RFT），一种后训练框架，旨在教会语言模型通过类比进行推理。RA-RFT利用黄金相关性蒸馏训练检索器，按预期推理收益而非语义重叠对上下文进行排序，并通过检索到的类比演示进行强化微调，使模型学会在可验证结果奖励下利用推理轨迹。我们进一步分析检索上下文的多样性，发现推理感知检索能呈现互补的解决方案策略，为不同问题提供独特的推理支撑。在多个具有挑战性的数学推理基准测试中，RA-RFT始终优于标准强化微调方法。例如，在AIME 2025平均@32准确率上，相较于GRPO，Qwen3-1.7B和Qwen3-4B分别提升7.1和2.8分——表明推理感知检索是提升性能的互补维度，且与奖励设计或训练课程的改进正交。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of conventional retrieval methods in supporting complex reasoning, this work introduces Retrieval-Augmented Reinforcement Fine-Tuning (RA-RFT), which trains a retriever to prioritize contexts based on expected reasoning benefit rather than semantic similarity, and fine-tunes a language model using those analogous demonstrations via reinforcement learning. Experimental results show RA-RFT consistently outperforms standard reinforcement fine-tuning on mathematical reasoning benchmarks, improving AIME 2025 average@32 accuracy by 7.1 and 2.8 points for Qwen3-1.7B and Qwen3-4B respectively, demonstrating that reasoning-aware retrieval enhances performance independently of reward design or training curriculum.
为了解决传统检索方法在复杂推理任务中的不足，本文提出检索增强强化微调（RA-RFT）框架，通过检索类比示例训练语言模型进行类比推理。RA-RFT首先训练检索器根据预期推理收益而非语义相似性排序上下文，再通过强化学习使用这些检索到的示例微调语言模型，利用可验证的结果奖励引导推理。在多个数学推理基准测试中，RA-RFT表现优于标准强化微调方法，Qwen3-1.7B和Qwen3-4B在AIME 2025平均@32准确率上分别提升7.1和2.8分，表明推理感知检索是与奖励设计或训练课程改进互补的独立提升维度。
InterleaveThinker: Reinforcing Agentic Interleaved Generation
Authors: Dian Zheng, Harry Lee, Manyuan Zhang, Kaituo Feng, Zoey Guo, Ray Zhang, Hongsheng Li
First: 2026-06-11T17:59:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:59:50+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://zhengdian1.github.io/InterleaveThinker-proj/ Code: https://github.com/zhengdian1/InterleaveThinker
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
Recent image generators have demonstrated impressive photorealism and instruction-following capabilities in single-image generation and editing. However, constrained by their architectures, they cannot achieve interleaved generation (text-image sequence), which has crucial applications in visual narratives, guidance, and embodied manipulation. Even the latest open-source Unified Multimodal Models (UMMs) exhibit limited performance in this regard. In this paper, we introduce InterleaveThinker, the first multi-agent pipeline designed to endow any existing image generator with interleaved generation capabilities. Specifically, we employ a planner agent to organize the image-text input sequence, instructing the image generator on the required execution at each step. Subsequently, we introduce a critic agent to evaluate the generator's outputs, identify samples that deviate from the planned instructions, and refine the instructions for regeneration. To implement this pipeline, we construct the Interleave-Planner-SFT-80k and Interleave-Critic-SFT-112k to perform a format cold-start. Then we develop Interleave-Critic-RL-13k to reinforce the step-wise instruction correction capability within a generation trajectory using GRPO. Since a single interleaved generation trajectory may involve over 25 generator calls, optimizing the entire trajectory is computationally impractical. Therefore, we propose accuracy reward and step-wise reward, allowing single-step RL to effectively guide the entire generation trajectory. The results show that InterleaveThinker improves performance across various image generators. On interleaved generation benchmarks, it achieves performance comparable to Nano Banana and GPT-5. Surprisingly, it also significantly enhances the base model on reasoning-based benchmarks; for example, on 4-step FLUX.2-klein, we observe substantial gains on WISE and RISE.
中文标题/摘要
标题：InterleaveThinker：强化代理交错生成
近期图像生成器在单图像生成与编辑中展现了出色的逼真度和指令遵循能力。然而，受限于其架构，它们无法实现交错生成（文本-图像序列），这在视觉叙事、引导和具身操作中具有关键应用。即使最新的开源统一多模态模型（UMMs）在此方面表现也有限。本文提出InterleaveThinker，首个旨在赋予任何现有图像生成器交错生成能力的多代理管道。具体而言，我们采用规划代理组织图像-文本输入序列，指导图像生成器在每一步执行所需操作；随后引入批评代理评估生成器输出，识别偏离规划指令的样本，并优化指令以重新生成。为实现该管道，我们构建了Interleave-Planner-SFT-80k和Interleave-Critic-SFT-112k以进行格式冷启动，再开发Interleave-Critic-RL-13k，利用GRPO强化生成轨迹中逐步指令修正能力。由于单条交错生成轨迹可能涉及超过25次生成器调用，优化整个轨迹在计算上不现实。因此，我们提出准确度奖励和逐步奖励，使单步强化学习能有效引导整个生成轨迹。实验表明，InterleaveThinker在各类图像生成器上均提升性能。在交错生成基准测试中，其表现可媲美Nano Banana和GPT-5。令人惊讶的是，它还显著增强了基础模型在基于推理的基准测试中的表现；例如，在4步FLUX.2-klein上，我们在WISE和RISE上观察到显著提升。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of interleaved text-image generation in current multimodal models despite strong single-image capabilities, InterleaveThinker introduces a multi-agent pipeline to retrofit existing image generators. It uses a planner agent to sequence text-image inputs and a critic agent to evaluate and refine instructions step-by-step. Trained via supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning with GRPO, the system employs accuracy and step-wise rewards to guide long trajectories without full trajectory optimization. Experiments show it matches or exceeds Nano Banana and GPT-5 on interleaved benchmarks and significantly boosts reasoning performance on multi-step tasks like FLUX.2-klein.
鉴于当前多模态模型虽在单图生成方面表现优异，但缺乏文本与图像交替生成能力，InterleaveThinker提出一种多智能体框架，可为任意图像生成器赋予该能力。其通过规划智能体组织图文输入序列，批评智能体评估输出、修正指令并引导重生成，结合监督微调与基于GRPO的强化学习优化。为应对长生成轨迹的计算开销，采用分步奖励与准确率奖励机制。实验表明，该方法在交替生成基准上表现媲美Nano Banana与GPT-5，并显著提升基础模型在推理任务（如4步FLUX.2-klein）上的表现，尤其在WISE与RISE指标上取得明显进步。
Mana: Dexterous Manipulation of Articulated Tools
Authors: Zhao-Heng Yin, Guanya Shi, Pieter Abbeel, C. Karen Liu
First: 2026-06-11T17:59:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:59:49+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://zhaohengyin.github.io/mana
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Articulated tool manipulation remains a major challenge in dexterous robotics due to the need to coordinate internal degrees of freedom and contact-rich interactions. While prior work has largely focused on rigid objects, articulated tool use remains underexplored because of its physical complexity and the difficulty of learning functional grasping and manipulation policies. We present Mana (Manipulation Animator), a general sim-to-real framework that reinterprets dexterous manipulation as an animation problem. Inspired by computer animation, Mana employs a coarse-to-fine pipeline that transforms procedurally-generated grasp keyframes into manipulation trajectories through motion planning and reinforcement learning. The data generation process is largely automatic, requiring only a few mouse clicks to specify functional affordances (<1 minute per tool). Across four articulated tools spanning different scales and joint types, Mana achieves zero-shot sim-to-real transfer for both grasping and in-hand manipulation, demonstrating a scalable approach to dexterous articulated tool use.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Mana：灵巧操作铰接工具
由于需要协调内部自由度和丰富的接触交互，铰接工具操作在灵巧机器人领域仍是一个重大挑战。尽管先前工作主要集中在刚性物体上，但铰接工具的使用因物理复杂性和学习功能性抓取与操作策略的困难而研究较少。我们提出了Mana（Manipulation Animator），一个通用的仿真到现实框架，将灵巧操作重新诠释为动画问题。受计算机动画启发，Mana采用由粗到细的流程，通过运动规划和强化学习，将程序生成的抓取关键帧转化为操作轨迹。数据生成过程基本自动化，仅需几次鼠标点击即可指定功能用途（每工具不到一分钟）。在四种不同尺度和关节类型的铰接工具上，Mana实现了抓取和手内操作的零样本仿真到现实迁移，展示了一种可扩展的灵巧铰接工具使用方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty of coordinating internal tool joints and contact-rich interactions in dexterous robotics, the authors introduce Mana, a sim-to-real framework that treats articulated tool manipulation as an animation problem. Using a coarse-to-fine pipeline, Mana generates grasp keyframes procedurally and refines them into manipulation trajectories via motion planning and reinforcement learning, requiring only minimal user input to define functional affordances. Experimental results across four diverse articulated tools show Mana achieves zero-shot transfer for both grasping and in-hand manipulation, demonstrating scalability and generalization without task-specific tuning.
为解决关节工具操作中协调内部自由度与丰富接触交互的复杂性，作者提出Mana框架，将灵巧操作视为动画问题。该框架通过粗到细的流程，将程序化生成的抓取关键帧转化为操作轨迹，结合运动规划与强化学习，仅需少量鼠标点击即可定义工具的功能性操作需求。实验在四种不同尺度与关节类型的工具上验证，结果显示Mana可实现零样本仿真到现实的迁移，涵盖抓取与手内操作，展现出良好的可扩展性与泛化能力。
Improving Robotic Generalist Policies via Flow Reversal Steering
Authors: Andy Tang, William Chen, Andrew Wagenmaker, Chelsea Finn, Sergey Levine
First: 2026-06-11T17:59:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:59:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Generalist policies can learn a wide range of skills from diverse robot datasets. In order to solve or improve on challenging news tasks, we need a way to infer and invoke the appropriate actions from the policy's rich behavioral prior, especially when directly commanding the policy fails. We focus on flow matching generalists and propose Flow Reversal Steering (FRS): a method that takes suboptimal but ``reasonable'' actions, finds their latent noises by passing them through the flow policy in reverse, and maps them to nearby generalist action modes. We evaluate FRS across many simulated and real-world manipulation settings. First, FRS can turn coarse semantic guidance from humans or vision-language models (VLMs) into corresponding good robot actions, improving zero-shot control. These gains can be distilled with behavioral cloning by training an auxiliary policy to output noises that the generalist maps to good actions -- showing up to 95% absolute task success rate boosts in under a minute of training. Finally, FRS enables policy improvement by bootstrapping reinforcement learning with semantic knowledge, improving on several tasks that standard RL fails to improve on.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过流反转引导提升机器人通用策略
通用策略可从多样化的机器人数据集中学习广泛技能。为解决或改进新挑战任务，我们需要从策略丰富的行为先验中推断并调用适当动作，尤其当直接命令策略失败时。我们聚焦于流匹配通用策略，提出流反转引导（FRS）方法：该方法接受次优但“合理”的动作，通过反向流策略找到其潜在噪声，并将其映射到邻近的通用动作模式。我们在多种模拟和现实世界操作场景中评估FRS。首先，FRS可将人类或视觉-语言模型（VLM）提供的粗粒度语义引导转化为对应的良好机器人动作，提升零样本控制效果。这些收益可通过行为克隆蒸馏：训练辅助策略输出噪声，使通用策略映射为良好动作——在不到一分钟的训练中，任务成功率提升高达95%。最后，FRS通过语义知识引导强化学习，实现策略改进，优于标准RL在多个任务上的表现。
Summary / 总结
To enable robots to perform novel tasks using learned generalist policies, this work addresses the challenge of translating vague or suboptimal human or vision-language guidance into effective actions. It introduces Flow Reversal Steering (FRS), which reverses the flow policy to decode latent noise from observed actions and maps them to nearby action modes in the generalist policy’s latent space. Evaluated across simulated and real-world manipulation tasks, FRS successfully converts semantic guidance into high-performing robot actions, achieving up to 95% task success rate gains with behavioral cloning in under a minute of training. Additionally, FRS enhances reinforcement learning by bootstrapping it with semantic knowledge, enabling improvements on tasks where standard RL fails.
为解决直接指令失效时机器人如何利用其行为先验应对新任务的问题，本文提出流反转引导（FRS）方法：通过逆向流匹配将次优但合理的动作映射为潜在噪声，再投影到通用策略中的邻近动作模式。在模拟与真实操作任务中，FRS能将人类或视觉语言模型的粗粒度语义指令转化为有效机器人动作，实现零样本控制提升。通过行为克隆蒸馏，FRS可在不到一分钟训练内将任务成功率提升至95%。此外，FRS还能通过语义知识引导强化学习，提升标准RL难以改进的任务表现。
Modality Forcing for Scalable Spatial Generation
Authors: Bardienus Pieter Duisterhof, Deva Ramanan, Jeffrey Ichnowski, Justin Johnson, Keunhong Park
First: 2026-06-11T17:59:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:59:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Text-to-image (T2I) models contain rich spatial priors. Synthesizing photorealistic, cluttered scenes requires an understanding of geometry, including perspective and relative scale. Prior works adapt T2I models to leverage this prior for depth prediction, but they require dense depth data and involve complex recipes. We propose Modality Forcing, a simple, scalable post-training recipe for joint image-depth generation using a single DiT trained on sparse depth data. Modality Forcing enables conditional and joint generation of image and depth in any permutation by assigning separate noise levels per modality. Per-modality decoders let us train on sparse, real-world depth and achieve strong, generalizable depth prediction. We further show that Modality Forcing inherits the scalability of T2I pre-training: by training a set of T2I models from scratch (370M to 3.3B parameters), we find that larger models trained on more image data produce more accurate depth. Our strongest model is competitive with state-of-the-art monocular depth estimators and reduces AbsRel by 57% relative to existing joint image-depth generative models. These results provide strong evidence that image generation is a scalable pre-training objective for spatial perception. https://modality-forcing.github.io/
中文标题/摘要
标题：模态强制用于可扩展的空间生成
文本到图像（T2I）模型包含丰富的空间先验。合成逼真且杂乱的场景需要理解几何结构，包括透视和相对尺度。先前工作通过调整T2I模型利用该先验进行深度预测，但需密集深度数据且涉及复杂流程。我们提出模态强制（Modality Forcing），这是一种简单、可扩展的后训练方法，利用单个DiT模型在稀疏深度数据上联合生成图像与深度。模态强制通过为每种模态分配独立噪声水平，实现图像与深度的任意顺序条件联合生成。每模态解码器使我们能在稀疏真实世界深度数据上训练并获得强泛化能力的深度预测。我们进一步表明，模态强制继承了T2I预训练的可扩展性：通过从零训练一组T2I模型（3.7亿至33亿参数），发现更大模型在更多图像数据上训练可产生更准确的深度。我们的最强模型在单目深度估计器中表现与当前最先进方法相当，相较现有联合图像-深度生成模型，绝对相对误差（AbsRel）降低57%。这些结果有力证明图像生成是空间感知可扩展的预训练目标。https://modality-forcing.github.io/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to generate photorealistic, cluttered scenes with accurate spatial geometry, this work introduces Modality Forcing, a simple post-training method that enables joint image-depth generation using a single DiT model trained on sparse depth data. By assigning separate noise levels per modality and using per-modality decoders, the approach allows flexible conditional generation in any order and trains effectively on real-world sparse depth. Experimental results show that larger models trained on more image data yield more accurate depth predictions, with the strongest model outperforming existing joint image-depth generative models by 57% in AbsRel and matching state-of-the-art monocular depth estimators, demonstrating that image generation can serve as a scalable pre-training objective for spatial perception.
为生成具有准确空间几何结构的逼真复杂场景，本文提出Modality Forcing方法，这是一种基于单个DiT模型、仅需稀疏深度数据即可实现图像与深度联合生成的简单后训练方案。通过为不同模态分配独立噪声水平并使用模态特定解码器，该方法支持任意顺序的条件生成，同时在真实世界稀疏深度数据上训练。实验表明，参数规模更大、训练图像数据更多的DiT模型能生成更精确的深度预测，最强模型在绝对相对误差（AbsRel）上比现有联合生成模型降低57%，性能可媲美当前最先进的单目深度估计器。
RepWAM: World Action Modeling with Representation Visual-Action Tokenizers
Authors: Junke Wang, Qihang Zhang, Shuai Yang, Yiming Luo, Yujun Shen, Zuxuan Wu, Yu-Gang Jiang, Yinghao Xu
First: 2026-06-11T17:59:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:59:43+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://wdrink.github.io/RepWAM
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
This work presents RepWAM, a representation-centric world action model (WAM) built on representation visual-action tokenizers. Existing WAMs typically inherit reconstruction-oriented video tokenizers from pretrained video generation models. Although these tokenizers preserve visual fidelity, pixel reconstruction alone provides limited guidance for learning instruction-following dynamics that connect future prediction with robot control. To address this, we explore a semantic visual-action latent space for representation-centric world action modeling. Specifically, we train a representation visual-action tokenizer that maps visual inputs into aligned visual and latent action tokens. We then pretrain our WAM to jointly model future visual states and the latent actions that connect them under language instructions, followed by adaptation to real robot trajectories for closed-loop manipulation. Experiments on real-world manipulation tasks and simulation benchmarks show that RepWAM delivers strong performance across diverse manipulation settings, while ablations highlight the value of semantic visual-action tokenization over reconstruction-oriented alternatives. These results establish representation visual-action tokenization as a promising foundation for world action models and a step toward generalist robot policies. Code and weights will be available at https://github.com/wdrink/RepWAM.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RepWAM：基于表征视觉-动作分词器的世界动作建模
本文提出了RepWAM，一种以表征为中心的世界动作模型（WAM），其构建于表征视觉-动作分词器之上。现有WAM通常继承自预训练视频生成模型的重建导向视频分词器。尽管这些分词器保持了视觉保真度，但仅靠像素重建难以有效指导学习遵循指令的动态，以连接未来预测与机器人控制。为此，我们探索了面向表征中心的世界动作建模的语义视觉-动作潜在空间。具体而言，我们训练了一个表征视觉-动作分词器，将视觉输入映射为对齐的视觉与潜在动作分词。随后，我们预训练WAM以联合建模未来视觉状态与连接它们的潜在动作（在语言指令下），并进一步适配真实机器人轨迹以实现闭环操作。在真实世界操作任务和仿真基准测试中，RepWAM在多种操作场景下均表现出色，消融实验突显了语义视觉-动作分词相较于重建导向替代方案的价值。这些结果确立了表征视觉-动作分词作为世界动作模型的有前景基础，并迈向通用机器人策略的一步。代码和权重将在https://github.com/wdrink/RepWAM发布。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of reconstruction-focused video tokenizers in guiding instruction-following robot control, this work introduces RepWAM, a world action model that uses representation visual-action tokenizers to map visual inputs into aligned visual and latent action tokens. The model is pretrained to jointly predict future visual states and corresponding latent actions under language instructions, then adapted to real robot trajectories for closed-loop manipulation. Experimental results on real-world and simulation tasks demonstrate strong performance across diverse manipulation settings, with ablation studies confirming the superiority of semantic visual-action tokenization over pixel-reconstruction methods.
为了解决现有基于像素重建的视频标记器难以引导机器人按指令执行动作的问题，该研究提出了RepWAM，一种基于语义视觉-动作标记器的世界动作模型。该模型将视觉输入映射为对齐的视觉与潜在动作标记，并在语言指令下联合预测未来视觉状态与对应动作，随后适配真实机器人轨迹进行闭环操作。实验表明，该模型在多种现实与仿真操作任务中表现优异，消融实验进一步验证了语义视觉-动作标记化相比重建导向方法更具优势。
SpatialClaw: Rethinking Action Interface for Agentic Spatial Reasoning
Authors: Seokju Cho, Ryo Hachiuma, Abhishek Badki, Hang Su, Byung-Kwan Lee, Chan Hee Song, Sifei Liu, Subhashree Radhakrishnan, Seungryong Kim, Yu-Chiang Frank Wang, Min-Hung Chen
First: 2026-06-11T17:59:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:59:36+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://spatialclaw.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Spatial reasoning, the ability to determine where objects are, how they relate, and how they move in 3D, remains a fundamental challenge for vision-language models (VLMs). Tool-augmented agents attempt to address this by augmenting VLMs with specialist perception modules, yet their effectiveness is bounded by the action interface through which those tools are invoked. In this work, we study how the design of this interface shapes the agent's capacity for open-ended spatial reasoning. Existing spatial agents either employ single-pass code execution, which commits to a full analysis strategy before any intermediate result is observed, or rely on a structured tool-call interface that often offers less flexibility for freely composing operations or tailoring the analysis to each task. Both designs offer limited flexibility for open-ended, complex 3D/4D spatial reasoning. We therefore propose SpatialClaw, a training-free framework for spatial reasoning that adopts code as the action interface. SpatialClaw maintains a stateful Python kernel pre-loaded with input frames and a suite of perception and geometry primitives, letting a VLM-backed agent write one executable cell per step conditioned on all prior outputs, enabling the agent to flexibly compose and manipulate perception results and adapt its analysis to both intermediate text and visual observations and the demands of each problem. Evaluated across 20 spatial reasoning benchmarks spanning a broad range of static and dynamic 3D/4D spatial reasoning tasks, SpatialClaw achieves 59.9% average accuracy, outperforming the recent spatial agent by +11.2 points, with consistent gains across six VLM backbones from two model families without any benchmark- or model-specific adaptation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SpatialClaw：重新思考代理空间推理的动作接口
空间推理——即确定物体位置、相互关系及运动方式的能力——仍是视觉-语言模型（VLMs）的基本挑战。工具增强型代理试图通过为VLMs增加专业感知模块来解决此问题，但其效果受限于调用这些工具的动作接口。本研究探讨该接口设计如何影响代理进行开放性空间推理的能力。现有空间代理要么采用单次代码执行，即在观察任何中间结果前即承诺完整分析策略，要么依赖结构化工具调用接口，往往缺乏自由组合操作或针对每个任务定制分析的灵活性。这两种设计均难以支持开放性、复杂的3D/4D空间推理。因此，我们提出SpatialClaw，一种无需训练的空间推理框架，采用代码作为动作接口。SpatialClaw维护一个预加载输入帧及感知与几何原语的有状态Python内核，使基于VLM的代理能在每一步根据所有先前输出编写一个可执行单元，从而灵活组合与操作感知结果，并根据中间文本与视觉观察及每个问题的需求调整分析策略。在涵盖广泛静态与动态3D/4D空间推理任务的20个空间推理基准测试中，SpatialClaw平均准确率达59.9%，比最新空间代理高出11.2个百分点，在不进行任何基准或模型特定适配的情况下，于两个模型家族的六种VLM后端均保持一致提升。

SpatialClaw addresses this by introducing a training-free framework that uses code as its action interface, allowing a VLM-backed agent to write executable Python cells step-by-step, leveraging a stateful kernel with perception primitives and prior outputs to dynamically compose operations. Evaluated across 20 spatial reasoning benchmarks, SpatialClaw achieves 59.9% average accuracy, outperforming the previous state-of-the-art by 11.2 points, with consistent gains across six VLM backbones without any task- or model-specific tuning.
为解决现有工具增强代理在开放性三维/四维空间推理中的局限性，本文提出SpatialClaw，一个无需训练的框架，以代码作为其动作接口。该框架允许视觉语言模型逐步生成可执行的Python单元，借助预加载感知原语和输入帧的状态化内核，实现空间操作的灵活组合与对中间视觉及文本反馈的自适应调整。在20个空间推理基准测试中，SpatialClaw平均准确率达59.9%，比最新方法高出11.2个百分点，并在六种来自两个模型家族的视觉语言模型上均保持稳定提升，无需任何任务特定调优。
$\texttt{WEAVER}$, Better, Faster, Longer: An Effective World Model for Robotic Manipulation
Authors: Arnav Kumar Jain, Yilin Wu, Jesse Farebrother, Gokul Swamy, Andrea Bajcsy
First: 2026-06-11T17:59:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:59:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
The potential impacts of world models (WMs, i.e., learned simulators) on robotics are far-reaching -- policy evaluation, policy improvement, and test-time planning -- all with limited real-world interaction. To unlock these downstream capabilities, a WM needs to jointly satisfy three desiderata: $\textit{(i)}$ fidelity (i.e., producing simulated trajectories that correlate with reality), $\textit{(ii)}$ consistency (i.e., producing simulated trajectories that are coherent over long horizons), and $\textit{(iii)}$ efficiency (i.e., producing simulated trajectories quickly). We propose $\texttt{WEAVER}$ (World Estimation Across Views for Embodied Reasoning): a WM architecture that simultaneously achieves all three desiderata, providing state-of-the-art results on robotic manipulation tasks. $\texttt{WEAVER}$ is a multi-view WM trained to predict future latents and reward values via a flow-matching loss. We distill the key design decisions across model architecture, memory, and prediction objectives required to unlock the kinds of long-horizon dynamic manipulation tasks that have confounded prior world modeling approaches. We apply $\texttt{WEAVER}$ in robotic hardware, demonstrating its effectiveness at policy evaluation ($ρ$=0.870 correlation with real-world success rate), policy improvement (real-world success rate improvement of $38\%$ on top of the $π_{0.5}$ robot foundation model), and test-time planning (real-world success rate improvement of $14\%$ with a $5-10\times$ speedup over prior WMs). $\texttt{WEAVER}$ also demonstrates better performance than prior WMs when evaluated on out-of-distribution scenarios. Code, models, and videos at: https://arnavkj1995.github.io/WEAVER/ .
中文标题/摘要
标题：WEAVER：更优、更快、更持久：一种用于机器人操作的有效世界模型
世界模型（WM，即学习到的模拟器）对机器人技术的潜在影响深远——包括策略评估、策略改进和测试时规划，且均只需有限的真实世界交互。为解锁这些下游能力，世界模型需同时满足三个要求：(i) 真实性（即生成的模拟轨迹与现实相关），(ii) 一致性（即生成的模拟轨迹在长时域内保持连贯），(iii) 效率（即快速生成模拟轨迹）。我们提出WEAVER（用于具身推理的多视角世界估计）：一种同时实现上述三项要求的世界模型架构，在机器人操作任务上达到最先进水平。WEAVER是一种多视角世界模型，通过流匹配损失预测未来潜在变量和奖励值。我们提炼了模型架构、记忆机制和预测目标的关键设计决策，以解锁此前世界建模方法难以处理的长时域动态操作任务。我们在机器人硬件上应用WEAVER，证明其在策略评估（与真实成功率相关系数ρ=0.870）、策略改进（在π₀.₅机器人基础模型之上提升38%真实成功率）和测试时规划（在比先前世界模型快5-10倍的前提下提升14%真实成功率）方面的有效性。WEAVER在分布外场景评估中也优于先前世界模型。代码、模型和视频见：https://arnavkj1995.github.io/WEAVER/。

Designed to simultaneously satisfy fidelity, consistency, and efficiency, WEAVER achieves state-of-the-art performance on robotic manipulation tasks. Experimental results show it improves real-world success rates by 38% for policy improvement and 14% for test-time planning with 5–10x speedup over prior models, while also outperforming existing world models in out-of-distribution scenarios.
为支持机器人在有限真实交互下进行策略评估、改进与规划，作者提出WEAVER，一种通过流匹配损失训练的多视角世界模型，用于预测未来潜变量与奖励。该模型同时满足保真度、一致性与效率三大要求，达到机器人操作任务的最先进性能。在真实硬件实验中，它在策略评估上与真实成功率相关性达0.870，策略改进提升38%（基于π₀.₅基础模型），测试时规划成功率提升14%，速度比前代模型快5–10倍，且在分布外场景中表现更优。
Understanding Truncated Positional Encodings for Graph Neural Networks
Authors: James Flora, Mitchell Black, Weng-Keen Wong, Amir Nayyeri
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-06-11T17:58:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:58:56+00:00
Comments: 28 pages, 4 figures, ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Positional encodings (PEs) enhance the power of graph neural networks (GNNs), both theoretically and empirically. Two of the most popular families of PEs - spectral (e.g., Laplacian eigenspaces, effective resistance) and walk-based (polynomials of the adjacency matrix) - are theoretically equivalent in expressive power, with expressivity between the 1-WL and 3-WL tests. However, this equivalence assumes the GNN uses the "complete" version of these PEs, which requires $O(n^3)$ time and space complexity. Instead, practitioners commonly use truncated variants of these encodings, such as the first $k$ eigenspaces or powers of the adjacency matrix. However, the theoretical properties of these truncated PEs are unknown. In this work, we initiate the study of these truncated PEs. Theoretically, we show that, under truncation, several families of PEs are fundamentally different in expressive power. As a corollary, we show that truncated spectral PEs are no longer stronger than the 1-WL test. We also study a family of spectral PEs, the $k$-harmonic distances, to highlight the differences in expressive power of even closely related truncated PEs. Finally, we experimentally show that a mix of truncated PEs is preferable to any single family on real-world datasets.
中文标题/摘要
标题：理解图神经网络中的截断位置编码
位置编码（PEs）在理论上和实证上都能增强图神经网络（GNNs）的能力。两种最流行的PE家族——谱编码（如拉普拉斯特征空间、有效电阻）和基于游走的编码（邻接矩阵多项式）——在表达能力上理论上等价，介于1-WL与3-WL测试之间。然而，这种等价性假设GNN使用这些PE的“完整”版本，需要$O(n^3)$的时间和空间复杂度。实践中，人们通常使用这些编码的截断变体，如前$k$个特征空间或邻接矩阵的幂。但这些截断PE的理论性质尚不明确。本文首次研究这些截断PE。理论上，我们证明，在截断条件下，若干类PE在表达能力上根本不同。作为推论，我们证明截断谱PE不再强于1-WL测试。我们还研究了一类谱PE——$k$-谐波距离，以突出即使高度相关的截断PE在表达能力上的差异。最后，我们在真实数据集上实验证明，混合使用截断PE优于单一PE家族。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates truncated positional encodings in graph neural networks, motivated by the practical use of truncated versions despite their lack of theoretical understanding. They introduce k-harmonic distances as a case study to demonstrate expressive differences among similar truncated encodings. Experiments on real-world datasets confirm that combining multiple truncated PE families outperforms using any single family.
该论文研究图神经网络中截断位置编码的理论与实践表现，动机在于实际应用中常使用计算效率更高的截断版本而非完整编码。作者理论证明截断会破坏谱编码与行走编码在表达能力上的等价性，并表明截断谱编码弱于1-WL测试。通过k-谐波距离等案例，揭示了相近截断编码间表达能力的差异。实验表明，混合多种截断编码在真实数据集上优于单一编码方案。
Automated reproducibility assessments in the social and behavioral sciences using large language models
Authors: Tobias Holtdirk, Pietro Marcolongo, Anna Steinberg Schulten, Felix Henninger, Stefan Rose, Sarah Ball, Bolei Ma, Frauke Kreuter, Markus Weinmann, Stefan Feuerriegel
First: 2026-06-11T17:58:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:58:36+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reproducibility in the social and behavioral sciences is typically evaluated by independent researchers who reanalyze the original data to assess whether the published findings can be recovered. However, such approaches are resource-intensive and difficult to scale. Here, we show that large language models (LLMs) can automate reproducibility assessments. Using N=76 published studies with predefined claims from the behavioral and social sciences, we compare LLM-generated analysis with the original findings and human reanalysis. For 7 studies, the LLM could not produce a viable effect size estimate. For the remaining studies, our LLM pipeline recovered the original effect sizes in 41% of studies using a +/-0.05 tolerance in Cohen's d. Further, our LLM pipeline reached the same qualitative conclusion as the original study in 96% of cases, where conclusions indicate whether the reanalysis supports the original claim. For comparison, human reanalysts recovered the original effect sizes in 34% of studies and reached the same qualitative conclusion in 74% of cases. Together, these results show that LLMs can serve as a scalable tool for automated reproducibility assessment and provide a foundation for systematic auditing of empirical results in the social and behavioral sciences.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用大型语言模型在社会科学和行为科学中实现自动化可重复性评估
社会科学和行为科学中的可重复性通常由独立研究人员重新分析原始数据来评估，以判断已发表的发现是否可重现。然而，此类方法资源密集且难以扩展。本文表明，大型语言模型（LLMs）可自动化可重复性评估。我们使用76项来自行为与社会科学的已发表研究，其研究声明已预先定义，比较了LLM生成的分析结果与原始发现及人工重新分析。在7项研究中，LLM无法生成可行的效果量估计；其余研究中，我们的LLM流程在Cohen's d ±0.05容差范围内，41%的研究恢复了原始效应量。此外，LLM流程在96%的案例中得出与原始研究相同的定性结论（即是否支持原始主张）。相比之下，人工重新分析仅在34%的研究中恢复原始效应量，且在74%的案例中得出相同定性结论。这些结果表明，LLM可作为可扩展的自动化可重复性评估工具，并为社会科学和行为科学实证结果的系统性审计奠定基础。

Motivated by the high cost and scalability challenges of manual reproducibility assessments in social and behavioral sciences, this study explores using large language models (LLMs) to automate such evaluations. The method involves applying LLMs to 76 published studies with predefined claims, comparing their generated effect size estimates and qualitative conclusions against original findings and human reanalyses.
