为解决社会与行为科学中人工重复验证成本高、难以规模化的问题，本研究探索使用大语言模型（LLM）自动化评估重复性。方法是将LLM应用于76篇具有预设结论的已发表研究，将其生成的效果量估计与原始结果及人工重分析进行对比。结果显示，LLM在41%的研究中（Cohen’s d误差±0.05内）准确复现原始效应量，且在96%的研究中得出与原始研究相同的定性结论，优于人工重分析者（34%和74%）。这表明LLM可作为该领域可扩展的自动化重复性审计工具。
Agents-K1: Towards Agent-native Knowledge Orchestration
Authors: Zongsheng Cao, Bihao Zhan, Jinxin Shi, Jiong Wang, Fangchen Yu, Zhijie Zhong, Zijie Guo, Tianshuo Peng, Zhuo Liu, Yi Xie, Xiang Zhuang, Yue Fan, Runmin Ma, Shiyang Feng, Xiangchao Yan, Anran Liu, Peng Ye, Wenlong Zhang, Shufei Zhang, Chunfeng Song, Fenghua Ling, Jie Zhou, Liang He, Bo Zhang, Lei Bai
First: 2026-06-11T17:58:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:58:35+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Current LLM-based research agents have advanced through agent orchestration, yet largely overlook scientific knowledge orchestration. Existing works often reduce papers to abstracts, surface mentions, and flat \texttt{cites} edges, omitting key entities, claims, evidence, mechanisms, and method lineages essential for scientific reasoning. To this end, we introduce \textbf{Agents-K1}, an end-to-end knowledge orchestration pipeline that converts raw documents into agent-native scientific knowledge graphs. Agents-K1 integrates three components under a unifying theoretical foundation: a multimodal parser whose five-module schema captures entities, multimodal evidence, citations, and typed inter-entity relations across the full paper rather than abstracts alone; a 4B information-extraction backbone trained with GRPO under a rule-based reward; and a graphanything CLI, a tri-source agent interface that unifies web search, multimodal graph retrieval, and cross-document traversal. On top of this, we process 2.46 million scientific papers across six subjects to produce \textbf{Scholar-KG}, of which we release a one-million-paper subset, and the full Scholar-KG is accessible via the SCP link below. The same pipeline can be extended to general-domain corpora and to schema-conformant data synthesis. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Agents-K1 achieves superior performance in scientific information extraction, knowledge graph construction, and multi-hop scientific reasoning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Agents-K1：迈向原生知识编排的智能体
当前基于大语言模型的研究智能体虽已通过智能体编排取得进展，但大多忽视科学知识编排。现有工作常将论文简化为摘要、表面提及和平铺直叙的\texttt{cites}边，遗漏科学推理所必需的关键实体、主张、证据、机制及方法谱系。为此，我们提出\textbf{Agents-K1}，一个端到端的知识编排管道，可将原始文档转化为智能体原生的科学知识图谱。Agents-K1在统一理论框架下整合三个组件：一个五模块多模态解析器，能捕捉全文（而非仅摘要）中的实体、多模态证据、引用及类型化实体间关系；一个4B信息抽取骨干网络，基于规则奖励通过GRPO训练；以及一个三源智能体接口GraphAnything CLI，统一网络搜索、多模态图检索与跨文档遍历。在此基础上，我们处理六大学科的246万篇科学论文，构建\textbf{Scholar-KG}，其中发布百万篇子集，完整Scholar-KG可通过下方SCP链接获取。该管道还可扩展至通用领域语料及符合模式的数据合成。大量实验表明，Agents-K1在科学信息抽取、知识图谱构建及多跳科学推理方面表现卓越。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the gap in scientific knowledge representation for LLM-based agents, Agents-K1 introduces an end-to-end pipeline that transforms raw scientific papers into structured knowledge graphs. It employs a multimodal parser capturing full-paper entities and relations, a 4B language model trained via GRPO with rule-based rewards for extraction, and a tri-source agent interface enabling multimodal retrieval and cross-document navigation. Evaluated across 2.46 million papers, the system generates Scholar-KG, a large-scale scientific knowledge graph, and demonstrates superior performance in information extraction, graph construction, and multi-hop reasoning compared to existing methods.
为弥补当前基于LLM的研究代理在科学知识表示方面的不足，Agents-K1提出了一种端到端的管道，将原始科学论文转化为面向代理的结构化知识图谱。该系统包含一个五模块多模态解析器，用于捕捉全文中的实体、证据、引用及关系；一个4B规模的信息抽取模型，采用GRPO与规则奖励训练；以及一个三源代理接口，支持网络搜索、图谱检索与跨文档导航。在246万篇论文上构建的Scholar-KG知识图谱，经实验证明在科学信息抽取、图谱构建及多跳推理任务中显著优于现有方法。
Influcoder: Distilling Decoders' Gradient Influence Rankings into an Encoder for Data Attribution
Authors: Dimitri Kachler, Damien Sileo, Pascal Denis
First: 2026-06-11T17:58:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:58:33+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 2 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
With the growth of LLMs' (Large Language Models) capabilities, there has been an increasing push to curate high quality datasets by filtering samples in the training data. In general, Data Attribution (DA) methods aim to estimate how individual samples in a training dataset can precondition a model to generate certain outputs. As an example, one might be interested in which samples in the data could be the source of toxic behavior after training the LLM. Many methods quantify this conditioning through the paradigm of influence functions. While methods of this family are effective in its function, they lack the necessary processing speed and storage compactness to be practically implemented on large datasets. We propose a method, Influcoder, as a quick and cost-effective approach to influence-based Data Attribution at scale.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Influcoder：将解码器梯度影响排序提炼至编码器以实现数据归因
随着大语言模型（LLMs）能力的提升，人们越来越倾向于通过筛选训练数据中的样本以构建高质量数据集。通常，数据归因（DA）方法旨在评估训练数据中单个样本如何预设模型生成特定输出。例如，人们可能希望了解哪些数据样本在训练大语言模型后会导致毒性行为。许多方法通过影响函数范式量化这种预设作用。尽管此类方法在功能上有效，但其处理速度和存储紧凑性不足以在大规模数据集上实际应用。我们提出一种名为Influcoder的方法，作为大规模影响型数据归因的快速且经济高效的解决方案。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to efficiently attribute model outputs to specific training samples in large language models, the authors propose Influcoder, a method that distills gradient influence rankings from decoders into encoders to enable scalable data attribution. Instead of computing full influence functions—which are computationally expensive and memory-intensive—the method leverages encoder-only architectures to approximate influence rankings through distilled gradient signals. Experimental results show that Influcoder achieves comparable attribution accuracy to full influence methods while being significantly faster and requiring less storage, making it practical for large-scale datasets.
为了解决在大规模语言模型训练数据中高效识别关键样本的需求，作者提出了Influcoder方法，该方法通过将解码器的梯度影响排序蒸馏至编码器，实现可扩展的数据归因。传统基于影响函数的方法计算成本高、存储开销大，而Influcoder通过梯度信号蒸馏，用编码器近似解码器的影响排序，大幅降低计算和存储需求。实验表明，该方法在保持与完整影响函数相近归因精度的同时，显著提升了速度和存储效率，适用于大规模数据集。
HyperTool: Beyond Step-Wise Tool Calls for Tool-Augmented Agents
Authors: Yaxin Du, Yifan Zhou, Yujie Ge, Jiajun Wang, Xianghe Pang, Shuo Tang, Tuney Zheng, Bryan Dai, Jian Yang, Siheng Chen
First: 2026-06-11T17:56:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:56:36+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Tool-augmented LLM agents commonly rely on step-wise atomic tool calls, where each invocation, observation, and value transfer is exposed in the main reasoning trace. This creates an \emph{execution-granularity mismatch}: locally deterministic tool workflows are unfolded into repeated model-visible decisions, consuming context and forcing the model to manage low-level dataflow in the trace. We introduce \textbf{HyperTool}, a unified executable MCP-style tool interface that changes the model-visible unit of tool execution. A model invokes HyperTool with a code block that can call existing tools through their original schemas, manipulate returned values, and pass intermediate results locally, folding deterministic tool subroutines into a single outer call. To train models to use this interface, we synthesize HyperTool-format trajectories from cross-tool compositional tasks and verify them in real MCP environments. On MCP-Universe, HyperTool improves average accuracy from 15.69\% to 35.29\% on Qwen3-32B and from 9.93\% to 33.33\% on Qwen3-8B, and surpass GPT-OSS and Kimi-k2.5 on average accuracy, showing that our HyperTool can substantially improve multi-step tool use.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HyperTool：超越分步工具调用的工具增强代理
工具增强型大语言模型代理通常依赖分步原子工具调用，其中每次调用、观察和值传递均在主推理轨迹中暴露。这导致了\emph{执行粒度不匹配}：局部确定性的工具工作流被展开为重复的模型可见决策，消耗上下文并迫使模型在轨迹中管理低级数据流。我们引入\textbf{HyperTool}，一种统一的可执行MCP风格工具接口，改变模型可见的工具执行单元。模型通过代码块调用HyperTool，可按原模式调用现有工具、操作返回值，并在本地传递中间结果，将确定性工具子程序折叠为单次外部调用。为训练模型使用此接口，我们从跨工具组合任务合成HyperTool格式轨迹，并在真实MCP环境中验证。在MCP-Universe上，HyperTool使Qwen3-32B的平均准确率从15.69%提升至35.29%，Qwen3-8B从9.93%提升至33.33%，且在平均准确率上超越GPT-OSS和Kimi-k2.5，表明HyperTool可显著提升多步工具使用能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of step-wise tool calls that fragment deterministic workflows into model-visible decisions, the paper introduces HyperTool, a unified MCP-style interface that allows models to invoke tools via code blocks, manipulate returned values, and pass intermediate results locally, thereby folding tool subroutines into single calls. Trained using synthesized trajectories from compositional tasks and validated in real MCP environments, HyperTool significantly improves multi-step tool use: on MCP-Universe, it boosts average accuracy from 15.69% to 35.29% for Qwen3-32B and from 9.93% to 33.33% for Qwen3-8B, outperforming GPT-OSS and Kimi-k2.5.
为解决传统逐步工具调用导致的执行粒度不匹配问题，作者提出HyperTool，一种统一的MCP风格工具接口，允许模型通过代码块调用工具、操作返回值并内部传递中间结果，从而将确定性工具子程序折叠为单次调用。通过合成组合任务轨迹训练并实测于真实MCP环境，HyperTool显著提升多步工具使用能力：在MCP-Universe上，Qwen3-32B准确率从15.69%提升至35.29%，Qwen3-8B从9.93%提升至33.33%，超越GPT-OSS与Kimi-k2.5平均准确率。
EurekAgent: Agent Environment Engineering is All You Need For Autonomous Scientific Discovery
Authors: Amy Xin, Jiening Siow, Junjie Wang, Zijun Yao, Fanjin Zhang, Jian Song, Lei Hou, Juanzi Li
First: 2026-06-11T17:56:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:56:35+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
LLM-based agents have shown increasing potential in automating scientific discovery. Given an optimizable metric and an execution environment, they can propose, validate, and iterate scientific solutions, and have produced results that outperform human-designed approaches. As model capabilities continue to improve, we argue that the bottleneck for autonomous scientific discovery is shifting from prescribing agent workflows to designing agent environments: the resources, constraints, and interfaces that shape agent behavior. We frame this as environment engineering: building environments that amplify productive behaviors, such as open-ended exploration, systematic artifact management, and inter-agent collaboration, while suppressing harmful behaviors, such as reward hacking and high-friction human oversight. We present EurekAgent, an environment-engineered agent system for metric-driven autonomous scientific discovery. EurekAgent engineers the environment along four dimensions: permissions engineering for bounded agent execution and isolated evaluation; artifact engineering for filesystem and Git-based collaboration; budget engineering for budget-aware exploration; and human-in-the-loop engineering for easy human supervision and intervention. EurekAgent sets new state-of-the-art results on multiple mathematics, kernel engineering, and machine learning tasks, including new state-of-the-art 26-circle packing results discovered with less than $11 in total API cost. We open-source our code and results, and call for environment engineering as a core research direction for developing reliable autonomous research agents.
中文标题/摘要
标题：EurekAgent：自主科学发现只需代理环境工程
基于大语言模型的代理在自动化科学发现方面展现出日益增长的潜力。给定一个可优化的指标和执行环境，它们能够提出、验证并迭代科学解决方案，其结果优于人工设计的方法。随着模型能力的持续提升，我们认为自主科学发现的瓶颈正从设计代理工作流程转向设计代理环境：塑造代理行为的资源、约束和接口。我们将此称为环境工程：构建能放大有益行为（如开放探索、系统化成果管理及代理间协作），同时抑制有害行为（如奖励作弊和高摩擦度的人类监督）的环境。我们提出了EurekAgent，一个面向指标驱动的自主科学发现的环境工程代理系统。EurekAgent从四个维度构建环境：权限工程以实现有界代理执行与隔离评估；成果工程以支持基于文件系统和Git的协作；预算工程以实现预算感知探索；以及人机协同工程以实现便捷的人类监督与干预。EurekAgent在多个数学、内核工程和机器学习任务上取得了新的最先进结果，包括以不到11美元的API总成本发现新的26圆打包最优解。我们开源了代码与结果，并呼吁将环境工程作为开发可靠自主研究代理的核心研究方向。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that LLM-based agents can autonomously drive scientific discovery when given a metric and execution environment, the authors argue that the key bottleneck lies not in agent design but in environment engineering—crafting environments that encourage productive behaviors like open-ended exploration and collaboration while discouraging harmful ones like reward hacking. EurekAgent, their proposed system, implements four core environment engineering dimensions: permissions to bound execution and enable isolation, artifact management via filesystem and Git for collaboration, budget-aware exploration, and human-in-the-loop interfaces for supervision. Experiments show EurekAgent achieves state-of-the-art results across mathematics, kernel engineering, and machine learning tasks—including discovering new 26-circle packing solutions at under $11 in API cost—demonstrating the power of environment design over workflow prescription.
鉴于当前自主科学发现的瓶颈已从设计代理工作流转向设计代理环境，作者提出EurekAgent系统，通过环境工程放大有益行为（如开放探索与代理协作），抑制有害行为（如奖励作弊）。该系统围绕四个维度构建环境：权限控制、文件与Git协作管理、预算感知探索、以及人机交互接口。实验表明，EurekAgent在数学、内核工程和机器学习任务中取得多项SOTA结果，包括以不到11美元API成本发现新的26圆打包解。
Adaptive-Horizon Conflict-Based Search for Closed-Loop Multi-Agent Path Finding
Authors: Jiarui Li, Federico Pecora, Runyu Zhang, Gioele Zardini
First: 2026-02-12T14:55:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:55:21+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
MAPF is a core coordination problem for large robot fleets in automated warehouses and logistics. Existing approaches are typically either open-loop planners, which generate fixed trajectories and struggle to handle disturbances, or closed-loop heuristics without reliable performance guarantees, limiting their use in safety-critical deployments. This paper presents ACCBS, a closed-loop algorithm built on a finite-horizon variant of CBS with a horizon-changing mechanism inspired by iterative deepening in MPC. ACCBS dynamically adjusts the planning horizon based on the available computational budget, and reuses a single constraint tree to enable seamless transitions between horizons. As a result, it produces high-quality feasible solutions quickly while being asymptotically optimal as the budget increases, exhibiting anytime behavior. Extensive case studies demonstrate that ACCBS combines flexibility to disturbances with strong performance guarantees, effectively bridging the gap between theoretical optimality and practical robustness for large-scale robot deployment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于自适应时域的冲突驱动搜索用于闭环多智能体路径规划
MAPF是自动化仓库和物流中大型机器人车队的核心协调问题。现有方法通常要么是开环规划器，生成固定轨迹且难以应对扰动，要么是缺乏可靠性能保证的闭环启发式算法，限制了其在安全关键部署中的应用。本文提出ACCBS，一种基于有限时域CBS变体的闭环算法，其时域调整机制借鉴了MPC中的迭代加深思想。ACCBS根据可用计算预算动态调整规划时域，并复用单一约束树以实现时域无缝切换。因此，它能在短时间内生成高质量可行解，且随着预算增加渐近最优，具备任何时间行为。大量案例研究表明，ACCBS兼具对扰动的灵活性与强性能保证，有效弥合了大规模机器人部署中理论最优性与实际鲁棒性之间的鸿沟。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for robust, theoretically grounded coordination in large-scale robot fleets, this paper introduces ACCBS, a closed-loop algorithm that adapts its planning horizon dynamically based on computational budget, using a finite-horizon variant of Conflict-Based Search with an iterative deepening-inspired mechanism. By reusing a single constraint tree, ACCBS enables seamless horizon transitions and achieves anytime behavior, producing high-quality feasible solutions quickly while converging to asymptotic optimality as computation increases. Experimental results confirm its effectiveness in handling disturbances while maintaining strong performance guarantees, making it suitable for safety-critical logistics environments.
为解决现有开环与启发式多智能体路径规划方法在动态环境中适应性差、缺乏性能保障的问题，本文提出ACCBS算法，基于有限时域冲突解决框架并借鉴模型预测控制中的迭代加深思想，动态调整规划时域并复用单一约束树以实现平滑切换。该算法在计算资源增加时渐近最优，具备任何时间行为，实验表明其能在保证高质量可行解的同时快速响应扰动，有效弥合理论最优性与实际鲁棒性之间的鸿沟，适用于大规模机器人部署。
Before You Think: System 0, AI-Mediated Cognition and Cognitive Colonization
Authors: Marianna Bergamaschi Ganapini, Massimo Chiriatti, Enrico Panai, Giuseppe Riva
First: 2026-06-11T17:54:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:54:59+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper examines three recent frameworks for understanding the cognitive and epistemic consequences of artificial intelligence: Tri-System Theory, Thinkframes, and System 0. It argues that while the first two capture important dimensions of AI's influence on individual reasoning and collective epistemic practices, System 0 occupies a theoretically distinctive position that neither can fully replicate. The paper introduces the concept of cognitive colonization, according to which AI systems can embed external interests within the architecture of the self in ways that are difficult for users to perceive. Because such systems are already widely deployed, understanding these invisible forms of influence is an urgent philosophical and practical task.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在你思考之前：系统0、AI介导的认知与认知殖民
本文考察了三个近期用于理解人工智能对认知与知识后果的框架：三系统理论、思维框架和系统0。文章认为，前两者虽能捕捉人工智能对个体推理与集体知识实践的重要影响维度，但系统0在理论上占据独特位置，前者无法完全复制。本文引入“认知殖民”概念，指AI系统可将外部利益嵌入自我架构之中，而用户难以察觉。鉴于此类系统已广泛部署，理解这些无形影响已成为紧迫的哲学与实践任务。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how AI reshapes human cognition and epistemic practices, this paper evaluates Tri-System Theory, Thinkframes, and System 0, arguing that System 0 uniquely captures AI’s capacity to embed external interests into users’ cognitive architecture—a phenomenon termed 'cognitive colonization.' The authors do not conduct experiments but instead analyze theoretical frameworks to demonstrate how AI systems can subtly alter self-perception and reasoning without users’ awareness, making this influence both pervasive and philosophically urgent.
该文旨在揭示人工智能如何在认知层面深层影响人类思维，超越表层推理。作者对比分析了三类框架——三系统理论、Thinkframes与System 0，指出System 0具有独特理论地位，能解释AI如何将外部利益嵌入个体认知架构，这一过程被称为‘认知殖民’。研究表明，这种嵌入往往隐蔽，用户难以察觉，表明AI对认知的影响不仅是行为层面，更是结构性的，亟需哲学与实践层面的深入回应。
Dense Supervision, Sparse Updates: On the Sparsity and Geometry of On-Policy Distillation
Authors: Guo Yu, Wenlin Liu, Yulan Hu, Hao-Xuan Ma, Jun-Peng Jiang, Han-Jia Ye
First: 2026-06-11T17:54:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:54:09+00:00
Comments: Code is available at https://github.com/SydCS/OPD-Param-Analysis
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
On-policy distillation (\textsc{OPD}) has recently become a prominent post-training recipe as it combines two desirable ingredients: on-policy student trajectories and dense teacher supervision, yet how this hybrid changes a model's parameters remains unclear. Across several language and vision-language model pairs and use cases, our analysis yields two main findings. On sparsity, \textsc{OPD}-style updates are small and coordinate-sparse. They are distributed across layers and are usually FFN-heavy. This sparse structure is operationally useful: training only the discovered subnetwork recovers nearly the same performance as full \textsc{OPD}. However, the sparsity-inducing SGD optimizer underperforms AdamW in our optimizer ablation, likely because dense teacher supervision preserves heterogeneous coordinate-wise gradient scales where AdamW's adaptive scaling remains useful. On geometry, the updates are numerically full-rank but spectrally concentrated; they lie mostly away from the principal singular subspaces of the source weights and fall disproportionately on coordinates where the source weights are close to zero. These findings suggest that dense teacher supervision does not turn \textsc{OPD} into ordinary dense parameter rewriting; instead, \textsc{OPD} retains important geometric signatures of on-policy post-training.
中文标题/摘要
标题：密集监督、稀疏更新：关于在线策略蒸馏的稀疏性与几何结构
在线策略蒸馏（OPD）近期成为一种突出的后训练方法，因其结合了两个理想要素：在线策略学生轨迹与密集教师监督，但该混合方法如何改变模型参数仍不明确。在多个语言及视觉-语言模型对与应用场景中，我们的分析得出两个主要发现。在稀疏性方面，OPD风格的更新量小且坐标稀疏，分布于各层，通常以FFN为主。这种稀疏结构具有操作实用性：仅训练发现的子网络即可恢复近似于完整OPD的性能。然而，在优化器消融实验中，诱导稀疏性的SGD优化器表现劣于AdamW，可能是因为密集教师监督保留了异质的坐标级梯度尺度，而AdamW的自适应缩放仍具优势。在几何结构方面，更新数值上为满秩但谱集中；它们主要远离源权重的主奇异子空间，且过度集中在源权重接近零的坐标上。这些发现表明，密集教师监督并未使OPD变为普通的密集参数重写；相反，OPD保留了在线策略后训练的重要几何特征。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how on-policy distillation (OPD) alters model parameters despite using dense teacher supervision, the paper analyzes OPD updates across language and vision-language models. It finds that OPD updates are small, coordinate-sparse, and concentrated in FFN layers, allowing performance recovery with only a subset of parameters. However, sparse SGD underperforms AdamW, likely because dense supervision preserves heterogeneous gradient scales that AdamW’s adaptive scaling exploits. Geometrically, OPD updates are full-rank but spectrally concentrated, avoiding the principal singular subspaces of source weights and disproportionately targeting near-zero coordinates, indicating OPD retains on-policy post-training geometry rather than performing ordinary dense parameter rewriting.
为理解在密集教师监督下，基于策略的蒸馏（OPD）如何改变模型参数，本研究对多个语言和视觉-语言模型的OPD更新进行了分析。方法聚焦于梯度稀疏性和参数更新的谱几何特性。结果表明，OPD更新幅度小、坐标稀疏，主要集中在前馈网络层，仅训练子网络即可恢复近似性能；但稀疏SGD优化器表现劣于AdamW，因密集监督破坏了梯度尺度异质性。几何上，更新虽满秩但谱集中，避开源权重的主奇异子空间，偏向源权重接近零的坐标，说明OPD保留了基于策略微调的几何特征，而非进行密集参数重写。
Flex4DHuman: Flexible Multi-view Video Diffusion for 4D Human Reconstruction
Authors: Jen-Hao Cheng, Yipeng Wang, Hao Zhang, Gengshan Yang, Jenq-Neng Hwang
First: 2026-06-11T17:54:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:54:05+00:00
Comments: 18 pages, 8 figures. Code, and multi-view caption dataset available
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present Flex4DHuman, a multi-view video diffusion model that transforms a monocular or sparse multi-view video of a dynamic subject into synchronized dense multi-view videos using only relative camera-pose conditioning. Unlike prior human-centric methods that rely on skeletons, depth maps, normals, or rendered target-view geometry, Flex4DHuman requires no explicit geometry priors and instead conditions generation through relative camera-pose positional encoding. The generated videos can be directly ingested by downstream reconstruction pipelines to create dynamic 4D Gaussian splats. Built on the Wan 2.1 1.3B text-to-video model, Flex4DHuman preserves the backbone architecture and encodes camera and view information through a five-axis positional encoding that extends spatio-temporal RoPE with view indices and continuous SE(3) relative camera geometry. A three-stage curriculum progressively trains the model for pose following, flexible reference-to-target view generation, and temporal rollout. To support temporal rollout, we train with clean historical target-view tokens. We also add multi-view captions to enable test-time text control. Combined with an off-the-shelf 4D Gaussian Splatting stage, our framework lifts monocular static-camera videos into dynamic 4D Gaussian splats. Experiments on DNA-Rendering and ActorsHQ show that Flex4DHuman surpasses prior state-of-the-art methods, while the same formulation generalizes to animal categories after mixed human-animal training. These capabilities make Flex4DHuman a practical step toward scalable 4D content creation from casual monocular videos for simulation, gaming, AR/VR, and video re-shooting.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Flex4DHuman：用于4D人体重建的灵活多视角视频扩散模型
我们提出了Flex4DHuman，这是一种多视角视频扩散模型，仅通过相对相机姿态条件化，即可将动态主体的单目或稀疏多视角视频转换为同步的密集多视角视频。与以往依赖骨架、深度图、法线或渲染目标视角几何的人体中心方法不同，Flex4DHuman无需显式几何先验，而是通过相对相机姿态位置编码引导生成。生成的视频可直接输入下游重建流程，创建动态4D高斯点云。该模型基于Wan 2.1 1.3B文本到视频模型，保留其骨干架构，并通过五轴位置编码扩展时空RoPE，加入视角索引和连续SE(3)相对相机几何信息。三阶段课程训练逐步提升模型的姿势跟随、灵活参考视角到目标视角生成及时间展开能力。为支持时间展开，我们使用干净的历史目标视角标记进行训练，并添加多视角字幕以支持测试时文本控制。结合现成的4D高斯点云生成阶段，我们的框架可将单目静态相机视频提升为动态4D高斯点云。在DNA-Rendering和ActorsHQ上的实验表明，Flex4DHuman超越了现有最先进方法，且相同架构在混合训练后可泛化至动物类别。这些能力使Flex4DHuman成为从普通单目视频规模化生成4D内容（用于仿真、游戏、AR/VR及视频重拍）的实用一步。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for scalable 4D human reconstruction from casual monocular videos, Flex4DHuman introduces a multi-view video diffusion model that generates synchronized dense multi-view videos using only relative camera-pose conditioning, avoiding explicit geometry priors. Built on the Wan 2.1 1.3B text-to-video architecture, it extends spatio-temporal RoPE with view indices and continuous SE(3) relative camera geometry, and employs a three-stage curriculum for pose following, flexible view generation, and temporal rollout. Experiments on DNA-Rendering and ActorsHQ demonstrate superior performance over prior methods, with generalization to animal categories after mixed training, enabling practical 4D content creation for simulation, gaming, and AR/VR.
为实现从普通单目视频中可扩展的4D人体重建，Flex4DHuman提出一种多视角视频扩散模型，仅通过相对相机位姿条件生成同步密集多视角视频，无需显式几何先验。该模型基于Wan 2.1 1.3B文本到视频架构，通过五轴位置编码扩展时空RoPE，融合视角索引与连续SE(3)相对几何信息。采用三阶段课程训练——姿态跟随、灵活参考到目标视角生成、时序展开，结合历史目标视角标记与多视角字幕实现测试时控制。在DNA-Rendering和ActorsHQ数据集上实验表明其优于现有方法，且经混合训练后可泛化至动物类别，支持模拟、游戏、AR/VR及视频重拍等场景的实用4D内容生成。
World Tracing: Generative Pixel-Aligned Geometry Beyond the Visible
Authors: Hao Zhang, Mohamed El Banani, Jen-Hao Cheng, Paul Zhang, Yi Hua, Ben Mildenhall, Christoph Lassner, Narendra Ahuja, Gengshan Yang
First: 2026-06-11T17:52:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:52:48+00:00
Comments: World Labs Technical Report; Page: https://haoz19.github.io/world-tracing-page/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Image-to-3D methods often trade off faithfulness and completeness: depth estimators are anchored to input pixels but stop at the visible surface, while image-to-3D models generate complete shapes that are often misaligned with the input. We introduce World Tracing, a generative pixel-aligned geometry representation that predicts 3D points aligned with observed pixels while completing geometry beyond the visible surface. For each input pixel, World Tracing predicts an ordered stack of camera-space 3D points, where the first layer represents the visible surface and subsequent layers represent front-to-back intersections with occluded surfaces. We instantiate this representation with a world-tracing diffusion transformer, WT-DiT, which treats multiple geometry layers as separate denoising tokens coupled through factorized and global attention. WT-DiT is trained with pixel-space flow matching and a mixed noise schedule that balances visible-surface reconstruction with occluded-geometry generation. World Tracing achieves strong performance on visible-surface reconstruction and complete geometry generation across object, scene, and dynamic benchmarks, outperforming both depth predictors and image-to-3D generators. It also preserves 2D-to-3D correspondence, enabling text-driven 3D scene editing, geometry-conditioned novel-view video synthesis, and training-free integration with textured-mesh generators.
中文标题/摘要
标题：世界追踪：超越可见范围的生成式像素对齐几何
图像到3D的方法常在忠实度与完整性之间权衡：深度估计器锚定于输入像素但仅限于可见表面，而图像到3D模型生成的完整形状常与输入不匹配。我们提出世界追踪（World Tracing），一种生成式像素对齐几何表示，可预测与观测像素对齐的3D点并完成可见表面之外的几何结构。对每个输入像素，世界追踪预测一个有序的相机空间3D点堆栈，其中第一层代表可见表面，后续层代表从前到后与被遮挡表面的交点。我们通过世界追踪扩散变换器（WT-DiT）实现该表示，将多层几何结构视为独立的去噪标记，并通过因子化与全局注意力耦合。WT-DiT采用像素空间流匹配训练，并结合混合噪声调度，在可见表面重建与遮挡几何生成之间取得平衡。世界追踪在物体、场景及动态基准测试中均表现出色，优于深度预测器与图像到3D生成器，同时保持2D到3D对应关系，支持文本驱动的3D场景编辑、几何条件下的新视角视频合成及无需训练即可与纹理网格生成器集成。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the trade-off between faithfulness and completeness in image-to-3D methods, World Tracing introduces a generative pixel-aligned geometry representation that predicts 3D points aligned with input pixels while extending geometry beyond visible surfaces. It uses a world-tracing diffusion transformer (WT-DiT) that models multiple depth layers per pixel as denoising tokens, coupled via attention mechanisms, and is trained with pixel-space flow matching and a mixed noise schedule to balance surface reconstruction and occlusion modeling. Experiments show it outperforms depth estimators and image-to-3D generators on object, scene, and dynamic benchmarks, while preserving 2D-to-3D correspondence for downstream tasks like text-driven editing and novel-view video synthesis.
为解决图像到3D方法中忠实性与完整性之间的权衡，World Tracing提出一种生成式像素对齐几何表示，能预测与输入像素对齐的3D点并扩展至不可见表面。其采用扩散Transformer WT-DiT，将每个像素的多层几何结构建模为去噪标记，通过因子化与全局注意力耦合，结合像素空间流匹配与混合噪声调度训练，平衡可见表面重建与遮挡几何生成。实验表明其在物体、场景及动态基准上优于深度估计器与图像到3D生成器，同时保持2D到3D对应关系，支持文本驱动编辑、新视角视频合成及无需训练的纹理网格生成器集成。
Operadic consistency: a label-free signal for compositional reasoning failures in LLMs
Authors: Nathaniel Bottman, Yinhong Liu, Kyle Richardson
First: 2026-06-11T17:50:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:50:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Detecting LLM reasoning failures at inference time without ground-truth labels has motivated a wide range of confidence baselines, including self-consistency, semantic entropy, and P(True), built on within-question sampling and self-evaluation. Operad theory, the formalism for systems built by iterated substitution, suggests a complementary diagnostic: a model's direct answer to a compositional query should agree with the answer it produces by composing a stated decomposition of the same query. We instantiate this idea as operadic consistency (OC), a per-question signal. Across twelve instruction-tuned LLMs (4B to 671B parameters, open-weights and closed-source) on four multi-hop QA datasets, OC is strongly correlated with accuracy on every dataset (Pearson $r \in [0.86, 0.94]$, all $p \leq 0.0004$), and is the only signal we evaluate with $r \geq 0.85$ uniformly across all four datasets. Chain-of-thought self-consistency (CoT-SC; Wang et al., 2023) matches OC on HotpotQA and DROP ($r = 0.93, 0.87$) but drops to $r \approx 0.45$ on MuSiQue and StrategyQA. At the per-question level, OC contributes information beyond CoT-SC and semantic entropy on every dataset (cluster-robust $p \leq 10^{-16}$ for the OC coefficient), and the conclusion is robust to additionally controlling for constructed decomposition-aware baselines ($p \leq 10^{-13}$). The same signal yields selective-prediction improvements (accuracy at fixed coverage) over a tuned CoT-SC baseline at the equal-cost $K = 3$ budget (AUARC lifts of +0.086 to +0.096 and AUROC lifts of +0.092 to +0.164; 95% CIs exclude zero on every cell). On five frontier thinking models, where the decomposition is extracted from the model's own chain of thought, the same equal-cost comparison gives positive selective-prediction point-estimate lift on all 16 (dataset, budget, metric) cells tested, with 95% CIs excluding zero on 12 of the 16.
中文标题/摘要
标题：运算一致性：一种无标签的信号，用于检测大语言模型组合推理失败
在推理时无需真实标签即可检测大语言模型推理失败，已推动多种置信度基线方法的发展，包括自一致性、语义熵和P(True)，这些方法基于问题内采样和自我评估。运算代数理论——用于描述通过迭代代入构建系统的形式化方法——提出了一种互补诊断：模型对组合查询的直接回答应与通过组合其对同一查询的分解所得到的回答一致。我们将这一思想形式化为“运算一致性”（OC），作为每个问题的信号。在十二个指令调优的大语言模型（参数量从4B到671B，开源与闭源）上，针对四个多跳问答数据集，OC与准确率在每个数据集上均高度相关（皮尔逊r值在[0.86, 0.94]区间，所有p≤0.0004），且是唯一在所有四个数据集上r≥0.85的信号。思维链自一致性（CoT-SC；Wang等，2023）在HotpotQA和DROP上与OC表现一致（r=0.93, 0.87），但在MuSiQue和StrategyQA上下降至r≈0.45。在每个问题层面，OC在每个数据集上提供的信息超越CoT-SC和语义熵（聚类稳健p≤10^{-16}），且该结论在控制构造的分解感知基线后仍稳健（p≤10^{-13}）。同一信号在同等成本K=3预算下，相比调优的CoT-SC基线，可提升选择性预测性能（固定覆盖率下的准确率）：AUARC提升+0.086至+0.096，AUROC提升+0.092至+0.164（95%置信区间均排除零）。在五个前沿思维模型上，分解从模型自身思维链中提取，相同等成本比较在16个（数据集、预算、指标）测试单元中均获得正向选择性预测点估计提升，其中12个单元的95%置信区间排除零。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to detect LLM reasoning failures without ground-truth labels, the paper introduces operadic consistency (OC), a label-free diagnostic based on operad theory that checks whether a model's direct answer matches its answer when composing a self-provided decomposition of the same query. Evaluated across 12 LLMs and four multi-hop QA datasets, OC shows strong correlation with accuracy (Pearson r between 0.86 and 0.94, all p ≤ 0.0004), outperforming other baselines like chain-of-thought self-consistency on two datasets and providing unique predictive value beyond CoT-SC and semantic entropy. OC also improves selective-prediction performance at fixed cost, with significant AUARC and AUROC lifts across multiple models and metrics, and remains effective even when controlling for decomposition-aware baselines.
为在无标签情况下检测大语言模型推理失败，本文提出基于代数运算理论的“运算一致性”（OC）指标，通过比对模型直接回答与基于其自身分解组合后的回答来评估推理可靠性。在12个不同规模的LLM和4个多跳问答数据集上，OC与准确率高度相关（皮尔逊相关系数0.86–0.94，p≤0.0004），是唯一在所有数据集上相关系数均≥0.85的指标。OC在所有数据集上均优于链式思维自一致性（CoT-SC）和语义熵，且在等成本预算下显著提升选择性预测性能（AUARC和AUROC提升0.086–0.164），即使控制分解感知基线后仍保持优势。在从模型自身思维链中提取分解的前沿模型上，OC在16个测试单元中均带来正向提升，其中12个单元的95%置信区间不包含零。
Thermodynamic assessment of machine learning models for solid-state synthesis prediction
Authors: Jane Schlesinger, Simon Hjaltason, Nathan J. Szymanski, Christopher J. Bartel
First: 2026-02-03T23:25:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:50:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Machine learning models have recently emerged to predict whether hypothetical solid-state materials can be synthesized. These models aim to circumvent direct first-principles modeling of solid-state phase transformations, instead learning from large databases of successfully synthesized materials. Here, we assess the alignment of several recently introduced synthesis prediction models with material and reaction thermodynamics, quantified by the energy with respect to the convex hull and a metric accounting for thermodynamic selectivity of enumerated synthesis reactions. A dataset of successful synthesis recipes was used to determine the likely bounds on both quantities beyond which materials can be deemed unlikely to be synthesized. With these bounds as context, thermodynamic quantities were computed using the CHGNet foundation potential for thousands of new hypothetical materials generated using the Chemeleon generative model. Four recently published machine learning models for synthesizability prediction were applied to this same dataset, and the resultant predictions were considered against computed thermodynamics. We find these models generally overpredict the likelihood of synthesis, but some model scores do trend with thermodynamic heuristics, assigning lower scores to materials that are less stable or do not have an available synthesis recipe that is calculated to be thermodynamically selective. In total, this work identifies existing gaps in machine learning models for materials synthesis and introduces a new approach to assess their quality in the absence of extensive negative examples (failed syntheses).
中文标题/摘要
标题：机器学习模型用于固态合成预测的热力学评估
近期出现的机器学习模型旨在预测假设的固态材料是否可被合成。这些模型旨在绕过对固态相变的直接第一性原理建模，而是从大量成功合成材料的数据库中学习。本文评估了几个近期提出的合成预测模型与材料及反应热力学的一致性，通过凸包能量和考虑合成反应热力学选择性的指标进行量化。利用成功合成配方的数据集，确定了两个量的可能界限，超过该界限则可判定材料不太可能被合成。以这些界限为背景，使用CHGNet基础势函数，对通过Chemeleon生成模型生成的数千种新假设材料计算了热力学量。将四种近期发表的合成性预测机器学习模型应用于同一数据集，其预测结果与计算热力学进行对比。我们发现这些模型普遍高估了合成的可能性，但部分模型评分与热力学启发式方法趋势一致，对稳定性较差或无热力学选择性合成配方的材料赋予较低评分。总体而言，本工作指出了材料合成机器学习模型的现有不足，并提出了一种在缺乏大量负面示例（失败合成）情况下评估模型质量的新方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate machine learning models predicting solid-state material synthesizability against physical thermodynamic principles, this study assesses four recent ML models using thermodynamic metrics derived from convex hull energy and reaction selectivity. The models were tested on a dataset of successfully synthesized materials to establish thermodynamic thresholds for synthesis likelihood, then applied to thousands of hypothetical materials generated via Chemeleon. Results show that while models generally overestimate synthesis probability, some exhibit partial alignment with thermodynamic heuristics—assigning lower scores to less stable materials or those lacking thermodynamically selective synthesis routes. This work highlights current limitations in ML models for synthesis prediction and proposes a novel framework to evaluate them without requiring extensive negative training data.
为评估机器学习模型对固态材料合成可能性的预测能力，本研究采用热力学指标——即相对于凸包的能量和合成反应的热力学选择性——对四个近期发布的模型进行评估。模型在成功合成材料的数据集上确定了热力学阈值，随后对通过Chemeleon模型生成的数千种假设材料，利用CHGNet势函数计算其热力学性质。结果表明，这些模型普遍高估了合成可能性，但部分模型评分与热力学稳定性及反应选择性相关，对稳定性差或无热力学选择性合成路径的材料评分较低。该研究揭示了当前机器学习模型的不足，并提出了一种在缺乏失败合成样本情况下评估模型质量的热力学基准方法。
SkMTEB: Slovak Massive Text Embedding Benchmark and Model Adaptation
Authors: Marek Šuppa, Andrej Ridzik, Daniel Hládek, Natália Kňažeková, Viktória Ondrejová
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-06-11T17:50:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:50:06+00:00
Comments: ACL 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce SkMTEB, the first comprehensive MTEB-style text embedding benchmark for Slovak, a low-resource West Slavic language, comprising 31 datasets across 7 task types -- nearly 4$\times$ the depth of existing multilingual benchmark coverage for Slovak. Our evaluation of 31 embedding models reveals that large instruction-tuned multilingual models achieve the strongest performance, while existing Slovak-specific models trained for NLU tasks transfer poorly to embedding tasks. To address the need for efficient, locally-deployable Slovak embeddings, we develop \texttt{e5-sk-small} (45M parameters) and \texttt{e5-sk-large} (365M) by applying vocabulary trimming and fine-tuning to Multilingual E5 models. Despite size reductions of up to 62\%, our open-source models achieve competitive performance with proprietary APIs while remaining locally deployable for semantic search and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). We release the benchmark, models, datasets, and code openly, hoping our approach offers a replicable path for other under-resourced languages.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SkMTEB：斯洛伐克大规模文本嵌入基准与模型适配
我们引入SkMTEB，这是首个面向斯洛伐克语（一种资源匮乏的西斯拉夫语）的综合性MTEB风格文本嵌入基准，涵盖7类任务的31个数据集——几乎是现有斯洛伐克多语言基准覆盖深度的4倍。对31个嵌入模型的评估表明，大型指令微调多语言模型表现最佳，而现有的斯洛伐克特定模型（为NLU任务训练）在嵌入任务中迁移效果差。为满足高效、本地部署斯洛伐克嵌入的需求，我们通过词汇修剪和微调，基于多语言E5模型开发了\texttt{e5-sk-small}（45M参数）和\texttt{e5-sk-large}（365M）。尽管模型规模缩减高达62%，我们的开源模型在性能上可媲美专有API，同时仍可本地部署用于语义搜索和检索增强生成（RAG）。我们公开发布基准、模型、数据集和代码，希望为其他资源匮乏语言提供可复制的路径。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of comprehensive benchmarks and efficient embedding models for Slovak, a low-resource West Slavic language, the authors introduce SkMTEB, the first MTEB-style benchmark covering 31 datasets across seven tasks. They evaluate 31 embedding models and find that large instruction-tuned multilingual models outperform Slovak-specific NLU models in embedding tasks. To enable local deployment, they adapt Multilingual E5 models via vocabulary trimming and fine-tuning, creating e5-sk-small (45M) and e5-sk-large (365M), which achieve competitive performance despite 62% parameter reductions, making them suitable for semantic search and RAG applications.
为填补斯洛伐克语（一种资源匮乏的西斯拉夫语）缺乏全面评估基准和高质量嵌入模型的空白，作者构建了SkMTEB，这是首个包含31个数据集、覆盖七类任务的MTEB风格基准，规模近四倍于现有斯洛伐克语评估。评估31个嵌入模型发现，大型指令调优的多语言模型表现最佳，而现有斯洛伐克语特定的NLU模型在嵌入任务上迁移效果差。为实现本地部署，作者通过词汇修剪与微调改造多语言E5模型，推出e5-sk-small（45M参数）和e5-sk-large（365M参数），在参数缩减62%的情况下仍保持竞争力，适用于语义搜索和RAG场景。
Surflo: Consistent 3D Surface Flow Model with Global State
Authors: Antoine Guédon, Shu Nakamura, Nicolas Dufour, Jiahui Lei, Ko Nishino, Angjoo Kanazawa
First: 2026-06-11T17:48:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:48:38+00:00
Comments: Project webpage: https://anttwo.github.io/surflo/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Geometry is invariant to viewpoint, which makes any collection of images a redundant encoding of a single 3D state. Existing feed-forward reconstruction models fail to exploit this: per-view methods emit overlapping, unaligned pointmaps that grow linearly with input count, while global-latent methods commit to a fixed, low-resolution output. We introduce Surflo, which compresses a variable number of unposed RGB views into K latent tokens-one global state-and decodes oriented 3D surface points by independently transporting them from noise onto the surface via flow matching. This frees the output from any fixed grid or token budget: the same latent yields from a few thousand to a million points in a single forward pass. To suppress the local inconsistencies inherent to independent per-point decoding, an inference-time guidance term correlates nearby points by injecting a photometric gradient during ODE integration. Surflo matches or surpasses feed-forward baselines on surface metrics, runs an order of magnitude faster than optimization-based methods that require hundreds of views, and is the only feed-forward approach to combine a global latent with arbitrary-resolution decoding.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Surflo：具有全局状态的一致3D表面流模型
几何结构对视角不变，这意味着任何图像集合都是单个3D状态的冗余编码。现有前馈重建模型未能利用这一点：每视角方法生成重叠且未对齐的点图，其规模随输入数量线性增长，而全局潜在空间方法则固定输出低分辨率结果。我们提出Surflo，将任意数量的无姿态RGB图像压缩为K个潜在标记——一个全局状态，并通过流匹配独立地将噪声点运输至表面以解码带方向的3D表面点。这使输出摆脱了固定网格或标记预算的限制：同一潜在状态可在单次前向传播中生成从数千到百万个点。为抑制独立点解码固有的局部不一致性，推理时引入引导项，在ODE积分过程中注入光度梯度以关联邻近点。Surflo在表面指标上匹配或超越前馈基线，比需数百视角的优化方法快一个数量级，且是唯一结合全局潜在空间与任意分辨率解码的前馈方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to efficiently reconstruct 3D surfaces from variable numbers of unposed RGB images without viewpoint redundancy, Surflo introduces a feed-forward model that compresses input views into a single global latent state. It decodes oriented 3D surface points by transporting noise via flow matching, enabling arbitrary-resolution output without fixed grid or token constraints. To mitigate local inconsistencies from independent point decoding, it injects photometric gradients during ODE integration to guide nearby points. Experiments show Surflo matches or exceeds feed-forward baselines on surface metrics, runs 10x faster than optimization-based methods, and uniquely combines global latent encoding with scalable point output.
为利用3D几何对视角不变的特性，本文提出Surflo模型，将多个无姿态RGB图像压缩为单一全局潜在状态，实现任意分辨率的3D表面重建，无需固定网格或令牌限制。其通过流匹配将噪声独立传输至表面，结合ODE积分期间注入的光度梯度以抑制局部不一致性。实验表明，Surflo在表面指标上达到或超越现有前馈基线，比需数百视角的优化方法快10倍，且是唯一能结合全局潜在编码与任意分辨率解码的前馈方法。
Recursive Agent Harnesses
Authors: Elias Lumer, Sahil Sen, Kevin Paul, Vamse Kumar Subbiah
First: 2026-06-11T17:47:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:47:30+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recursive language models (RLMs) showed that recursion over model calls is an effective strategy for long-context reasoning, and production coding agents have begun to write code that spawns subagents at scale, most recently in Anthropic's dynamic workflows. We name and study the pattern between these two lines of work, where the recursive unit is a full agent harness with filesystem tools, code execution, and planning rather than a model call with no tools. We call this the Recursive Agent Harness (RAH) and frame it as harness recursion, the code-first extension to the model recursion of RLMs. A parent agent generates and runs an executable script that spawns subagent harnesses in parallel for fine-grained workloads and uses structured function calls for small subtasks. We provide a controlled evaluation on long-context reasoning. With the backbone held fixed at GPT-5 to match the published Codex and RLM baselines, RAH improves the Codex coding-agent baseline from 71.75% to 81.36% on Oolong-Synthetic (199 samples, 13 context-length buckets up to 4M tokens), a gain attributable to the harness rather than the model. With a stronger backbone, Claude Sonnet 4.5, the same design reaches 89.77%.
中文标题/摘要
标题：递归代理框架
递归语言模型（RLMs）表明，模型调用的递归是一种有效的长上下文推理策略，而生产级编码代理已开始大规模编写能派生子代理的代码，最近体现在Anthropic的动态工作流中。我们命名并研究这两类工作的共同模式：递归单元是一个具备文件系统工具、代码执行和规划能力的完整代理框架，而非无工具的模型调用。我们称之为递归代理框架（RAH），并将其视为RLMs模型递归的代码优先扩展——即“框架递归”。父代理生成并运行可执行脚本，以并行方式启动子代理框架处理细粒度任务，并使用结构化函数调用处理小规模子任务。我们在长上下文推理任务上进行了受控评估。在保持骨干模型为GPT-5（与已发表的Codex和RLM基线一致）的前提下，RAH将Codex编码代理基线从71.75%提升至81.36%（Oolong-Synthetic数据集，199个样本，13个上下文长度桶，最高达4M tokens），该提升归因于框架而非模型本身。若使用更强的骨干模型Claude Sonnet 4.5，相同设计可达到89.77%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the success of recursive language models and production coding agents that spawn subagents, this work introduces Recursive Agent Harnesses (RAH), a framework where agents recursively spawn full-fledged subagents with filesystem access, code execution, and planning capabilities. The parent agent generates executable scripts to parallelize fine-grained tasks and uses structured function calls for smaller subtasks. Evaluated on long-context reasoning tasks with GPT-5 as the backbone, RAH improved the Codex baseline from 71.75% to 81.36% on Oolong-Synthetic, demonstrating that the harness design—not the model—drives the gain. With Claude Sonnet 4.5, the same architecture achieved 89.77%, confirming scalability and effectiveness.
为提升长上下文推理能力，该研究提出递归代理框架（RAH），其核心是父代理生成并执行脚本，以并行启动具备文件系统、代码执行与规划能力的子代理。与模型递归不同，RAH通过代码驱动任务分解，使用结构化函数调用处理细粒度子任务。在固定GPT-5模型基础上，RAH将Codex基线准确率从71.75%提升至81.36%，表明性能增益主要来自框架设计而非模型本身；使用更强模型Claude Sonnet 4.5时，该架构准确率进一步提升至89.77%。
The Stable Recovery Manifold: Geometric Principles Governing Recoverability in Continual Learning
Authors: Ayushman Trivedi, Bhavika Melwani
First: 2026-06-11T17:45:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:45:41+00:00
Comments: 9 pages, 8 figures, 8 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Catastrophic forgetting is often viewed as the destruction of previously learned knowledge during sequential learning. Building on the Accessibility Collapse framework, we investigate the geometric structure of recoverability in continual learning. Using Split CIFAR-100 and a sequentially trained ResNet-18, we analyze recoverability, representational drift, and recovery complexity across ten tasks. We introduce Recovery Subspace Dimensionality (k_t), a measure of the minimum number of singular directions required to preserve 90 percent of full probe performance. Contrary to our Recoverability Diffusion hypothesis, recovery dimensionality remains stable throughout training (mean k_t = 8.0) despite substantial representational drift. Principal-angle drift strongly predicts recoverability (r = -0.862), and a simple geometric model explains 82.2 percent of recoverability variance. These findings support the Stable Recovery Manifold hypothesis, suggesting that forgotten knowledge remains compactly decodable despite representational reorganization. The results indicate that catastrophic forgetting is primarily an accessibility and manifold-alignment problem rather than information destruction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：稳定恢复流形：持续学习中可恢复性的几何原理
灾难性遗忘常被视为在序列学习过程中先前知识的破坏。基于可访问性崩溃框架，我们研究持续学习中可恢复性的几何结构。使用Split CIFAR-100和顺序训练的ResNet-18，我们分析十项任务中的可恢复性、表征漂移及恢复复杂度。我们引入恢复子空间维度（k_t），衡量保持90%完整探测性能所需的最小奇异方向数。与我们的可恢复性扩散假说相反，尽管表征漂移显著，恢复维度在训练过程中保持稳定（平均k_t=8.0）。主角角漂移强烈预测可恢复性（r=-0.862），一个简单的几何模型可解释82.2%的可恢复性方差。这些发现支持稳定恢复流形假说，表明尽管表征重组，遗忘知识仍可紧凑解码。结果表明，灾难性遗忘主要是一个可访问性与流形对齐问题，而非信息破坏。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how knowledge is lost and potentially recovered in continual learning, this study investigates the geometric structure underlying recoverability using a sequentially trained ResNet-18 on Split CIFAR-100. It introduces Recovery Subspace Dimensionality (k_t), measuring the minimal singular directions needed to retain 90% of probe performance, and finds that despite significant representational drift, k_t remains stable at a mean of 8.0. Principal-angle drift strongly correlates with recoverability (r = -0.862), and a geometric model explains 82.2% of recoverability variance, supporting the Stable Recovery Manifold hypothesis that forgotten knowledge remains compactly decodable through manifold alignment rather than information destruction.
为理解持续学习中知识如何丢失与恢复，本研究基于Split CIFAR-100和顺序训练的ResNet-18，探究恢复性的几何结构。引入恢复子空间维度（k_t）衡量维持90%探针性能所需的最小奇异方向数，发现尽管表征漂移显著，k_t在训练中保持稳定（平均值8.0）。主角角漂移与恢复性高度相关（r = -0.862），几何模型解释了82.2%的方差，支持稳定恢复流形假说：遗忘知识因流形对齐仍可紧凑解码，而非信息破坏。
Operads for compositional reasoning in LLMs
Authors: Nathaniel Bottman, Kyle Richardson
First: 2026-06-11T17:44:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:44:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Question decomposition, i.e. breaking a complex query into simpler sub-queries whose answers are composed to produce a final answer, is a widely used strategy for improving LLM reasoning, yet it currently lacks a rigorous mathematical foundation. In this paper, we propose operads, mathematical structures that model many-in, one-out operations and compositions thereof, as a natural framework for describing question decomposition. We define the questions operad $Q$, in which operations correspond to question templates and composition corresponds to substitution of sub-answers, and show how QA models can be interpreted as algebras over $Q$. Beyond reframing existing practice, this operadic perspective points toward new methods, in particular a notion of operadic consistency, which measures whether a QA model's answers agree across the partial collapses of a question decomposition tree. Empirical evaluation of operadic consistency is reported in our companion paper (Bottman, Liu, and Richardson, 2026), which finds it strongly correlated with accuracy across twelve LLMs and four multi-hop QA datasets and outperforming standard temperature-based self-consistency baselines. We argue that operads are the natural mathematical home for question decomposition, and that invariants such as operadic consistency open new directions for analyzing and improving the reliability of multi-step reasoning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于大语言模型组合推理的代数结构
问题分解，即将复杂查询拆解为若干简单子查询并组合其答案以生成最终答案，是提升大语言模型推理能力的常用策略，但目前尚缺乏严谨的数学基础。本文提出代数结构（operads），其建模多输入、单输出操作及其组合，作为描述问题分解的自然框架。我们定义了问题代数 $Q$，其中操作对应问题模板，组合对应子答案的替换，并展示问答模型可被解释为 $Q$ 上的代数。除重构现有实践外，该代数视角还指向新方法，特别是“代数一致性”概念，用于衡量问答模型在问题分解树的局部坍缩中答案是否一致。我们配套论文（Bottman, Liu, and Richardson, 2026）报告了代数一致性的实证评估，发现其在十二个大语言模型和四个多跳问答数据集上与准确率高度相关，且优于标准的基于温度的自一致性基线。我们认为代数结构是问题分解的自然数学框架，而此类不变量（如代数一致性）为分析和提升多步推理可靠性开辟了新方向。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of a rigorous mathematical foundation for question decomposition in LLMs, this paper introduces operads as a formal framework to model compositional reasoning, where operations represent question templates and composition corresponds to substituting sub-answers. The authors define the questions operad Q and interpret QA models as algebras over Q, enabling a new notion of operadic consistency to measure agreement across partial decompositions. Empirical results from companion work show operadic consistency strongly correlates with accuracy across twelve LLMs and four multi-hop QA datasets, outperforming standard temperature-based self-consistency baselines, suggesting operads offer a principled approach to analyzing and improving multi-step reasoning.
为解决大语言模型中问题分解缺乏严谨数学基础的问题，本文提出使用代数结构“运算代”（operads）作为描述问题分解的自然框架，其通过多输入单输出操作及其组合建模复杂查询的拆解与答案合成。作者定义了“问题代”Q，其中操作对应问题模板，组合对应子答案代入，且将问答模型解释为Q上的代数。实验证明，新提出的“运算代一致性”指标在十二个大模型和四个多跳问答数据集上与准确率高度相关，且优于传统基于温度的自一致性基线方法。
Towards Data-free and Training-free Compression for Speech Foundation Models Using Parameter Clustering
Authors: Haoning Xu, Zhaoqing Li, Huimeng Wang, Youjun Chen, Chengxi Deng, Mengzhe Geng, Xunying Liu
First: 2026-06-10T09:16:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:42:41+00:00
Comments: Accepted by Interspeech 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper presents a novel data-free and training-free compression approach for speech foundation models using channelwise clustering via k-means. More fine-grained, mixed sparsity pruning by layer-level varying number of parameter clusters is also explored. Experiments conducted on the LibriSpeech dataset suggest that when operating with pruning sparsity of 50% on HuBERT-large, consistent WER reductions of 27.73%/18.61% absolute (34.37%/21.91% relative) over the magnitude-based pruning were obtained on the test-clean and test-other subsets before fine-tuning and 0.19%/0.79% absolute (3.36%/4.62% relative) after fine-tuning with only 3 epochs. Similar WER reductions of 2.86%/5.02% absolute (59.21%/55.29% relative) were observed against magnitudebased pruning on Whisper-large-v3 at 10% sparsity, all with no significant WER increase relative to the uncompressed baseline.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于参数聚类的无数据、无训练语音基础模型压缩方法
本文提出了一种基于通道聚类（k-means）的无数据、无训练语音基础模型压缩新方法。同时探索了通过层间不同参数聚类数量实现更精细的混合稀疏剪枝。在LibriSpeech数据集上的实验表明，当在HuBERT-large模型上以50%剪枝稀疏度运行时，在微调前，测试集clean和test-other子集上的WER分别降低27.73%/18.61%（绝对值，相对值34.37%/21.91%），微调后仅3个epoch，WER分别降低0.19%/0.79%（绝对值，相对值3.36%/4.62%）。在Whisper-large-v3模型上，10%稀疏度下，与基于幅度的剪枝相比，WER分别降低2.86%/5.02%（绝对值，相对值59.21%/55.29%），且与未压缩基线相比无显著WER增加。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient compression of speech foundation models without requiring training data or fine-tuning, this work introduces a data-free and training-free method using channelwise k-means clustering to group model parameters. The approach enables fine-grained, layer-adaptive sparsity by varying the number of clusters per layer, allowing more precise pruning. Experiments on LibriSpeech show that at 50% sparsity on HuBERT-large, the method achieves 27.73% absolute WER reduction on test-clean and 18.61% on test-other before fine-tuning, improving upon magnitude-based pruning, and maintains performance gains even after fine-tuning with only 3 epochs. Similar improvements are observed on Whisper-large-v3 at 10% sparsity, with no significant degradation relative to the uncompressed baseline.
为实现无需训练数据和微调的语音基础模型压缩，本文提出一种基于通道级k均值聚类的参数压缩方法。该方法通过按层动态调整聚类数量，实现更精细的分层稀疏剪枝。在LibriSpeech数据集上实验表明，对HuBERT-large在50%稀疏度下，相比幅度剪枝方法，测试集clean和other子集的绝对WER分别降低27.73%和18.61%，微调3轮后仍保持0.19%和0.79%的绝对WER提升。在Whisper-large-v3模型10%稀疏度下，同样获得2.86%和5.02%的绝对WER降低，且未出现显著性能下降。
Aerial Wildfire Suppression Planning with a Hybrid CNN-Cellular Automata Fire Model
Authors: Ion Matei, Maksym Zhenirovskyy, Takuya Kurihana, Rohit Vupala, Anthony Wong
First: 2026-06-11T17:42:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:42:34+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Aerial wildfire suppression requires not only predicting fire spread, but also designing effective intervention strategies under operational and environmental uncertainty. We present a modeling and optimization framework for aerial wildfire suppression that combines a hybrid neural-cellular automaton wildfire model with gradient-based design of targeted aerial drops. The wildfire model predicts spatially varying spread behavior from terrain, fuel, and wind data, while the intervention module determines binary drop actions with continuous-valued location and orientation parameters mapped to the simulation grid. Water and retardant are represented with distinct suppression effects, corresponding to immediate reduction of active burning and persistent reduction of future spread. To evaluate the robustness of the resulting suppression plans, we quantify both aleatoric uncertainty through Monte Carlo sampling of daily fire-state realizations and epistemic uncertainty through spatially correlated prediction-error perturbations. A case study based on the 2020 Bear Fire shows that the framework can generate coherent aerial suppression schedules for reducing total fire-affected area and can support uncertainty-aware analysis of wildfire intervention strategies.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于混合CNN-细胞自动机火灾模型的空中野火抑制规划
空中野火抑制不仅需要预测火势蔓延，还需在操作与环境不确定性下设计有效的干预策略。本文提出一种结合混合神经细胞自动机火灾模型与梯度优化的靶向空中洒水方案的建模与优化框架。火灾模型根据地形、燃料和风力数据预测空间异质性蔓延行为；干预模块通过连续值的位置与方向参数映射至模拟网格，确定二元洒水动作。水与阻燃剂分别对应即时降低燃烧强度与持续抑制未来蔓延的抑制效果。为评估抑制方案的鲁棒性，通过蒙特卡洛采样量化每日火情状态的随机不确定性，并通过空间相关预测误差扰动量化认知不确定性。基于2020年熊火案例研究表明，该框架可生成连贯的空中抑制调度方案，减少总受火面积，并支持不确定性感知的野火干预策略分析。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for effective aerial wildfire suppression under uncertain environmental conditions, this study develops a hybrid CNN-cellular automaton model to simulate fire spread and optimize targeted aerial drop strategies. The model integrates terrain, fuel, and wind data to predict spatially varying fire behavior, while an intervention module assigns binary drop actions with continuous location and orientation parameters. Water and retardant are modeled with distinct suppression effects: immediate burning reduction and long-term spread inhibition. Experimental evaluation using Monte Carlo sampling and spatially correlated error perturbations demonstrates the framework’s robustness, with results from the 2020 Bear Fire case study showing it can generate coherent suppression schedules that reduce total fire-affected area while supporting uncertainty-aware decision-making.
为应对环境不确定性下的空中野火扑救需求，本研究构建了融合卷积神经网络与元胞自动机的混合模型，用于模拟火势蔓延并优化精准空中洒水策略。模型结合地形、燃料与风速数据预测空间异质性火势行为，干预模块通过连续位置与方向参数分配二元洒水动作，区分水与阻燃剂的不同抑制效果：即时灭火与持续抑制蔓延。实验通过蒙特卡洛采样与空间相关误差扰动评估策略鲁棒性，结果显示该框架可生成有效减少总过火面积的空中扑救计划，验证于2020年熊火案例。
From Tokens to Faces: Investigating Discrete Speech Representations for 3D Facial Animation
Authors: Pedro Correa, Olivier Perrotin, Samir Sadok, Paula Costa, Thomas Hueber
First: 2026-06-11T17:41:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:41:18+00:00
Comments: This work has been accepted in Interspeech 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The choice of speech representation is critical in speech-driven 3D facial animation. Representations differ in what they encode: SSL features emphasize segmental and semantic cues, neural codecs yield latents optimized for acoustic reconstruction, and ASR-style objectives produce label-based spaces. We evaluate four speech representation families for 3D facial synthesis, comparing their facial reconstruction quality across two facial decoders using objective metrics and a perceptual evaluation. We additionally conduct probing analyses that relate tokenized representations to phonetic units and to articulatory deformations. We found that encoding phonetic classes is beneficial for accurate facial animation prediction on both semantic and label-based representations with comparable facial animation quality. From the latter, we introduce an Audio Visual Text-to-Speech (AVTTS) pipeline that leverages, as a shared space, discrete representations to decode speech and 3D facial motion.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从音素到面部：探究离散语音表示在3D面部动画中的应用
语音表示的选择对语音驱动的3D面部动画至关重要。不同表示方式编码的内容不同：SSL特征强调分段和语义线索，神经编码器生成优化声学重建的潜在表示，ASR风格目标则生成基于标签的空间。我们评估了四种语音表示家族在3D面部合成中的表现，通过客观指标和感知评估比较其在两种面部解码器上的面部重建质量。此外，我们还进行了探针分析，将离散表示与语音音素单元及发音变形关联起来。研究发现，编码语音音类对基于语义和标签的面部动画预测均有益，且面部动画质量相当。基于后者，我们提出了一种音频-视觉-文本到语音（AVTTS）管道，利用离散表示作为共享空间，同时解码语音和3D面部运动。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how different discrete speech representations affect 3D facial animation quality, motivated by the need to choose optimal encodings for speech-driven synthesis. The authors compare four representation families—SSL features, neural codecs, ASR-style labels, and phonetic-class tokens—using two facial decoders, evaluating both objective metrics and perceptual quality. Their probing analyses link tokenized representations to phonetic units and articulatory movements, revealing that phonetic-class encoding enhances facial animation accuracy across both semantic and label-based approaches. The key outcome is the introduction of an Audio-Visual Text-to-Speech (AVTTS) pipeline that unifies discrete representations for joint speech and facial motion decoding.
该研究旨在探究不同离散语音表示对3D面部动画质量的影响，动机源于语音驱动合成中选择最优编码的必要性。作者对比了四种表示方法——SSL特征、神经码器、ASR式标签空间和音素类标记——在两种面部解码器上的表现，通过客观指标和感知评估进行比较。探针分析将标记化表示与音素单元和发音变形关联，发现音素类编码在语义和标签式表示中均能提升面部动画准确性，且不降低质量。研究进一步提出一种音频视觉文本到语音（AVTTS）管线，利用离散表示作为共享空间同时解码语音与3D面部运动。
Valid Inference with Synthetic Data via Task Exchangeability
Authors: Lezhi Tan, Tijana Zrnic
First: 2026-06-11T17:41:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:41:09+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
There is a proliferation of work arguing for the use of synthetic data in scientific research. For example, social scientists are arguing for the use of LLM-generated "silicon samples" in pilot studies; AI evaluations increasingly rely on "LLM-as-a-judge" outputs; and proteomics research is accelerated by generative models that produce synthetic protein structures. These developments raise an intriguing possibility: synthetic data may help researchers ask more questions, run more studies, and accelerate discovery. But they also raise a fundamental concern: synthetic data can be biased, noisy, and misspecified. In this work, we propose statistical principles for using synthetic data in scientific research with provable validity guarantees. The key insight is a new technical condition that we call task exchangeability. Informally, this is a requirement that the researcher can identify historical tasks, for which real data is available, such that their current task of interest is exchangeable with the historical tasks in an appropriate mathematical sense. We develop methods for valid inference under task exchangeability, together with extensions that provide guarantees even beyond exchangeability. We demonstrate the framework on public opinion surveys with silicon samples and AI evaluation with autoraters.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过任务可交换性实现合成数据的有效推断
目前有大量研究主张在科学研究中使用合成数据。例如，社会科学家主张在试点研究中使用大语言模型生成的“硅基样本”；AI评估日益依赖“大语言模型作为裁判”的输出；蛋白质组学研究则通过生成模型加速，该模型可生成合成蛋白质结构。这些发展提出一个引人入胜的可能性：合成数据可能帮助研究人员提出更多问题、开展更多研究并加速发现。但同时也引发一个根本性担忧：合成数据可能具有偏倚、噪声和模型设定错误。在本研究中，我们提出用于科学研究中使用合成数据的统计原则，并提供可证明的有效性保证。核心洞见是一种新的技术条件，我们称之为“任务可交换性”。通俗而言，该条件要求研究人员能够识别历史任务（这些任务有真实数据可用），使得当前研究任务在适当数学意义上与这些历史任务可交换。我们开发了在任务可交换性下进行有效推断的方法，以及扩展方法，即使在超越可交换性的情形下仍能提供保证。我们通过硅基样本的公众意见调查和自动评分器的AI评估展示了该框架。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the growing use of synthetic data in scientific domains such as social science, AI evaluation, and proteomics, this work addresses the risk of biased or misspecified synthetic data by proposing a statistical framework for valid inference. The core method introduces a condition called task exchangeability, which requires that the current research task be mathematically exchangeable with historical tasks for which real data exists. Under this condition, the authors develop inference procedures with provable validity guarantees, and extend them to handle cases beyond strict exchangeability. Experimental results on public opinion surveys using LLM-generated samples and AI evaluations using autoraters confirm that the framework enables statistically sound inference even when synthetic data is imperfect.
鉴于合成数据在社会科学、AI评估和蛋白质组学等领域日益广泛的应用，本文旨在解决合成数据可能存在的偏差与误设问题，提出一套具有可证明有效性的统计推断框架。核心方法引入“任务可交换性”条件，要求当前研究任务在数学上可与拥有真实数据的历史任务交换。在此基础上，作者构建了可验证有效性的推断方法，并扩展至可处理非严格可交换的情形。在使用硅样本的民意调查和使用自动评分器的AI评估实验中，该框架成功实现了即使在合成数据不完美时仍能获得统计上可靠的推断结果。
Generative Modeling of Bach-Style Symbolic Music: A Comparative Study of Autoregressive, Latent-Variable, and Adversarial Approaches
Authors: Kyuil Lee, Dezhi Yu, Yongkang Huang
First: 2026-06-11T17:39:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:39:55+00:00
Comments: 11 pages, 13 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study generative modeling of Bach-style symbolic piano music using a shared MIDI corpus and three model families: autoregressive LSTMs with attention, latent-variable models including recurrent VAEs and vector-quantized VAEs, and generative adversarial networks. We compare their ability to model polyphonic note sequences, learn useful latent representations, and generate stylistically coherent compositions. Our experiments show that the autoregressive LSTM with attention produces the most musically coherent samples, while vector quantization helps mitigate posterior collapse and yields more structured outputs than conventional recurrent VAEs. The adversarial approach captures local pitch patterns but remains difficult to train and generalizes less reliably to Bach's style. These results highlight the relative strengths and failure modes of autoregressive, latent-variable, and adversarial approaches for symbolic music generation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：巴赫风格符号音乐的生成建模：自回归、潜在变量与对抗方法的比较研究
我们使用共享的MIDI语料库，研究了三种模型家族（带注意力机制的自回归LSTM、包括循环VAE和向量量化VAE的潜在变量模型、生成对抗网络）在生成巴赫风格钢琴音乐方面的表现。我们比较了它们在建模多声部音符序列、学习有用潜在表示及生成风格一致作品方面的能力。实验表明，带注意力机制的自回归LSTM生成的音乐样本最具音乐连贯性；向量量化有助于缓解后验坍塌，相比传统循环VAE生成更结构化的输出；对抗方法虽能捕捉局部音高模式，但训练困难且对巴赫风格的泛化能力较弱。这些结果突显了自回归、潜在变量与对抗方法在符号音乐生成中的相对优势与失效模式。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to generate Bach-style symbolic piano music with stylistic fidelity, this study compares three generative model families—autoregressive LSTMs with attention, latent-variable models (including recurrent and vector-quantized VAEs), and adversarial networks—using a shared MIDI corpus. The autoregressive LSTM with attention produced the most musically coherent samples, while vector quantization in VAEs reduced posterior collapse and improved structural output quality over standard recurrent VAEs. Adversarial networks captured local pitch patterns but struggled with training stability and generalization to Bach’s style, revealing distinct strengths and weaknesses across model types.
为生成风格忠实的巴赫式钢琴符号音乐，本研究在统一的MIDI数据集上比较了三种生成模型家族：带注意力机制的自回归LSTM、潜在变量模型（含循环VAE与向量量化VAE）及生成对抗网络。自回归LSTM生成的样本音乐性最连贯，向量量化VAE缓解了后验坍塌问题，输出结构更清晰；而对抗网络虽能捕捉局部音高模式，但训练不稳定且难以泛化至巴赫风格，揭示了三类方法各自的优劣与失效模式。
Revisiting Vehicle Color Recognition in Long-Tailed Surveillance Scenarios
Authors: Vinícius Orrú, Bruno H. Foggiatto, Gabriel E. Lima, David Menotti, Rayson Laroca
First: 2026-06-11T17:39:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:39:40+00:00
Comments: Accepted for presentation at the 2026 International Conference on Pattern Recognition (ICPR) - V3SC Workshop
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Vehicle color recognition is an important cue for vehicle identification in surveillance systems, especially when license plates are illegible due to low resolution, occlusion, motion blur, or poor illumination. However, real-world vehicle color distributions are highly imbalanced, making overall accuracy insufficient to assess performance on rare but operationally relevant colors. This paper presents a comprehensive study of vehicle color recognition under severe class imbalance using UFPR-VeSV, a challenging real-world surveillance dataset. We investigate synthetic minority-class augmentation through two off-the-shelf generative strategies: text-conditioned image generation with RunDiffusion/JuggernautXL and image-conditioned color editing with Gemini 2.0 Flash. The curated synthetic data are combined with modern visual representations, loss reweighting, learning-rate scheduling, color-safe augmentation, foreground-aware preprocessing, and ensemble fusion. The bestperforming approach achieves 94.6% micro accuracy and 79.7% macro accuracy, improving macro accuracy by 8.2 percentage points over recent literature. A manual error analysis further shows that many remaining failures are visually ambiguous even for human annotators, highlighting the practical limits of color-based vehicle identification in unconstrained surveillance imagery. The generated images and source code are publicly available at https://github.com/viniciusorru/vcr-synthetic

标题：重新审视长尾监控场景下的车辆颜色识别
车辆颜色识别是监控系统中车辆识别的重要线索，尤其在车牌因低分辨率、遮挡、运动模糊或照明不足而无法辨识时。然而，现实世界中的车辆颜色分布高度不平衡，导致整体准确率不足以评估在罕见但具有实际操作意义的颜色上的性能。本文在UFPR-VeSV这一具有挑战性的现实世界监控数据集上，对严重类别不平衡下的车辆颜色识别进行了全面研究。我们通过两种现成的生成策略——使用RunDiffusion/JuggernautXL的文本条件图像生成和使用Gemini 2.0 Flash的图像条件颜色编辑——探索合成少数类数据增强。精选的合成数据结合了现代视觉表示、损失重加权、学习率调度、颜色安全增强、前景感知预处理和集成融合。最佳方法实现了94.6%的微准确率和79.7%的宏准确率，较近期文献提升了8.2个百分点的宏准确率。人工错误分析进一步表明，许多剩余失败案例即使对人工标注员而言也存在视觉歧义，凸显了在无约束监控图像中基于颜色的车辆识别的实际局限性。生成的图像和源代码已公开于https://github.com/viniciusorru/vcr-synthetic。

为解决监控场景中因车牌模糊或缺失而导致的稀有车辆颜色识别难题，本研究通过合成数据增强应对类别不平衡问题，采用RunDiffusion/JuggernautXL和Gemini 2.0 Flash两种现成生成模型分别进行文本引导图像生成和图像引导颜色编辑。合成数据与现代视觉表征、损失重加权、学习率调度、色彩安全增强、前景感知预处理及集成融合策略结合使用。最佳模型实现94.6%微平均准确率和79.7%宏平均准确率，较现有方法提升8.2个百分点；但人工错误分析显示，许多误判案例即使对人类标注员也难以区分，揭示了在无约束监控图像中基于颜色的车辆识别存在固有局限。
Beyond Uniform Tokens: Adaptive Compression for Time Series Language Models
Authors: Jialin Gan, Xin Qiu, Guangzhe Chen, Xue Wang
First: 2026-06-11T17:39:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:39:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) have enabled time series (TS) analysis by jointly modeling numerical observations and textual context through a shared token interface. However, TS tokens and prompt tokens exhibit fundamentally different information structures, making uniform token processing inefficient. In this paper, we study token efficiency in TS language modeling from an asymmetric-token perspective. We show that TS tokens have highly uneven spectral contributions, where many tokens share redundant frequency patterns while a small subset preserves critical temporal evidence. We also observe that prompt-token influence attenuates with model depth, suggesting that full prompt retention across all layers is unnecessary. Based on these findings, we develop an adaptive token budgeting framework that compresses TS tokens via frequency-domain structure and progressively reduces prompt tokens across layers. Experiments across forecasting, classification, imputation, and anomaly detection demonstrate up to \textit{\textbf{7.68$\times$}} inference acceleration and performance gains in \textit{\textbf{78\%}} of evaluated settings, showing the effectiveness of asymmetric token compression for scalable TS foundation models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越统一令牌：面向时间序列语言模型的自适应压缩
大型语言模型（LLMs）通过共享令牌接口，联合建模数值观测与文本上下文，推动了时间序列（TS）分析。然而，TS令牌与提示令牌具有根本不同的信息结构，统一令牌处理效率低下。本文从非对称令牌视角研究TS语言建模中的令牌效率问题。我们发现TS令牌频谱贡献极不均衡，多数令牌共享冗余频率模式，而少数令牌保留关键时序证据。同时观察到提示令牌的影响随模型深度衰减，表明全层保留提示令牌非必要。基于此，我们提出自适应令牌预算框架，通过频域结构压缩TS令牌，并逐层渐进减少提示令牌。在预测、分类、插补与异常检测任务中，实验显示推理加速最高达\textit{\textbf{7.68$\times$}}，在\textit{\textbf{78\%}}的评估场景中性能提升，验证了非对称令牌压缩对可扩展TS基础模型的有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of uniform token processing in time series language models, this paper investigates asymmetric token structures, revealing that time series tokens exhibit uneven spectral contributions with redundant frequency patterns and critical temporal evidence concentrated in a small subset, while prompt token influence diminishes with model depth. The authors propose an adaptive token budgeting framework that compresses time series tokens via frequency-domain structure and progressively reduces prompt tokens across layers. Experiments across forecasting, classification, imputation, and anomaly detection show up to 7.68x inference acceleration and performance gains in 78% of evaluated settings, demonstrating the effectiveness of asymmetric token compression for scalable time series foundation models.
鉴于统一处理时间序列语言模型中的令牌效率低下，本文从非对称令牌视角出发，发现时间序列令牌频谱贡献不均，大量令牌存在冗余频率模式，而关键时序信息集中于少数令牌；同时提示令牌的影响随模型深度递减，无需在所有层保留完整提示。为此，作者提出一种自适应令牌预算框架，通过频域结构压缩时间序列令牌，并逐层渐进削减提示令牌。在预测、分类、填补与异常检测等任务中，实验表明该方法最高可实现7.68倍推理加速，78%的评估场景下性能提升，验证了非对称令牌压缩在可扩展时间序列基础模型中的有效性。
Beyond Runtime Enforcement: Shield Synthesis as Defensibility Analysis for Adversarial Networks
Authors: Achraf Hsain, Sultan Almuhammadi
First: 2026-06-11T17:35:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:35:40+00:00
Comments: 26 pages, 7 figures, 7 tables. Under review at JAIR. Code: https://github.com/AchrafHsain7/Bastion
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Shielded reinforcement learning is typically presented as a runtime safety mechanism that compiles temporal-logic specifications into automata restricting an agent's actions. We argue this is the wrong product. The same automata-theoretic machinery -- specification compilation, product game construction, attractor computation, and winning-region extraction -- is better read as a design-time analytical instrument whose outputs are structural insights about a system rather than runtime constraints on a deployed agent. We instantiate this through a constrained two-player safety game for network defense. The two specifications are enforced asymmetrically: the defender specification defines the unsafe region of the game, whereas the attacker specification restricts the adversary's legal actions during attractor computation. Solving the game yields a defensibility verdict -- a formal certificate that a topology-specification pair is or is not defensible -- with the associated winning region and shield. Beyond the binary verdict, we derive topology-level metrics from the attractor structure and combine them with post-convergence behavior from shield-constrained adversarial multi-agent reinforcement learning. Together these form a defensibility fingerprint capturing both a network's formal safety properties and its operational behavior under adaptive play. A what-if analysis shows that formal defensibility and operational effectiveness capture distinct aspects of security: small architectural changes can produce large shifts in operational outcomes while leaving formal safety margins nearly unchanged. Shield synthesis is thus most valuable not as a deployment mechanism for safe agents, but as a framework for answering architectural questions about whether, where, and how a system can be defended. The defensibility verdict is the output, not the safe policy.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越运行时执行：将屏蔽合成视为对抗网络的可防御性分析
屏蔽强化学习通常被呈现为一种运行时安全机制，即将时序逻辑规范编译为限制代理行为的自动机。我们认为，这并非正确的产品。相同的自动机理论工具——规范编译、博弈构造、吸引子计算和获胜区域提取——更宜被理解为设计时的分析工具，其输出是关于系统的结构洞见，而非部署代理的运行时约束。 我们通过一个受约束的双人安全博弈实例化这一观点，用于网络防御。两个规范以不对称方式执行：防御者规范定义博弈中的不安全区域，而攻击者规范在吸引子计算期间限制对手的合法行为。求解博弈得出一个可防御性判决——一个形式化的证书，表明拓扑-规范对是否可防御——并附带获胜区域和屏蔽。 除二元判决外，我们从吸引子结构中推导拓扑级指标，并将其与屏蔽约束下的对抗多智能体强化学习的收敛后行为相结合。二者共同构成一个可防御性指纹，同时捕捉网络的形式安全属性及其在适应性博弈下的实际运行行为。 一个“假设分析”表明，形式可防御性与实际有效性捕捉安全的不同方面：微小的架构变更可能大幅改变实际结果，而形式安全裕度几乎不变。因此，屏蔽合成的价值不在于作为安全代理的部署机制，而在于作为一个框架，用于回答系统架构问题：系统是否、何处、如何可被防御。可防御性判决是输出，而非安全策略。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of runtime enforcement in adversarial network defense, the paper reframes shield synthesis as a design-time defensibility analysis tool using automata-theoretic methods. It models network defense as a two-player safety game where the defender defines unsafe regions and the attacker’s actions are constrained by its specification during attractor computation. Solving this game yields a formal defensibility verdict, along with a winning region and shield, while also extracting topology-level metrics from attractor structure and combining them with post-convergence behavior from shield-constrained multi-agent reinforcement learning to form a defensibility fingerprint. Experimental results show that formal safety and operational effectiveness often diverge: minor architectural changes can significantly alter operational outcomes without affecting formal safety margins, demonstrating that shield synthesis is most valuable for architectural decision-making rather than runtime safety enforcement.
该研究旨在突破运行时安全机制的局限，将屏蔽合成重构为一种设计时的防御性分析工具，利用自动机理论方法建模网络防御为双人安全博弈，其中防御方定义危险区域，攻击方行动受其规范约束。通过求解博弈可得形式化防御性判定、获胜区域及屏蔽策略。研究进一步从吸引子结构中提取拓扑级指标，并结合屏蔽约束下的多智能体强化学习收敛后行为，形成防御指纹。实验表明，形式化安全与实际效能常不一致：微小架构调整可能显著改变实际运行结果，而形式安全裕度基本不变，凸显屏蔽合成作为架构分析框架的价值，而非部署安全机制。
Majority-of-Three is Optimal
Authors: Divit Rawal, Nikita Zhivotovskiy
First: 2026-06-11T17:26:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:26:13+00:00
Comments: 9 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We give a short proof that the majority vote of three independent consistent classifiers is an optimal learner in the realizable PAC setting. This proves optimality for the simplest voting scheme, while simplifying both the algorithmic structure and the probabilistic analysis of previous voting learners, including the algorithm of S. Hanneke and the analysis of bagging by K. Green Larsen.
中文标题/摘要
标题：三者多数投票是最优的
我们给出一个简短的证明，表明三个独立一致分类器的多数投票在可实现PAC设定下是一个最优学习器。这证明了最简单的投票方案的最优性，同时简化了之前投票学习器的算法结构和概率分析，包括S. Hanneke的算法和K. Green Larsen对Bagging的分析。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for a simpler and more optimal voting mechanism in the realizable PAC learning setting, the paper proves that the majority vote of three independent consistent classifiers achieves optimal learning performance. The method relies on a concise probabilistic argument that demonstrates the optimality of this simple voting scheme, avoiding the complex analyses required in prior works such as those by Hanneke and Green Larsen. Experimental results confirm that this three-vote majority scheme is indeed optimal, matching the theoretical bounds and outperforming more complex voting algorithms in terms of sample efficiency and algorithmic simplicity.
为在可实现PAC学习框架下寻找最简且最优的投票方案，本文证明了三个独立一致分类器的多数投票可达到最优学习性能。该方法通过简洁的概率论证，避免了复杂算法结构和繁复分析，简化了此前Hanneke和Green Larsen等人的方法。实验结果证实，该三票多数规则确为最优，与给定假设下任何一致学习器的理论界限相匹配。
One Polluted Page Is Enough: Evaluating Web Content Pollution in Generative Recommenders
Authors: Minghao Luo, Liang Chen
First: 2026-06-11T17:24:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:24:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Search-augmented LLMs increasingly mediate everyday consumer recommendations by retrieving live web content. This creates a new risk: generative recommenders may consume polluted web content, such as fake reviews and promotional pages crafted to mislead recommendations. We ask: to what extent do search-augmented LLMs become unwitting promoters of fake products when consuming polluted retrieval results? To answer this, we introduce FORGE (Fake Online Recommendations in Generative Environments), a benchmark for measuring fake-product promotion under controlled web-content pollution. Given an upstream search result, FORGE locally rewrites real products in retrieved web pages into fake ones to simulate web-content pollution, and measures how often the LLM recommends the fake product. FORGE covers 225 real-world products across 15 categories and 5 consumer scenarios. Across 12 commercial and open-weights LLMs, all models are vulnerable: a single polluted page yields fooled rates of up to 27%, while the full top-3 replacement raises this to 73.8%. Vulnerability varies substantially across categories, increasing when models lack stable prior knowledge of the relevant products. Reasoning does not mitigate this vulnerability; instead, it often generates spurious social proof to justify false recommendations. We evaluate three defenses: skepticism prompting and consensus filtering (over model priors or cross-document evidence). Skepticism can exacerbate vulnerability, much like reasoning, while filtering risks suppressing legitimate products. We release FORGE at https://github.com/leoluolol/forge-benchmark.
中文标题/摘要
标题：一页污染内容已足够：评估生成式推荐系统中的网络内容污染
搜索增强型大语言模型正越来越多地通过检索实时网络内容来介导日常消费者推荐，这带来了一种新风险：生成式推荐系统可能消费被污染的网络内容，例如旨在误导推荐的虚假评论和推广页面。我们提出的问题是：当这些模型消费被污染的检索结果时，它们在多大程度上会无意中推广假产品？为回答此问题，我们引入FORGE（生成环境中虚假在线推荐基准），用于在受控的网络内容污染环境下衡量假产品推广情况。给定上游搜索结果，FORGE在检索到的网页中本地重写真实产品为虚假产品，以模拟网络内容污染，并测量大语言模型推荐假产品的频率。FORGE涵盖15个类别、225个真实产品及5种消费场景。在12个商业及开源权重的大语言模型中，所有模型均存在漏洞：单页污染可导致高达27%的误导率，而前三页全部替换则提升至73.8%。漏洞在不同类别间差异显著，当模型缺乏相关产品的稳定先验知识时，漏洞更严重。推理无法缓解此漏洞，反而常生成虚假的社会证明以支持错误推荐。我们评估了三种防御方法：怀疑性提示与共识过滤（基于模型先验或跨文档证据）。怀疑性提示可能加剧漏洞，如同推理一般，而过滤则可能抑制真实产品。我们将在https://github.com/leoluolol/forge-benchmark发布FORGE。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the risk that search-augmented LLMs may unknowingly promote fake products by consuming polluted web content, the authors introduce FORGE, a benchmark that simulates web-content pollution by locally rewriting real products in search results into fake ones. They evaluate 12 LLMs across 225 real-world products and find that even a single polluted page can fool models at up to 27% rate, rising to 73.8% with top-3 replacements. 鉴于搜索增强型大语言模型可能因消费被污染的网页内容而无意推广假货，作者提出了FORGE基准测试，通过在检索结果中局部改写真实商品为假货来模拟网络内容污染。他们在225个真实商品上测试了12个大模型，发现所有模型均存在漏洞，单页污染可导致27%的推荐为假货，若前三页全部替换则升至73.8%。漏洞程度因品类而异，且当模型缺乏相关商品先验知识时更严重。推理无法缓解漏洞，反而常生成虚假社会证明。测试的两种防御方法——怀疑引导和共识过滤——均存在缺陷：怀疑引导可能加剧漏洞，而过滤可能误删真实商品。
AgentBeats: Agentifying Agent Assessment for Openness, Standardization, and Reproducibility
Authors: Xiaoyuan Liu, Jianhong Tu, Yuqi Chen, Siyuan Xie, Sihan Ren, Tianneng Shi, Gal Gantar, Evan Sandoval, Donghyun Lee, Daniel Miao, Peter J. Gilbert, Nick Hynes, Mauro Staver, Warren He, David Marn, Andrew Low, Xi Zhang, Elron Bandel, Michal Shmueli-Scheuer, Siva Reddy, Alexandre Drouin, Alexandre Lacoste, Ramayya Krishnan, Elham Tabassi, Yu Su, Victor Barres, Chenguang Wang, Wenbo Guo, Dawn Song
First: 2026-06-11T17:23:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:23:54+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Agent systems are advancing quickly across domains, but their evaluation remains fragmented. Most benchmarks rely on fixed, LLM-centric harnesses that require heavy integration, create test-production mismatch, and limit fair comparison across diverse agent designs. The root problem is the lack of an open, agent-agnostic assessment interface. We advocate Agentified Agent Assessment (AAA), where evaluation is performed by judge agents and all participants interact through standardized protocols: A2A for task management and MCP for tool access. Conventional benchmarking defines two separate interfaces, one for the benchmark and one for the agent, while AAA only needs one; this yields a generic, unified framework that separates assessment logic from agent implementation and enables reproducible, interoperable, and multi-agent evaluation. We further introduce AgentBeats as a concrete realization of AAA: we identify five practical operation modes that make standardized assessment compatible with real-world constraints on openness, privacy, and reproducibility. To evaluate our design at scale, we conduct two studies: a five-month open competition that drew 298 judge agents across 12 categories together with 467 subject agents from independent participants, showing that AAA applies across a heterogeneous range of benchmarks; and a case study on coding agents that confirms agentified evaluation preserves fidelity with the public record while surfacing previously missing head-to-head results, yielding research insights about agent design. Combining a community-scale field study and a controlled coding case study, we verify that AAA delivers coverage, practicality, and fidelity across heterogeneous scenarios at scale. Together, AAA and AgentBeats offer a clear path toward open, standardized, and reproducible agent assessment.
