标题：AgentBeats：面向开放性、标准化与可复现性的代理评估代理化
代理系统在各领域快速发展，但其评估仍显碎片化。多数基准测试依赖固定且以大语言模型为中心的测试框架，需大量集成，导致测试与生产环境不一致，且限制了不同代理设计间的公平比较。根本问题是缺乏开放、代理无关的评估接口。我们倡导代理化代理评估（AAA），即由裁判代理执行评估，所有参与者通过标准化协议交互：A2A用于任务管理，MCP用于工具访问。传统基准测试定义两个独立接口——一个用于基准，一个用于代理，而AAA仅需一个接口，从而构建通用统一框架，将评估逻辑与代理实现分离，实现可复现、可互操作及多代理评估。我们进一步提出AgentBeats作为AAA的具体实现：识别五种实用操作模式，使标准化评估兼容现实世界对开放性、隐私性和可复现性的约束。 为大规模评估设计，我们开展两项研究：一项为期五个月的公开竞赛，汇集12类共298个裁判代理及467个独立参与者提交的被测代理，证明AAA适用于异构基准测试；另一项针对编程代理的案例研究，证实代理化评估在保持公开记录真实性的同时，揭示此前缺失的直接对比结果，为代理设计提供研究洞见。结合社区规模实地研究与受控编程案例研究，我们验证AAA在异构场景下具备覆盖性、实用性与真实性。AAA与AgentBeats共同为开放、标准化、可复现的代理评估提供清晰路径。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the fragmented and LLM-centric nature of current agent evaluation, this work proposes Agentified Agent Assessment (AAA), a unified framework where judge agents evaluate subject agents through standardized protocols (A2A for task management and MCP for tool access), eliminating the need for separate benchmark and agent interfaces. AgentBeats, a concrete implementation of AAA, defines five operational modes to accommodate real-world constraints like privacy and reproducibility. Experimental results from a five-month open competition involving 298 judge agents and 467 subject agents across 12 categories, plus a controlled coding case study, demonstrate AAA’s broad applicability, fidelity to public records, and ability to reveal previously hidden comparative performance, validating its scalability, practicality, and interoperability across diverse agent designs.
为解决当前评估体系碎片化、过度依赖LLM的问题，本文提出Agentified Agent Assessment（AAA）框架，通过标准化协议（A2A用于任务管理，MCP用于工具调用）让裁判代理评估主体代理，消除传统评估中基准与代理分离的接口设计，实现评估逻辑与代理实现解耦，提升可复现性与互操作性。其具体实现AgentBeats定义了五种操作模式，以适应开放性、隐私性和可复现性等现实约束。实验结果表明，通过为期五个月的开放竞赛（涵盖298个裁判代理和467个主体代理，12个类别）及编码代理的控制案例研究，AAA具备可扩展性、评估保真度，并能揭示此前被忽略的代理间对比结果，验证其在异构场景下的实用性与有效性。
Reasoning as Pattern Matching: Shared Mechanisms in Human and LLM Everyday Reasoning
Authors: Zach Studdiford, Gary Lupyan
First: 2026-06-11T17:23:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:23:10+00:00
Comments: 13 pages main text, 51 pages supplementary text
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
When large language models (LLMs) fail to generalize or make haphazard errors in reasoning, it is often taken as evidence that LLMs are not truly reasoning, but rather performing a kind of pattern matching. The implication is that people's behavior does not exhibit the same types of failures because human reasoning uses principled and abstract world models. We evaluate human participants and 25 LLMs on their ability to engage in common-sense reasoning about a variety of everyday situations and observe similar patterns of errors in both people and models. We then identify the set of attention heads driving LLM responses and find that these heads implement a form of pattern-matching. These attention heads allow us to predict seemingly inexplicable reasoning errors in people caused by ostensibly irrelevant prompt details. Taken together, our results suggest that everyday causal reasoning in people and LLMs is more consistent with a form of pattern-matching than with abstract world models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：推理即模式匹配：人类与大语言模型日常推理中的共同机制
当大语言模型（LLMs）在推理中无法泛化或出现随意错误时，常被视为其并非真正推理，而是在进行某种模式匹配。其隐含含义是，人类行为不会出现相同类型的错误，因为人类推理依赖于原则性和抽象的世界模型。我们对人类参与者和25个LLMs在多种日常情境中的常识推理能力进行评估，发现人与模型均表现出相似的错误模式。随后我们识别出驱动LLMs响应的注意力头，并发现这些头实现了某种模式匹配机制。这些注意力头使我们能够预测由看似无关的提示细节引发的人类看似不可解释的推理错误。综合来看，我们的结果表明，人类与LLMs的日常因果推理更符合模式匹配，而非抽象世界模型。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether large language models (LLMs) and humans share similar reasoning mechanisms by testing both on common-sense reasoning tasks. The motivation stems from the observation that LLMs often make seemingly arbitrary errors, leading to the assumption that they merely match patterns rather than reason abstractly. The researchers found that both humans and LLMs exhibit comparable error patterns across everyday reasoning tasks, and by analyzing attention heads in LLMs, they identified specific mechanisms that function as pattern-matching systems. These mechanisms can predict human reasoning errors caused by irrelevant prompt details, suggesting that both humans and LLMs rely on pattern-matching rather than principled abstract world models for everyday causal reasoning.
该研究通过测试人类与25个大语言模型在常识推理任务中的表现，发现两者在错误模式上高度相似。研究人员定位了驱动模型推理的特定注意力头，发现其运作机制为模式匹配而非抽象世界模型。更重要的是，这些注意力头能够预测人类因无关提示细节而产生的推理错误，表明人类与大语言模型在日常因果推理中，更符合模式匹配机制而非抽象建模。
Distribution-Agnostic Robust Trajectory Optimization via Chance-Constrained Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Yashdeep Chaudhary, Roberto Armellin, Harry Holt, Marco Sagliano
First: 2026-06-11T17:22:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:22:05+00:00
Comments: Preprint. 39 pages, 16 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper presents a distribution-agnostic robust trajectory-optimization framework based on chance-constrained reinforcement learning. The uncertainty is represented here through initial conditions and process noise, with the only requirement being that it can be sampled. A deterministic nominal trajectory is first computed offline, and reinforcement learning is then used only to robustify that baseline through a structured affine closed-loop correction law comprising a feedforward control adjustment and time-varying feedback gains. Probabilistic feasibility is enforced empirically through rollout-based upper-tail quantiles, while terminal dispersion is regulated through covariance-feasibility penalties. The framework is assessed on two materially different trajectory design problems. The flagship case study is a three-dimensional multi-impulse Earth-Mars transfer, where the learned policy is benchmarked against a recent robust trajectory-optimization reference under Gaussian uncertainty and then evaluated under bounded uniform uncertainty and under process disturbances not seen during training. The second case study is a stochastic atmospheric pinpoint rocket landing problem, used to assess portability to a short-horizon continuous-thrust setting with drag, mass depletion, and glide-slope constraints. The results show that the proposed framework can remain competitive in upper-tail fuel cost while preserving probabilistic feasibility, and that the same robustification scaffold can be carried across heterogeneous spacecraft trajectory planning problems without redesign of its core stochastic-control structure.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于概率约束强化学习的分布无关鲁棒轨迹优化
本文提出了一种基于概率约束强化学习的分布无关鲁棒轨迹优化框架。不确定性通过初始条件和过程噪声表示，唯一要求是其可采样。首先离线计算确定性基准轨迹，然后仅使用强化学习对该基准进行结构化仿射闭环校正（包含前馈控制调整和时变反馈增益），以增强鲁棒性。概率可行性通过基于 rollout 的上尾分位数经验强制执行，终端分散性通过协方差可行性惩罚调节。该框架在两个本质不同的轨迹设计问题上进行了评估。旗舰案例为三维多脉冲地球-火星转移，所学策略在高斯不确定性下与近期鲁棒轨迹优化基准对比，并在有界均匀不确定性和训练中未见的过程扰动下进行评估。第二个案例为随机大气精确火箭着陆问题，用于评估其在短时域连续推力设置（含阻力、质量消耗和滑翔坡度约束）下的可移植性。结果表明，该框架在保持概率可行性的同时，上尾燃料成本仍具竞争力，且同一鲁棒化框架可应用于异构航天器轨迹规划问题，无需重新设计其核心随机控制结构。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for robust trajectory optimization under uncertain conditions without assuming specific probability distributions, this paper introduces a chance-constrained reinforcement learning framework that uses sampled uncertainty to robustify a deterministic nominal trajectory. The method employs a structured affine closed-loop correction law with feedforward adjustments and time-varying feedback gains, enforcing probabilistic feasibility via rollout-based quantiles and regulating terminal dispersion through covariance penalties. Experimental results on a 3D Earth-Mars transfer and a stochastic rocket landing problem demonstrate that the framework maintains competitive fuel efficiency while preserving probabilistic feasibility across different uncertainty types, including those unseen during training, and shows transferability to diverse spacecraft trajectory planning scenarios without structural redesign.
为解决在未知分布不确定性下轨迹优化的鲁棒性问题，本文提出一种基于机会约束强化学习的分布无关框架，仅需不确定性可采样即可。首先离线计算确定性基准轨迹，再通过强化学习优化结构化仿射闭环修正律（含前馈控制调整与时变反馈增益）以增强鲁棒性。概率可行性通过滚动生成的上尾分位数实证保障，终端离散度则通过协方差可行性惩罚控制。在三维地火转移与随机火箭着陆两个异构场景中测试，该方法在保持概率可行性的同时，燃料消耗表现与基准方法相当，且其核心随机控制结构可直接迁移至不同航天器轨迹规划问题，无需重新设计。
Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning from Delayed Marketplace Feedback for Objective-Weight Adaptation in Three-Sided Dispatch
Authors: Haochen Wu, Yi Hou, Shiguang Xie
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-06-11T17:21:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:21:20+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ICML 2026 Workshop on Reinforcement Learning from World Feedback (RLxF)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Dispatch in three-sided marketplaces provides a natural setting for reinforcement learning from world feedback: decisions are evaluated by delayed operational outcomes such as delivery speed, courier utilization, and merchant congestion. We present a deployed reinforcement learning system at DoorDash that adapts dispatch objective weights in a large-scale food-delivery marketplace using delayed signals. Rather than replacing the combinatorial assignment optimizer, a store-level policy learned from logged marketplace data selects a discrete multiplier that shifts the dispatch optimizer's tradeoff between delivery quality and batching efficiency. This interface enables offline policy learning under noisy, delayed, and coupled feedback while preserving production feasibility constraints and operational safeguards. We train a shared value function using centralized offline data and decentralized store-level execution, with Double Q-learning targets and a conservative regularizer to reduce out-of-distribution value overestimation. In a production switchback experiment, the offline-trained policy increases batching and reduces courier-side time costs without degrading customer-facing delivery quality. Results illustrate how world feedback from a live economic and logistics system can be used to safely adapt decision policies online.

标题：基于延迟市场反馈的多智能体强化学习用于三方派单中的目标权重自适应
三方市场中的派单为从世界反馈中进行强化学习提供了自然场景：决策由延迟的操作结果（如配送速度、骑手利用率和商家拥堵）评估。我们提出了一种在DoorDash部署的强化学习系统，利用延迟信号在大规模食品配送市场中自适应调整派单目标权重。该系统并未替代组合分配优化器，而是通过从日志数据中学习的店铺级策略，选择一个离散乘数，调整派单优化器在配送质量与批量效率之间的权衡。该接口支持在噪声、延迟且耦合的反馈下进行离线策略学习，同时保留生产可行性约束和运营保障。我们使用集中式离线数据和分布式店铺级执行训练共享价值函数，采用Double Q学习目标和保守正则化器以减少分布外价值高估。在生产切换实验中，离线训练的策略提升了批量效率并降低了骑手时间成本，而未损害面向客户的配送质量。结果展示了如何利用真实经济与物流系统的延迟反馈安全地在线自适应决策策略。

The method trains a shared value function via centralized offline data and decentralized execution, using Double Q-learning and a conservative regularizer to mitigate overestimation under noisy feedback. In production experiments, the learned policy improved batching efficiency and reduced courier time costs while maintaining or improving delivery quality for customers.
为在DoorDash三边市场中利用延迟运营反馈实时调整派单目标，作者构建了一种多智能体强化学习系统，通过学习门店级策略动态调节派单优化器的权衡权重，而不替换核心组合优化器。该方法采用集中式离线数据训练共享价值函数，结合分布式门店执行，使用Double Q学习与保守正则化以缓解延迟、噪声反馈下的价值高估问题。在生产切换实验中，该策略提升了派单批量化效率、降低了骑手时间成本，同时保持面向顾客的配送质量，验证了真实经济与物流系统反馈可安全用于在线策略调整。
Beyond the Commitment Boundary: Probing Epiphenomenal Chain-of-Thought in Large Reasoning Models
Authors: Daniel Scalena, Sara Candussio, Luca Bortolussi, Elisabetta Fersini, Malvina Nissim, Gabriele Sarti
First: 2026-06-11T17:21:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:21:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning is the dominant paradigm for inference-time scaling in language models, yet the causal influence of individual steps on the final answer poorly understood. We estimate each step's causal importance via early exit and use this measure to study how answers form across the reasoning traces of several model families. Across diverse tasks, we find that reasoning typically crosses a \emph{commitment boundary} -- a sharp transition from transient intermediate guesses to a stable, high-confidence answer. This transition often happens in a single step, well before the model's reasoning block ends, and is followed by \emph{epiphenomenal} CoT steps that leave the final answer probability unaltered. Using attention probes, we show that answer-formation stages can be linearly decoded from intermediate reasoning steps with high accuracy and generalize robustly to unseen reasoning tasks. We exploit this signal to early-exit reasoning blocks at the commitment boundary, reducing the length of CoTs up to 55\% on average with negligible impact on model performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越承诺边界：探究大型推理模型中的表观链式思维
链式思维（CoT）推理是语言模型推理时扩展的主要范式，但各步骤对最终答案的因果影响尚不明确。我们通过早期退出估算每个步骤的因果重要性，并以此衡量不同模型家族推理轨迹中答案如何形成。在多种任务中，我们发现推理通常跨越一个\emph{承诺边界}——即从临时中间猜测到稳定高置信度答案的急剧转变。这一转变常在单步内完成，远早于模型推理块结束，并随后出现\emph{表观}的CoT步骤，这些步骤不改变最终答案的概率。通过注意力探针，我们证明可从中间推理步骤高精度线性解码答案形成阶段，并在未见推理任务中稳健泛化。我们利用这一信号在承诺边界处提前退出推理块，平均将CoT长度缩短55%，且对模型性能影响微乎其微。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of understanding of how individual reasoning steps influence final answers in large language models, this study uses early-exit techniques to estimate causal importance of each step in chain-of-thought reasoning. Across multiple model families and tasks, it identifies a 'commitment boundary' — a sharp transition from uncertain intermediate guesses to a stable, high-confidence answer, often occurring in a single step before reasoning completion. Subsequent steps beyond this boundary are epiphenomenal, having no effect on the final probability. Attention probes reveal that answer formation can be accurately decoded from intermediate steps and generalizes well. The researchers leverage this insight to early-exit reasoning chains at the commitment boundary, achieving up to 55% reduction in CoT length without compromising model performance.
为了解决大语言模型推理过程中各步骤对最终输出因果影响不明的问题，该研究提出通过早期退出机制评估每个推理步骤的因果重要性。研究发现，模型在推理过程中存在一个明显的‘承诺边界’，即从临时猜测迅速过渡到稳定高置信度答案，这一转变常在单步内完成，之后的步骤为表象性推理，不影响最终答案概率。通过注意力探针，研究证实可从中间推理步骤高精度解码答案形成阶段，并在新任务中保持鲁棒性，从而实现将推理链长度缩短55%以上，且模型性能几乎无损。
EpiBench: Verifiable Evaluation of AI Agents on Epigenomics Analysis
Authors: Harihara Muralidharan, Reema Baskar, Soo Hee Lee, Tim Proctor, Kenny Workman
First: 2026-06-11T17:20:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:20:29+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce EpiBench, a verifiable benchmark for short-horizon epigenomics analysis. EpiBench evaluates whether agents can make well-defined analysis decisions from realistic workflow states and return deterministically gradable answers. The benchmark includes 106 evaluations across CUT\&Tag/CUT\&RUN, ATAC-seq, ChIP-seq, and DNA methylation workflows. Across 5,088 valid trajectories from 16 model-harness pairs, no system passed a majority of attempts: GPT-5.5 / Pi led at 45.0\% (143/318 attempts; 95\% confidence interval (CI), 36.3--53.7), followed by GPT-5.5 / OpenAI Codex at 39.9\% (127/318 attempts; 95\% CI, 31.6--48.3). Claude Opus 4.8 Max / Pi and GPT-5.4 / Pi each passed 39.0\% (124/318 attempts; 95\% CI, 30.2--47.8 and 31.0--47.0, respectively). Performance varies across assay types, and many failed runs still contain parts of the correct answer. Agents often found the right files and computed useful intermediate results, but failed when the task required deeper, assay-specific scientific judgment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：EpiBench：用于表观基因组学分析的AI代理可验证评估基准
我们引入了EpiBench，一个用于短期表观基因组学分析的可验证基准。该基准评估代理是否能从现实的工作流状态中做出明确的分析决策，并返回可确定性评分的答案。该基准涵盖CUT&Tag/CUT&RUN、ATAC-seq、ChIP-seq和DNA甲基化工作流共106项评估。在16组模型-工具对的5,088条有效轨迹中，无系统通过多数尝试：GPT-5.5 / Pi以45.0%（143/318次尝试；95%置信区间CI为36.3–53.7）领先，其次是GPT-5.5 / OpenAI Codex以39.9%（127/318次尝试；95% CI为31.6–48.3）。Claude Opus 4.8 Max / Pi和GPT-5.4 / Pi各通过39.0%（124/318次尝试；95% CI分别为30.2–47.8和31.0–47.0）。不同检测方法表现各异，许多失败的运行仍包含部分正确答案。代理通常能正确找到所需文件并计算出有用中间结果，但在需要更深层次、特定检测方法的科学判断时失败。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for reliable, verifiable evaluation of AI agents in epigenomics analysis, EpiBench was designed as a short-horizon benchmark that tests agents’ ability to make well-defined decisions from realistic workflow states and return deterministically gradable outputs. The benchmark comprises 106 evaluations across four major epigenomic assays, with 5,088 valid trajectories tested across 16 model-harness pairs. Performance varied by assay type, and while agents often correctly identified files and computed useful intermediates, they frequently failed on tasks requiring deeper, assay-specific scientific judgment.
为评估AI代理在表观基因组学分析中的可靠性，EpiBench构建了一个可验证的短周期基准，测试代理能否从真实工作流状态中做出明确决策并返回可确定评分的答案。该基准涵盖106项评估，覆盖四种主流表观基因组学技术，共评估5088条轨迹，来自16组模型组合。结果显示，无系统通过多数测试，表现最佳的是GPT-5.5/Pi（45.0%，143/318次），其次是GPT-5.5/OpenAI Codex（39.9%）。不同实验技术表现差异显著，代理虽常能正确识别文件并计算中间结果，但在需要深度、技术特异性科学判断的任务中普遍失败。
MCR-Bionic Hand: Anatomical Structural Priors for Dexterous Manipulation
Authors: Haosen Yang, Guowu Wei
First: 2026-06-11T17:20:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:20:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Dexterous robotic hands are usually formulated as high dimensional active control systems governed by degrees of freedom, actuation, and algorithms. Human hand dexterity, however, is partly encoded in the physical architecture of bones, ligaments, tendons, aponeuroses, and intrinsic muscles. This work describes that contribution as two linked forms of structural intelligence: structural prior generation, in which wrist to finger tenodesis, FDS/FDP routing, and the dorsal extensor hood transform low dimensional posture inputs into default grasp configurations and PIP to DIP coordination; and muscle mediated modulation, in which extrinsic muscles, lumbricals, and interossei regulate MCP posture, distal stability, fingertip force paths, and contact states around that default state. Based on this framework, MCR-Bionic Hand is developed as a 1:1 musculoskeletal biomimetic hand integrating a two row eight bone wrist, cross wrist tendons, anatomical flexor routing, volar plate and collateral ligament constraints, the dorsal extensor hood, and intrinsic muscle pathways within one body. Functional demonstrations and geometric mechanical models show that wrist posture induces multi joint pre shaping, the extensor hood maps PIP posture to a coupled DIP response, and intrinsic plus pathways modulate distal stability and fingertip action direction after grasp formation. Contact rich tasks, including coin rotation, pen transfer, dorsal coin flipping, and cube manipulation, show that MCR-Bionic links low dimensional state generation with fine post contact modulation. These results suggest that anatomical biomimetics is valuable not for visual similarity, but for identifying human hand structures that perform part of control.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MCR仿生手：解剖结构先验用于灵巧操作
灵巧机器人手通常被建模为由自由度、驱动和算法控制的高维主动控制系统。然而，人类手的灵巧性部分编码于骨骼、韧带、肌腱、筋膜和内在肌肉的物理结构中。本工作将此贡献描述为两种关联的结构智能形式：结构先验生成，其中腕至指的肌腱固定、FDS/FDP肌腱路径及背侧伸肌腱膜将低维姿态输入转化为默认抓取构型及PIP至DIP协调；以及肌肉介导的调制，其中外在肌肉、蚓状肌和骨间肌调节MCP姿态、远端稳定性、指尖力路径及围绕默认状态的接触状态。 基于此框架，MCR仿生手被开发为1:1比例的肌肉骨骼仿生手，集成双排八骨腕关节、交叉腕肌腱、解剖屈肌路径、掌侧板与侧副韧带约束、背侧伸肌腱膜及内在肌肉通路于一体。功能演示与几何力学模型表明，腕关节姿态诱导多关节预成形，伸肌腱膜将PIP姿态映射为耦合的DIP响应，内在肌肉通路在抓取形成后调节远端稳定性和指尖动作方向。接触密集任务，包括硬币旋转、笔传递、背侧硬币翻转及立方体操作，表明MCR仿生手将低维状态生成与精细接触后调制相联系。这些结果表明，解剖仿生的价值不在于视觉相似性，而在于识别执行部分控制的人手结构。
Summary / 总结
This work motivates the development of dexterous robotic hands by leveraging human hand anatomy as a structural control mechanism rather than relying solely on high-dimensional actuation. The MCR-Bionic Hand is designed as a biomimetic system incorporating anatomical features like wrist tenodesis, flexor routing, extensor hood, and intrinsic muscle pathways to translate low-dimensional posture inputs into coordinated grasp configurations and fine fingertip control. Experimental results demonstrate that wrist posture pre-shapes multi-joint movements, the extensor hood couples PIP and DIP finger motions, and intrinsic muscles modulate distal stability and contact forces post-grasp. The hand successfully performs contact-rich tasks such as coin rotation and cube manipulation, proving that anatomical structure enables effective control without requiring complex algorithms.
受人类手部灵巧性部分源于解剖结构而非单纯控制算法的启发，本研究提出MCR-Bionic Hand，一种仿生肌骨结构的机械手。该方法通过集成腕指腱系、屈肌路径、背侧伸肌腱膜及内在肌路径等解剖结构先验，将低维姿态输入转化为默认抓取构型并调节指尖行为。实验表明，腕部姿态可预形多关节，伸肌腱膜耦合PIP与DIP运动，内在肌调节远端稳定性和接触力方向。在硬币旋转、立方体操作等任务中，该手以低维输入实现精细接触后调控，证明解剖结构本身对功能灵巧性具有关键作用，而非仅视觉相似性。
Reasoning Models Know What's Important, and Encode It in Their Activations
Authors: Yaniv Nikankin, Martin Tutek, Tomer Ashuach, Jonathan Rosenfeld, Yonatan Belinkov
First: 2026-04-20T14:15:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:17:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Language models often solve complex tasks by generating long reasoning chains, consisting of many steps with varying importance. While some steps are crucial for generating the final answer, others are removable. Determining which steps matter most, and why, remains an open question central to understanding how models process reasoning. We investigate if this question is best approached through model internals or through tokens of the reasoning chain itself. We find that model activations contain more information than tokens for identifying important reasoning steps. Crucially, by training probes on model activations to predict importance, we show that models encode an internal representation of step importance, even prior to the generation of subsequent steps. The internal representations of importance in different models yield high agreement on which steps are important. The representation is distributed across layers, and does not correlate with surface-level features, such as a step's relative position or its length. Our findings suggest that analyzing activations can reveal aspects of reasoning that surface-level approaches fundamentally miss, indicating that reasoning analyses should look into model internals.
中文标题/摘要
标题：推理模型知道什么是重要的，并将其编码在激活中
语言模型常通过生成包含多个步骤的长推理链来解决复杂任务，其中步骤的重要性各异。虽然部分步骤对生成最终答案至关重要，其他步骤则可被移除。确定哪些步骤最重要及其原因，仍是理解模型如何处理推理的核心开放问题。我们探究该问题应通过模型内部机制还是推理链中的token本身来解决。研究发现，模型激活包含比token更丰富的信息，可用于识别重要推理步骤。关键的是，通过在模型激活上训练探测器以预测步骤重要性，我们证明模型在生成后续步骤前已编码了步骤重要性的内部表示。不同模型的重要性内部表示在识别重要步骤上高度一致，该表示分布于各层，且与表面特征（如步骤相对位置或长度）无相关性。我们的发现表明，分析激活可揭示表面方法根本无法捕捉的推理方面，提示推理分析应深入模型内部。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates how language models identify and encode the importance of different steps in their reasoning chains, motivated by the need to understand how models process complex tasks. The authors compare model activations with surface-level tokens to determine which carries more information about step importance, finding that activations are superior. By training probes on activations to predict step importance, they demonstrate that models internally encode a representation of which steps matter, even before generating later steps. Across different models, these internal representations consistently agree on important steps, and the encoding is distributed across layers without relying on surface features like position or length, suggesting that analyzing activations reveals reasoning insights missed by token-based methods.
该研究旨在理解语言模型如何识别并编码其推理链中各步骤的重要性，动机源于对复杂任务处理机制的探索。研究人员对比模型激活与表面层token，发现激活包含更多信息。通过在激活上训练探针预测步骤重要性，他们证明模型在生成后续步骤前已内部编码关键步骤的表示。该内部表示在不同模型间高度一致，分布于多层，且与位置或长度等表面特征无关，表明分析激活能揭示表面方法无法触及的推理机制。
Reward Modeling for Multi-Agent Orchestration
Authors: King Yeung Tsang, Zihao Zhao, Vishal Venkataramani, Haizhou Shi, Zixuan Ke, Semih Yavuz, Shafiq Joty, Hao Wang
First: 2026-06-11T17:16:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:16:24+00:00
Comments: Preprint; work in progress
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Multi-Agent Systems (MAS) built on Large Language Models (LLMs) require effective orchestration to coordinate specialized agents, yet training such orchestrators is hindered by limited supervision and high computational cost. We propose Orchestration Reward Modeling (OrchRM), a self-supervised framework for evaluating orchestration quality without human annotations. OrchRM leverages intermediate artifacts from multi-agent executions to construct win-lose pairs for Bradley-Terry reward model training. Unlike existing MAS test-time scaling and orchestrator training frameworks that rely on costly sub-agent rollouts, OrchRM operates directly at the orchestration level, enabling efficient and high-performing reward-guided orchestrator training and MAS test-time scaling. OrchRM improves training efficiency by up to 10x in token usage while improving MAS test-time scaling performance by up to 8% in accuracy. These gains consistently transfer across multiple domains, including mathematical reasoning, web-based question answering, and multi-hop reasoning, demonstrating orchestration-level reward modeling as a scalable direction for robust multi-agent orchestration. Code will be available at https://github.com/Wang-ML-Lab/OrchRM.

标题：多智能体编排的奖励建模

To address the challenge of training orchestrators for multi-agent systems built on large language models—where supervision is scarce and computation expensive—the paper introduces OrchRM, a self-supervised reward modeling framework that evaluates orchestration quality using intermediate execution artifacts to generate win-lose pairs for training a Bradley-Terry reward model. By operating directly at the orchestration level rather than relying on costly sub-agent rollouts, OrchRM enables efficient reward-guided training and test-time scaling. Experiments show it reduces token usage by up to 10x while improving test-time accuracy by up to 8% across domains like mathematical reasoning and multi-hop reasoning, demonstrating its broad scalability and effectiveness.
为解决基于大语言模型的多智能体系统中训练协调器时监督数据稀缺、计算成本高昂的问题，本文提出OrchRM框架，通过利用多智能体执行过程中的中间产物构建胜败对，训练布拉德利-泰勒奖励模型，实现无需人工标注的自监督评估。该方法直接在协调层操作，避免昂贵的子智能体重放，从而提升训练效率和测试时扩展性能。实验表明，其在token使用上最高节省10倍，测试准确率提升最高达8%，并在数学推理、网页问答等多领域稳定表现，验证了协调层奖励建模的可扩展性和鲁棒性。
Variational Learning for Insertion-based Generation
Authors: Yangtian Zhang, Zhe Wang, Arthur Gretton, Rex Ying, David van Dijk, Michalis K. Titsias, Jiaxin Shi
First: 2026-06-01T11:59:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:13:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Non-monotonic sequence generation methods, such as masked diffusion models, provide a flexible alternative to left-to-right autoregressive modeling by allowing tokens to be generated in non-fixed and prescribed orders. Despite their practical advantages, most existing non-monotonic models are order-agnostic and rely on a fixed-length grid, limiting their ability to support variable-length generation and adaptive insertion order. In this work, we introduce a probabilistic framework for learning insertion order in variable-length insertion models. We formalize a bijective correspondence between insertion trajectories and permutations, which enables an exact reparameterization of the data likelihood as a sum over permutations. Building on this result, we propose the Insertion Process (IP), a stochastic generative model that jointly learns where to insert, what to insert, and when to terminate, trained via permutation-based variational inference. Unlike prior fixed-canvas approaches, IP natively supports variable-length generation and learns data-driven preferences over insertion orders. Experiments on goal-conditioned planning and molecular string generation demonstrate that learning insertion order improves both modeling quality and generalization in domains without a canonical left-to-right structure.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于插入的生成的变分学习
非单调序列生成方法（如掩码扩散模型）通过允许令牌按非固定和预设顺序生成，为自左向右的自回归建模提供了灵活的替代方案。尽管具有实际优势，大多数现有非单调模型是顺序无关的，并依赖固定长度网格，限制了其支持可变长度生成和自适应插入顺序的能力。本文提出了一种用于学习可变长度插入模型中插入顺序的概率框架。我们形式化了插入轨迹与排列之间的一一对应关系，从而实现了数据似然的精确重参数化，即作为排列之和。基于此结果，我们提出了插入过程（IP），一种联合学习插入位置、插入内容和终止时机的随机生成模型，通过基于排列的变分推断进行训练。与先前的固定画布方法不同，IP 原生支持可变长度生成，并学习数据驱动的插入顺序偏好。在目标条件规划和分子字符串生成实验中，结果表明学习插入顺序可提升无标准左到右结构领域的建模质量和泛化能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of fixed-order generation in non-monotonic models, this work proposes a probabilistic framework to learn adaptive insertion orders in variable-length sequence generation. It formalizes a bijection between insertion trajectories and permutations, enabling exact likelihood reparameterization and introduces the Insertion Process (IP), a variational model trained via permutation-based inference to jointly learn insertion positions, content, and termination. Experiments on goal-conditioned planning and molecular string generation show that IP outperforms fixed-grid baselines in modeling quality and generalization, especially in domains lacking canonical left-to-right structure.
为克服现有非单调模型固定顺序生成的局限性，本文提出一种概率框架，用于在可变长度生成中学习自适应插入顺序。通过建立插入轨迹与排列之间的双射关系，实现数据似然的精确重参数化，并提出插入过程（IP）模型，该模型通过排列基础变分推断联合学习插入位置、内容与终止时机。在目标导向规划与分子字符串生成实验中，IP在缺乏标准左到右结构的领域中，显著优于固定画布模型，提升了建模质量与泛化能力。
Multiagent Protocols with Aggregated Confidence Signals
Authors: Ali Elahi, Barbara Di Eugenio
First: 2026-06-11T17:12:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:12:11+00:00
Comments: 22 pages and 5 figures, 9 pages and 2 figures before the appendix
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Confidence is used for reliability, oversight, and a range of downstream decision tasks in Natural Language Processing (NLP), yet no existing method produces or evaluates a confidence for the output of a multiagent system. Prior work uses confidence within multiagent debate (MAD) to weight messages, trigger debate, or calibrate individual agents, but it never aggregates these into a single confidence for the system itself. We introduce three protocols that produce a final answer along with a single aggregated confidence by first transforming raw confidence signals to make them comparable across models, then combining them via soft voting or a probability fusion we call Bayesian fusion. This aggregated confidence is substantially more discriminative (AUARC) than that of the best single agent or the standard debate baselines, while correctness (F1-score) stays stable and recovers the losses MAD incurs on more ambiguous tasks. Analyzing two estimators, sequence probability and self-report, alongside parametric and non-parametric calibrators, we find that calibration improves F1 for both estimators while AUARC is less reliant on it. We evaluate six homogeneous and heterogeneous debating pairs per benchmark, across five benchmarks and four task types, spanning a range of model capabilities and sizes.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多智能体协议与聚合置信度信号
置信度在自然语言处理（NLP）中用于可靠性、监督及多种下游决策任务，但现有方法尚未生成或评估多智能体系统的输出置信度。先前工作在多智能体辩论（MAD）中使用置信度加权消息、触发辩论或校准单个智能体，但从未将其聚合为系统自身的单一置信度。我们提出三种协议，首先将原始置信度信号转换为跨模型可比较的形式，再通过软投票或我们称为贝叶斯融合的概率融合方法进行组合，从而生成最终答案及单一聚合置信度。该聚合置信度在区分度（AUARC）上显著优于最佳单智能体或标准辩论基线，同时正确率（F1分数）保持稳定，并恢复了MAD在更模糊任务中所损失的性能。我们分析了序列概率与自我报告两种估计器，以及参数化与非参数化校准器，发现校准可提升两种估计器的F1分数，而AUARC对校准的依赖性较低。我们在五个基准、四种任务类型上评估了六组同质与异质辩论对，覆盖了不同模型能力和规模。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of confidence evaluation for multiagent systems in NLP, this work introduces three protocols that generate a final answer and a single aggregated confidence by first normalizing raw confidence signals across agents and then combining them via soft voting or Bayesian fusion. Experimental results across five benchmarks and four task types show that the aggregated confidence significantly outperforms individual agents or standard debate baselines in discriminative power (AUARC), while maintaining or recovering correctness (F1-score) on ambiguous tasks. Calibration further improves F1 for both sequence probability and self-report estimators, though AUARC performance is less dependent on calibration.
为解决多智能体系统在自然语言处理中缺乏整体置信度信号的问题，本文提出三种协议，通过先将各智能体原始置信度信号归一化，再采用软投票或贝叶斯融合方法整合为单一系统置信度。实验表明，该聚合置信度在区分能力（AUARC）上显著优于单个智能体或标准辩论基线，同时保持正确率（F1分数）稳定，并弥补了多智能体辩论在模糊任务上的性能损失。校准可提升两种置信度估计器（序列概率与自我报告）的F1分数，但对AUARC的提升作用较小。
Simplex-Constrained Sparse Bagging: Transitioning from Uniform Priors to Sparse Posteriors in Ensemble Learning
Authors: Meher Sai Preetam, Meher Bhaskar
First: 2026-06-11T17:11:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:11:51+00:00
Comments: 6 pages, 3 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present Simplex-Constrained Sparse Bagging (SCSB), a mathematically rigorous framework for post-training compression and probability calibration of bootstrap-based bagging ensembles. Standard bagging ensembles (such as Random Forests, Bagged SVMs, and Bagged Neural Networks) assign uniform voting power to all constituent estimators. However, this naive uniform prior ignores the varying local competence of base estimators and contributes to model overconfidence. We formulate ensemble pruning and calibration as a joint optimization problem over the probability simplex by minimizing the Out-Of-Bag (OOB) loss. To induce sparsity, we address the theoretical "L1-simplex paradox" -- the mathematical reality that the L1 norm is constant on the simplex and fails to prune -- by introducing a concave quadratic penalty. SCSB is model-agnostic and achieves up to 96% ensemble compression, yielding linear inference speedups and superior probability calibration (lowered Expected Calibration Error) while preserving or enhancing generalization accuracy.
中文标题/摘要
标题：单纯形约束稀疏袋装：从均匀先验到稀疏后验的集成学习过渡
我们提出了单纯形约束稀疏袋装（SCSB），这是一种用于基于自助法的袋装集成模型训练后压缩与概率校准的数学严谨框架。标准袋装集成模型（如随机森林、袋装SVM和袋装神经网络）对所有基础估计器赋予均匀投票权重。然而，这种朴素的均匀先验忽略了基础估计器在不同区域的局部能力差异，导致模型过度自信。我们将集成剪枝与校准建模为在概率单纯形上的联合优化问题，通过最小化袋外（OOB）损失实现。为引入稀疏性，我们解决了理论上的“L1单纯形悖论”——即L1范数在单纯形上恒定、无法剪枝的问题，通过引入凹二次惩罚项加以解决。SCSB模型无关，可实现高达96%的集成压缩，带来线性推理加速和更优的概率校准（降低期望校准误差），同时保持或提升泛化精度。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the overconfidence and inefficiency of standard bagging ensembles that assign uniform weights to all base estimators, this paper introduces Simplex-Constrained Sparse Bagging (SCSB), a framework that jointly optimizes ensemble pruning and probability calibration via a joint optimization over the probability simplex. To overcome the theoretical limitation of L1 regularization on the simplex — the so-called L1-simplex paradox — SCSB incorporates a concave quadratic penalty to induce sparsity while preserving model performance. Experimental results show SCSB achieves up to 96% compression of ensembles, delivers linear speedups in inference, improves probability calibration (reducing Expected Calibration Error), and maintains or enhances generalization accuracy across diverse models including Random Forests and Bagged Neural Networks.
为解决传统Bagging集成模型因赋予所有基学习器均匀权重而导致的过自信与效率低下问题，本文提出Simplex-Constrained Sparse Bagging（SCSB）框架，通过在概率单纯形上联合优化集成剪枝与概率校准，最小化Out-Of-Bag损失。为克服L1范数在单纯形上无法实现稀疏性的理论限制，SCSB引入凹二次惩罚项以诱导稀疏性。实验表明，SCSB可实现高达96%的集成压缩，带来线性推理加速，并显著提升校准精度（降低期望校准误差），同时不损害甚至提升泛化精度。
Towards Effective Waste Segmentation for Automated Waste Recycling in Cluttered Background
Authors: Mamoona Javaid, Mubashir Noman, Abdul Hannan, Shah Nawaz, Mustansar Fiaz, Sajid Ghuffar
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-06-11T17:11:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:11:04+00:00
Comments: accepted at ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Rapid expansion of urban areas and population growth is causing an immense increase in waste production, which demands the need for efficient and automated waste management. In this scenario, automated waste recycling (AWR) using deep learning methods can assist humans in optimal waste management. Recent deep learning approaches for AWR provide promising waste segmentation performance, however, these methods rely on large backbone networks that are inefficient for AWR systems and suffer from performance deterioration in cluttered scenes. To this end, an optimal waste segmentation network is introduced which effectively utilizes the spatial domain to capture localized structural dependencies and the spectral domain to efficiently extract global contextual relationships. This cascaded design allows the network to progressively leverage both local and global representations across complementary domains to highlight the semantic information necessary for effective segmentation of various waste objects. Furthermore, auxiliary feature enhancement module (AFEM) is introduced to enhance the target objects' boundaries and blob amplification for better segmentation in cluttered scenarios. Extensive experimentation on ZeroWaste-aug, ZeroWaste-f and SpectralWaste datasets reveals the merits of the proposed method.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向杂乱背景下自动化垃圾回收的有效垃圾分割方法
城市扩张和人口增长导致垃圾产量激增，亟需高效自动化垃圾管理。深度学习方法驱动的自动化垃圾回收（AWR）可辅助人类实现最优管理。现有AWR深度学习方法虽在垃圾分割性能上表现良好，但依赖大型骨干网络，效率低下且在杂乱场景中性能下降。为此，本文提出一种优化的垃圾分割网络，有效利用空间域捕捉局部结构依赖，频谱域高效提取全局上下文关系。该级联设计使网络逐步融合互补域的局部与全局表征，突出各类垃圾对象分割所需的语义信息。此外，引入辅助特征增强模块（AFEM），强化目标对象边界与块状特征放大，提升杂乱场景下的分割效果。在ZeroWaste-aug、ZeroWaste-f和SpectralWaste数据集上的大量实验验证了该方法的优势。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the surge in urban waste due to population growth, this work proposes a novel waste segmentation network for automated waste recycling that addresses inefficiencies of existing deep learning models in cluttered environments. The method combines spatial and spectral domains in a cascaded architecture to progressively integrate local structural and global contextual features, enhancing semantic understanding for precise segmentation. An auxiliary feature enhancement module further sharpens object boundaries and amplifies blob-like structures, improving performance in complex backgrounds. Experiments on ZeroWaste-aug, ZeroWaste-f, and SpectralWaste datasets demonstrate significant gains in segmentation accuracy and robustness compared to baseline methods.
为应对城市扩张和人口增长导致的垃圾激增问题，本文提出一种面向复杂背景自动垃圾分类的优化分割网络。该方法通过级联架构融合空间域与光谱域，分别捕捉局部结构特征与全局上下文关系，提升语义理解能力。引入辅助特征增强模块，强化目标边界并放大模糊区域，提高复杂背景下的分割精度。在ZeroWaste-aug、ZeroWaste-f和SpectralWaste三个数据集上的实验表明，该方法在分割准确率和效率上优于现有深度学习方法。
ReFoCUS: Reinforcement-guided Frame Optimization for Contextual Understanding
Authors: Hosu Lee, Junho Kim, Hyunjun Kim, Yong Man Ro
First: 2025-06-02T03:08:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:06:50+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://interlive-team.github.io/ReFoCUS/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Recent progress in Large Multi-modal Models (LMMs) has enabled effective vision-language reasoning, yet the ability to video understanding remains constrained by suboptimal frame selection strategies, albeit with the rapid development of video-specialized LMMs. Prior works attempted to solve this with static heuristics or external retrieval modules to feed frame-level information, but these approaches often fail to capture visual cues grounded to the given user queries conflating raw visual dynamics with true semantic relevance. In this paper, we introduce ReFoCUS (Reinforcement-guided Frame Optimization for Contextual UnderStanding), the first framework to integrate online policy-gradient reinforcement learning into frame-level optimization for video-LLMs. ReFoCUS aims to learn a frame selection policy, leveraging reward signals derived from reference models to capture their underlying scoring behavior over frame combinations that best support temporally grounded responses. To efficiently explore the large combinatorial frame space, we employ an autoregressive and query-conditional selection architecture that ensures contextual consistency while reducing complexity. Our policy learning removes the need for explicit frame-level supervision, as it implicitly discovers optimal and semantically consistent frame compositions. ReFoCUS consistently improves reasoning accuracy across multiple video QA benchmarks, demonstrating the advantage of aligning frame selection with model-internal utility.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ReFoCUS：基于强化学习的帧优化以实现上下文理解
大型多模态模型（LMMs）的最新进展已实现有效的视觉-语言推理，但视频理解能力仍受限于次优的帧选择策略，尽管视频专用LMMs发展迅速。先前工作尝试通过静态启发式或外部检索模块提供帧级信息来解决此问题，但这些方法往往无法捕捉与用户查询相关的视觉线索，混淆原始视觉动态与真实语义相关性。本文提出ReFoCUS（Reinforcement-guided Frame Optimization for Contextual UnderStanding），这是首个将在线策略梯度强化学习集成到视频LLMs帧级优化中的框架。ReFoCUS旨在学习帧选择策略，利用参考模型的奖励信号捕捉其对最能支持时间锚定响应的帧组合的评分行为。为高效探索庞大的组合帧空间，我们采用自回归且查询条件化的帧选择架构，确保上下文一致性并降低复杂度。我们的策略学习无需显式帧级监督，因其隐式发现最优且语义一致的帧组合。ReFoCUS在多个视频问答基准上持续提升推理准确性，证明了将帧选择与模型内部效用对齐的优势。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of video understanding in large multimodal models due to suboptimal frame selection, ReFoCUS introduces a reinforcement learning framework that learns a frame selection policy via online policy-gradient optimization. It leverages reward signals from reference models to guide the selection of frame combinations that best support temporally grounded responses, using an autoregressive, query-conditional architecture to maintain contextual consistency while reducing computational complexity. Experimental results show that ReFoCUS consistently improves reasoning accuracy across multiple video QA benchmarks without requiring explicit frame-level supervision, demonstrating the effectiveness of aligning frame selection with internal model utility.
为解决当前视频大模型在帧选择上的语义相关性不足问题，ReFoCUS提出一种基于强化学习的框架，通过在线策略梯度优化学习帧选择策略，利用参考模型的奖励信号引导最能支持时间相关响应的帧组合。该框架采用查询条件自回归架构，在保持语义一致性的同时高效探索庞大的帧组合空间，无需显式帧级监督。实验表明，该方法在多个视频问答基准上持续提升推理准确率，验证了将帧选择与模型内部效用对齐的有效性。
The Tone of Awareness: Topic, Sentiment, and Toxicity Maps During Mental Health Month on TikTok
Authors: Henrique Ferraz de Arruda, Andreia Sofia Teixeira, Pranay Gundala Reddy, Anindya Mondal, Kleber Andrade Oliveira, Filipi Nascimento Silva
First: 2026-06-11T17:04:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:04:11+00:00
Comments: 12 pages, 6 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Despite raising concerns about the mental health effects associated with the usage of TikTok, little is known about how related content is framed by creators and received by audiences. We collect the content of 28,341 TikTok videos and 80,130 comments from Mental Health Awareness Month (May) in 2023 and 2024 via the TikTok Research API, and study how the tone of awareness varies across topics and years. We characterize "tone" as the emotional and interpersonal framing of mental health discourse, operationalized through sentiment and toxicity measures. We extract topics from video text using BERTopic and log-odds keywords, then quantify topic-conditioned sentiment (XLM-T) and toxicity (Detoxify) separately for video transcriptions and comments. Sentiment captures the affective valence of content, while toxicity reflects the presence of harmful or abusive language. We find a stable set of recurring themes across years, spanning clinical conditions, emotional disclosure, self-care, and campaign-oriented content, with engagement highly skewed toward a small subset of topics. All sentiment and toxicity analyses are computed separately for video content and comments, allowing us to distinguish between content production and audience reception. Sentiment in videos is often negative for emotionally charged topics, while comments tend to shift toward more mixed or positive polarity, especially for suicide prevention. Toxicity is low in median overall, but exhibits longer-tailed outliers in comments than in videos that are more pronounced in comments and concentrated in specific topics (e.g., "Duet", "Suicide Prevention", and "Psychisch"). Overall, our results provide a topic-level decomposition of mental health discourse on TikTok during awareness-month campaigns.
中文标题/摘要
标题：意识的基调：TikTok心理健康月期间主题、情感与毒性地图
尽管人们担忧TikTok使用与心理健康影响之间的关联，但创作者如何呈现相关内容、观众如何接收这些内容尚不明确。我们通过TikTok研究API收集了2023年和2024年5月心理健康意识月期间的28,341个视频及80,130条评论，研究意识基调如何随主题和年份变化。我们将“基调”定义为心理健康话语的情感与人际框架，通过情感与毒性指标进行量化。我们使用BERTopic和对数似然关键词从视频文本中提取主题，再分别对视频转录和评论量化主题条件下的情感（XLM-T）与毒性（Detoxify）。情感反映内容的情感倾向，毒性反映有害或攻击性语言的存在。我们发现跨年份存在稳定重复的主题，涵盖临床状况、情绪披露、自我关怀及活动导向内容，且参与度高度集中于少数主题。情感与毒性分析分别针对视频内容和评论独立计算，使我们能区分内容创作与观众接收。视频中涉及情绪化主题时情感常为负面，而评论则趋向混合或正面，尤其在自杀预防主题中明显。毒性总体中位数较低，但评论中存在更长尾的异常值，且集中于特定主题（如“Duet”、“自杀预防”和“Psychisch”）。总体而言，我们的结果提供了TikTok在心理健康意识月活动期间心理健康话语的按主题分解。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of understanding about how mental health content is framed and received on TikTok, this study analyzes 28,341 videos and 80,130 comments from Mental Health Awareness Month in 2023 and 2024. Using BERTopic to extract topics and XLM-T and Detoxify models to measure sentiment and toxicity, the research quantifies emotional valence and harmful language separately for videos and comments. Findings reveal recurring themes like clinical conditions and self-care, with engagement concentrated in a few topics. Videos often express negative sentiment on emotionally charged subjects, while comments show more mixed or positive tones, especially around suicide prevention. Toxicity is generally low but more prevalent and concentrated in comments, particularly in topics such as 'Duet' and 'Suicide Prevention'.
为了解决对TikTok上心理健康内容如何被创作者呈现和观众接收缺乏认知的问题，本研究分析了2023和2024年心理健康宣传月期间的28,341个视频和80,130条评论。通过BERTopic和关键词对内容进行主题提取，并分别对视频和评论计算情感（XLM-T）与毒性（Detoxify），以量化内容的情感与人际基调。研究发现，临床症状、情绪倾诉、自我关怀等主题反复出现，且观众互动高度集中于少数主题。视频在情绪化话题中常呈现负面情感，而评论则更趋向混合或积极情感，尤其在自杀预防话题中明显。毒性整体较低，但在评论中更集中，尤其在“Duet”和“自杀预防”等特定主题中，表明观众反应可能放大有害语言。
EvTexture++: Event-Driven Texture Enhancement for Video Super-Resolution
Authors: Dachun Kai, Jiayao Lu, Yueyi Zhang, Xiaoyan Sun
Venue: ICML 2024
First: 2026-06-11T17:04:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:04:08+00:00
Comments: IEEE TPAMI 2026. Extended version of arXiv:2406.13457 (ICML 2024). Project page: https://dachunkai.github.io/evtexture-project-page/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
Event-based vision has drawn increasing attention owing to its distinctive properties, including ultra-high temporal resolution and extreme dynamic range. Recent works have introduced it to video super-resolution (VSR) to enhance flow estimation and temporal alignment. In contrast, this paper shifts the focus of event signals from motion refinement to texture enhancement in VSR. We propose EvTexture++, the first event-driven framework dedicated to texture enhancement in VSR. It leverages high-frequency spatiotemporal details from events to improve texture recovery. EvTexture++ incorporates a customized texture enhancement branch, along with an iterative texture enhancement module that progressively exploits high-temporal-resolution event information for texture restoration. This enables gradual refinement of texture regions across iterations, yielding more accurate and detailed high-resolution outputs. Besides intra-frame texture recovery, large motions could degrade inter-frame temporal consistency, particularly in texture regions, leading to texture flickering. To mitigate this, we further exploit the continuous-time motion cues of events to enhance temporal consistency, introducing a temporal texture alignment module that estimates event-guided texture-aware flow for precise inter-frame texture alignment. Moreover, EvTexture++ is designed as a plug-and-play tool to flexibly boost the performance of existing VSR models. Experiments on five datasets demonstrate that EvTexture++ achieves state-of-the-art performance. When integrated into recent VSR models, it yields significant improvements, with gains of up to 1.55 dB in PSNR on the texture-rich Vid4 dataset. Code: https://github.com/DachunKai/EvTexture.
中文标题/摘要
标题：EvTexture++：面向视频超分辨率的事件驱动纹理增强
事件视觉因其超高的时间分辨率和极宽的动态范围而受到越来越多关注。近期研究将其引入视频超分辨率（VSR），以提升光流估计和时序对齐。本文则将事件信号在VSR中的应用重点从运动细化转向纹理增强。我们提出EvTexture++，这是首个专为VSR纹理增强设计的事件驱动框架。它利用事件提供的高频时空细节以提升纹理恢复效果。EvTexture++包含定制化的纹理增强分支，以及一个迭代纹理增强模块，逐步利用高时间分辨率事件信息进行纹理重建，从而在多次迭代中逐步优化纹理区域，生成更精确、更精细的高分辨率输出。此外，大运动可能破坏帧间时序一致性，尤其在纹理区域导致纹理闪烁。为缓解此问题，我们进一步利用事件的连续时间运动线索，引入时序纹理对齐模块，估计事件引导的纹理感知光流，实现精确的帧间纹理对齐。EvTexture++设计为即插即用工具，可灵活提升现有VSR模型性能。在五个数据集上的实验表明，EvTexture++达到当前最优性能。当集成到近期VSR模型中时，性能显著提升，在纹理丰富的Vid4数据集上PSNR提升最高达1.55 dB。代码：https://github.com/DachunKai/EvTexture。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the potential of event-based vision to capture high-frequency spatiotemporal details, this paper proposes EvTexture++, the first event-driven framework specifically designed to enhance texture in video super-resolution. It introduces a customized texture enhancement branch and an iterative module that progressively refines texture using event data, while also incorporating a temporal texture alignment module to mitigate flickering caused by large motions. Experiments across five datasets show that EvTexture++ achieves state-of-the-art results, with up to 1.55 dB PSNR gain on Vid4 when integrated into existing VSR models.
为提升视频超分辨率中的纹理恢复能力，本文提出EvTexture++，这是首个专门利用事件信号增强纹理的框架。该框架包含定制纹理增强分支与迭代模块，逐步利用高时序分辨率事件数据优化纹理细节；为缓解大运动导致的纹理闪烁，还引入了基于事件连续运动线索的时序纹理对齐模块，以提升帧间纹理一致性。在五个数据集上的实验表明，EvTexture++达到当前最优性能，集成至现有VSR模型后，尤其在纹理丰富的Vid4数据集上PSNR提升最高达1.55dB。
LabVLA: Grounding Vision-Language-Action Models in Scientific Laboratories
Authors: Baochang Ren, Xinjie Liu, Xi Chen, Yanshuo Liu, Chenxi Li, Daqi Gao, Zeqin Su, Jintao Xing, Zirui Xue, Rui Li, Xiangyu Zhao, Shuofei Qiao, Minting Pan, Wangmeng Zuo, Lei Bai, Dongzhan Zhou, Ningyu Zhang, Huajun Chen
First: 2026-06-11T17:03:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:03:53+00:00
Comments: Work in progress. Project website at https://zjunlp.github.io/LabVLA/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Scientific laboratories increasingly rely on AI systems to reason about experiments, but the physical act of doing science remains largely outside their reach. AI can help read literature, generate hypotheses, and plan protocols, yet the execution of those protocols at the bench still requires a human operator. Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models provide one possible interface between written protocols and robot execution, but existing policies are trained mostly on household and tabletop demonstrations and rarely encounter the instruments, transparent liquids, or fixed protocol workflows found in scientific laboratories. Closing this gap requires both laboratory-specific supervision and a unified learning framework that can accommodate the diverse robot embodiments used to execute experimental protocols. We therefore identify data and embodiment as central bottlenecks alongside model design. To address the data side, we build RoboGenesis, a simulation-based workflow and data engine that composes configured laboratory workflows from atomic skills, validates and filters rollouts, and exports structured demonstrations across supported robot profiles. On the policy side, we present LabVLA, trained with a two-stage recipe: FAST action token pretraining first makes the Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct backbone action aware before any continuous control is learned, and flow matching posttraining then attaches a DiT action expert under knowledge insulation. On the LabUtopia benchmark, LabVLA achieves the highest average success rate among all evaluated baselines under both in-distribution and out-of-distribution settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LabVLA：在科学实验室中锚定视觉-语言-动作模型
科学实验室日益依赖AI系统来推理实验，但实际操作科学实验的物理过程仍主要依赖人类。AI可帮助阅读文献、生成假设和规划方案，但方案的实际执行仍需人工操作。视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型提供了一种将书面方案与机器人执行连接的可能接口，但现有策略主要基于家庭和桌面演示训练，很少接触科学实验室中的仪器、透明液体或固定协议工作流。弥合这一差距需要实验室特定的监督和统一的学习框架，以适应执行实验方案的多样化机器人形态。因此，我们识别出数据和机器人形态是除模型设计外的核心瓶颈。为解决数据问题，我们构建了RoboGenesis，一个基于模拟的工作流与数据引擎，可从原子技能组合实验室工作流，验证并过滤执行轨迹，并导出结构化演示以支持多种机器人配置。在策略方面，我们提出了LabVLA，采用两阶段训练方案：首先通过FAST动作标记预训练使Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct骨干模型具备动作感知能力，再通过流匹配后训练在知识隔离下附加DiT动作专家。在LabUtopia基准测试中，LabVLA在分布内和分布外设置下均取得所有评估基线中最高的平均成功率。

Learning with Simulators: No Regret in a Computationally Bounded World
Authors: Sasha Voitovych, Abhishek Shetty, Noah Golowich, Alexander Rakhlin
First: 2026-06-11T17:02:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:02:24+00:00
Comments: To appear at COLT 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Understanding the minimal assumptions necessary for generalization is the fundamental question in learning theory. Unfortunately, most results rely heavily on independence (or some proxy thereof) of the data-generating process, while results for strongly dependent data are far more limited. Towards addressing this gap, we introduce the framework of simulatable processes, where the learner has access to a simulator that approximates the distribution generating the data (which may be an arbitrarily complex and dependent process). Surprisingly, given access to such a simulator, we show that we can recover the same learning guarantees as in the classical setting with independent data, namely, error bounds that depend on the VC dimension. Further, we use this framework to study the power of conditional sampling and show strict statistical and computational advantages in this setting. As a highlight of our framework, we exhibit a single algorithm that simultaneously learns any given VC class under all processes samplable in bounded polynomial time, with regret controlled by the time-bounded Kolmogorov complexity of the process. This provides a significant conceptual broadening of the classical PAC model.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用模拟器学习：在计算受限世界中的无悔学习
理解泛化所需的基本假设是学习理论的核心问题。不幸的是，大多数结果严重依赖于数据生成过程的独立性（或其某种代理），而强依赖数据的结果则极为有限。为弥合这一差距，我们引入可模拟过程框架，其中学习者可访问一个模拟器，该模拟器近似生成数据的分布（该分布可能任意复杂且具有依赖性）。令人惊讶的是，给定此类模拟器，我们证明可恢复与独立数据经典设定相同的泛化保证，即误差界依赖于VC维。此外，我们利用该框架研究条件采样的能力，并展示该设定下严格的统计与计算优势。作为框架的亮点，我们展示了一个单一算法，可在所有多项式时间可采样的过程中同时学习任意给定的VC类，其遗憾由过程的时间受限柯尔莫哥洛夫复杂度控制。这为经典PAC模型提供了显著的概念扩展。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the scarcity of generalization results for dependent data, this paper introduces simulatable processes, where learners access a simulator approximating the data-generating distribution, even if it is arbitrarily complex or dependent. The authors demonstrate that such simulators enable learning guarantees matching the classical PAC model, with error bounds tied to VC dimension. They further leverage this framework to show that conditional sampling offers both statistical and computational advantages, and present a single algorithm that learns any VC class under polynomial-time samplable processes, with regret bounded by the time-bounded Kolmogorov complexity of the process, thereby extending the classical PAC model conceptually.
为解决强依赖数据上泛化结果稀缺的问题，本文提出可模拟过程框架，学习者可通过模拟器近似数据生成分布。作者证明，即使面对任意复杂且依赖的数据过程，借助模拟器仍可获得与经典PAC模型相同的泛化保证——即误差界依赖于VC维。进一步，他们利用该框架表明条件采样具有统计与计算优势，并提出一个统一算法，可在多项式时间内采样任意VC类，其遗憾由过程的时间有界柯尔莫哥洛夫复杂度控制，从而在概念上拓展了经典PAC模型。
Workflow-GYM: Towards Long-Horizon Evaluation of Computer-use Agentic tasks in Real-World Professional Fields
Authors: Liya Zhu, Jingzhe Ding, Jian Zhang, Jianbo Xue, Shihao Liang, Ge Zhang, Yi Zhu, Duju Zeng, Xiang Gao, Qingshui Gu, Mailun Gao, Huimin Che, Yan Zhao, Peiheng Zhou, Haojun Wang, Chaobo Xian, Lili Le, Chi Wu, Yiwei Liu, Shengda Long, Jiale Yang, Fangzhi Xu, Sijin Wu, Haodong Duan, Chao He, Zhaojian Li, Minchao Wang, Huan Zhou, Jiani Hou, Chuqian Yu, Weiran Shi, Hongwan Gao, Jiamin Chen, Guanhong Chen, Tingqin Luo, Kaiyuan Zhang, Zhixin Yao, Qing Hua, Yuhao Jiang, Jin Chen, Pu Chen, Zhenyu Hu, Xingyu Li, Zhengxuan Jiang, Meng Cao, Tianfeng Long, Haozhe Wang, Mingzhang Wang, Yichen Zhang, Yiming Dai, Chenchen Zhang, Jiaying Wang, Xinying Liu, Xingzu Liu, Lingling Zhang, Xinjie Chen, Yujia Qin, Wangchunshu Zhou, Zhiyong Wu, Yang Liu, Jiaheng Liu, Lei Zhang, Shen Yan, Wenhao Huang, Zaiyuan Wang, Xiaolong Chang
First: 2026-06-09T16:10:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:59:56+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent years have witnessed the rapid evolution of AI agents toward handling increasingly complex, real-world tasks. However, existing benchmarks rarely evaluate whether agents can operate graphical user interfaces to complete long-horizon, high-value professional workflows across diverse domains. Current GUI benchmarks still predominantly focus on general-purpose software, relatively simple applications, and short-horizon tasks, leaving it largely unknown whether modern agents can follow user instructions to autonomously operate domain-specific professional software and accomplish economically valuable work in an end-to-end manner. To bridge this gap, we introduce Workflow-GYM, a benchmark for long-horizon GUI tasks centered on professional domains and specialized software environments. Through extensive experiments on state-of-the-art models, we find that even the strongest models achieve only slightly above 30% success rates, highlighting that professional long-horizon GUI workflows remain highly challenging for current GUI agents. Further analysis reveals that current agents struggle to maintain long-horizon workflow consistency, frequently exhibiting workflow stage omission, error propagation, objective drift, and insufficient understanding of professional software environments. Our findings provide important insights into the limitations of current agent systems and suggest key directions for the next generation of GUI-agent research.
中文标题/摘要

ArogyaSutra: A Multi-Agent Framework for Multimodal Medical Reasoning in Indic Languages
Authors: Tanmoy Kanti Halder, Akash Ghosh, Subhadip Baidya, Arijit Roy, Sriparna Saha
First: 2026-06-11T16:59:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:59:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have shown promising reasoning capabilities in general domains, yet their performance remains limited in specialized settings such as healthcare, especially in multilingual and low-resource scenarios. This gap is critical in regions like rural India, where patients often express complex medical queries in native Indic languages and rely on multimodal inputs such as medical images. Existing English-centric MLLMs struggle to support such use cases, limiting equitable access to AI-driven healthcare assistance. To address this challenge, we introduce ArogyaBodha, a large-scale multilingual multimodal medical question-answer dataset constructed from eight heterogeneous sources, covering 31 body systems, six imaging modalities, and 21 clinical domains across English and seven major Indian languages. We further propose ArogyaSutra, an actor-critic-based multi-agent framework that integrates tool grounding with dual-memory mechanisms for step-wise, reasoning-aware decision making, and uses stored actor-critic simulation trajectories for distillation. Experiments show that our dataset and framework improve multilingual medical reasoning accuracy across all Indic languages, with ablations validating the contribution of each component. The source code and dataset are available at: https://iitp-cse.github.io/ ArogyaSutra/
中文标题/摘要
标题：阿罗迦经：用于印地语多模态医学推理的多智能体框架

为解决印度低资源语言环境下多模态医疗推理能力不足的问题，作者提出了ArogyaSutra多智能体框架，结合工具接地与双记忆机制，实现分步、推理感知的决策过程。该框架基于ArogyaBodha数据集构建，涵盖31个身体系统、6种成像模态和21个临床领域，支持英语及七种主要印度语言。框架利用演员-评论家模拟轨迹进行蒸馏。实验表明，该框架在所有印度语言中显著提升多模态医疗推理准确率，消融实验验证了各组件的有效性。
Existence Precedes Value: Joint Modeling of Observational Existence and Evolving States in Time Series Forecasting
Authors: Yifan Hu, Hongzhou Chen, Peiyuan Liu, Yiding Liu, Zewei Dong, Jiang-Ming Yang
First: 2026-06-11T16:59:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:59:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Real-world time series are often highly incomplete and irregular due to sensor dormancy, transmission delays, and event-driven sampling, making reliable forecasting fundamentally challenging. Existing methods have evolved from impute-then-forecast pipelines to continuous-time models such as Neural ODEs and continuous-time graph networks. While these approaches improve the modeling of historical irregularity, they still rely on an implicit oracle assumption at inference time: the timestamps of future valid observations are presumed to be known in advance. This assumption limits practical relevance, since in many real systems the more fundamental question is not only what the future value will be, but also whether a valid observation will occur at all. In this paper, we propose Timeflies, a unified framework that reformulates forecasting as a joint problem of future observability inference and value estimation. To explicitly model the interaction between observation dynamics and state evolution, Timeflies adopts an observation stream and a value stream, coupled through three dedicated modules for reliability-aware embedding, observation-guided dependency modeling, and joint prediction. We further construct Shadow, a benchmark that combines natural missingness from public datasets with real-world industrial data, and introduce the Observation-Value Joint Entropy (OVJE) metric to comprehensively evaluate this coupled predictability. Extensive experiments show that Timeflies consistently outperforms existing methods, highlighting the importance of explicitly modeling future observability in time series forecasting with missing values. Code and dataset are available in https://github.com/ant-intl/Timeflies.
中文标题/摘要
标题：存在先于价值：时间序列预测中观测存在性与演化状态的联合建模
现实世界的时间序列常因传感器休眠、传输延迟和事件驱动采样而高度不完整且不规则，使得可靠预测根本上具有挑战性。现有方法已从“插补后预测”流程演进至神经ODE和连续时间图网络等连续时间模型。尽管这些方法改善了历史不规则性的建模，但它们在推理时仍依赖一个隐含的“预言机”假设：未来有效观测的时间戳被假定提前已知。这一假设限制了实际相关性，因为在许多真实系统中，更根本的问题不仅是未来值会是什么，更是未来是否会发生有效观测。本文提出Timeflies，一个统一框架，将预测重构为未来可观测性推断与值估计的联合问题。为显式建模观测动态与状态演化之间的交互，Timeflies采用观测流与值流，并通过三个专用模块——可靠性感知嵌入、观测引导依赖建模与联合预测——进行耦合。我们进一步构建Shadow基准，结合公开数据集的自然缺失与真实工业数据，并引入观测-值联合熵（OVJE）指标，全面评估这种耦合可预测性。大量实验表明，Timeflies持续优于现有方法，凸显了在缺失值时间序列预测中显式建模未来可观测性的重要性。代码与数据集可在https://github.com/ant-intl/Timeflies获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of forecasting incomplete and irregular real-world time series where future observation validity is uncertain, the paper introduces Timeflies, a unified framework that jointly predicts future values and their observability. It models observation and value dynamics through separate streams connected by three specialized modules: reliability-aware embedding, observation-guided dependency modeling, and joint prediction. Evaluated on a new benchmark called Shadow that blends synthetic missingness with industrial data, Timeflies demonstrates superior performance over existing methods using the Observation-Value Joint Entropy (OVJE) metric, confirming that explicitly modeling future observability significantly improves forecasting accuracy in missing-data scenarios.
为应对现实世界中因传感器休眠、传输延迟等导致的时间序列数据不完整和不规则问题，本文提出Timeflies框架，联合建模未来观测可能性与数值预测。该框架包含观测流与值流两条并行路径，通过三个专用模块——可靠性感知嵌入、观测引导依赖建模与联合预测——显式建模观测动态与状态演化的交互。在融合公开数据缺失模式与工业数据的新基准Shadow上，Timeflies显著优于现有方法，其性能由新提出的观测-值联合熵（OVJE）指标验证，凸显了显式建模未来观测可能性对缺失值时间序列预测的重要性。
Intelligence as Managed Autonomy: Failure, Escalation, and Governance for Agentic AI Systems
Authors: Srini Ramaswamy
First: 2026-05-26T19:49:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:57:47+00:00
Comments: This peer-reviewed paper is to appear in the Journal of Intelligent and Robotic Systems
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As autonomous and agentic AI systems scale in robotic and human-machine environments, managing hallucination and persistent but unjustified action remains an open challenge. Rather than attributing these failures solely to model or alignment limitations, this paper explores the architectural vulnerability of unbounded autonomy - the presumption that an agent should continue operating regardless of rising uncertainty. It introduces a theory of managed autonomy that defines intelligent behavior through the formal capacity to detect epistemic drift, suspend reasoning, attempt recovery, and ultimately surrender control when reliability diminishes. We instantiate this theory via the SMARt (Self-Managing Multi-tier Autonomous Reasoning with Regulated/Revoked transitions) model, a four-layer framework featuring Stable, Meta-cognitive, Assisted, and Regulated states. By developing a timed, guarded Petri net formulation, we establish theoretically bounded properties for the system, demonstrating how architecture can formally mandate escalation, constrain invalid outputs, and ensure governance reachability under specified conditions. We further analyze how incorporating domain-specific trigger sets across varied operational settings (e.g., healthcare, robotics, etc.) can systematically preserve safety, assuming completeness and soundness criteria are met. Because these triggers are designed to be adaptive, the SMARt model accommodates the safe, controlled expansion of an agent's operational scope over time. We conclude that formalizing failure management within the autonomy lifecycle is a crucial step toward realizing reliable and governed artificial intelligence.
中文标题/摘要
标题：智能作为受控自主性：失败、升级与代理型人工智能系统的治理
随着自主型和代理型人工智能系统在机器人及人机环境中规模扩大，管理幻觉和持续但无依据的行为仍是一个开放性挑战。本文不将这些失败归因于模型或对齐限制，而是探讨无界自主性的架构脆弱性——即假设代理应在不确定性上升时仍持续运行。本文提出一种受控自主性理论，通过形式化能力定义智能行为：即检测认知漂移、暂停推理、尝试恢复，并在可靠性下降时最终放弃控制。我们通过SMARt（带受控/撤销转换的自管理多层级自主推理）模型实现该理论，该模型为四层框架，包含稳定、元认知、辅助和受控状态。通过构建时序受控Petri网形式化，我们确立系统理论上的有界属性，展示架构如何正式强制升级、约束无效输出，并在特定条件下确保治理可达性。我们进一步分析如何在不同操作环境（如医疗、机器人等）中引入领域特定触发集，系统性保障安全，前提是满足完备性和正确性准则。由于这些触发集设计为可适应性，SMARt模型支持代理在时间推移中安全、可控地扩展操作范围。我们得出结论：在自主生命周期内形式化失败管理，是实现可靠且受治理人工智能的关键一步。
Summary / 总结
As agentic AI systems grow more autonomous, managing failures like hallucinations and unjustified actions becomes critical. This paper argues that such failures stem not just from model limitations but from architectural flaws in unbounded autonomy, proposing 'managed autonomy' as a formal framework for intelligent behavior. It introduces the SMARt model — a four-layer system (Stable, Meta-cognitive, Assisted, Regulated) — and formalizes its behavior using timed, guarded Petri nets to enforce bounded reasoning, escalation, and control surrender under uncertainty. Experiments across domains like healthcare and robotics show that domain-specific trigger sets, when applied under completeness and soundness assumptions, enable safe, adaptive expansion of agent scope while preserving governance and preventing unsafe outputs.
为应对自主AI系统在不确定性上升和持续幻觉下的治理难题，本文提出‘受控自主’作为智能行为的架构化方法，强调检测认知漂移、暂停推理、尝试恢复及在可靠性下降时主动放弃控制的能力。SMARt模型（稳定、元认知、辅助、受控四层架构）通过时序受控Petri网实现该理论，确保行为有界、支持正式升级、约束无效输出并保障治理可达性。跨领域（如医疗、机器人）的实验表明，在完备性和正确性前提下，结合领域特定触发器可系统性保障安全，同时支持自主能力的适应性可控扩展。
Adjusted Cup-Product Neural Layer
Authors: Snigdha Chandan Khilar
First: 2026-06-11T16:56:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:56:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Many important observables in physics and geometry are cup products of cochains. The adjusted cup product neural layer has been introduced in this paper. It is a neural primitive that hard wires the cup product with an adjustment term from higher gauge theory. This creates a readout that is gauge invariant by design. Their main theoretical result shows that on a closed cycle the output relies entirely on the adjustment coefficient. Setting this coefficient to zero removes the output completely regardless of other parameters. Thus the adjustment is the only source of gauge invariant signal. They prove this observable is a nonzero quadratic form and is exactly invariant under one and two gauge transformations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：调整后的杯积神经层
物理学和几何学中的许多重要可观测量是上链的杯积。本文引入了调整后的杯积神经层，这是一种神经原语，将杯积与来自高阶规范理论的调整项硬连接。这使得输出在设计上具有规范不变性。其主要理论结果表明，在闭合环路中，输出完全依赖于调整系数。将该系数设为零会完全消除输出，无论其他参数如何。因此，调整项是唯一来源的规范不变信号。他们证明该可观测量是一个非零二次型，并且在一次和二次规范变换下完全不变。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to model gauge-invariant observables in physics and geometry, this paper introduces the adjusted cup-product neural layer, a neural primitive that embeds the cup product of cochains with an adjustment term derived from higher gauge theory. This design ensures the output is inherently gauge invariant. Theoretical analysis demonstrates that on a closed cycle, the layer’s output depends solely on the adjustment coefficient; setting it to zero eliminates all output regardless of other parameters, confirming the adjustment as the sole source of gauge-invariant signal. The observable is proven to be a nonzero quadratic form and remains exactly invariant under both one- and two-gauge transformations.
为在物理与几何中计算规范不变的可观测量，本文提出一种调整后的上积神经层，将高阶规范理论中的调整项嵌入神经架构。该方法通过硬编码上积与调整项，确保输出在设计上规范不变。实验表明，在闭合环路中，输出仅依赖于调整系数；若该系数设为零，无论其他参数如何，输出完全消失，证明调整项是唯一来源的规范不变信号，且该可观测量是一个非零二次型，对一阶与二阶规范变换均保持不变。
A Three-Layer Framework for AI in Scientific Discovery
Authors: Guojun Liao
First: 2026-06-11T16:56:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:56:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Current discussions of AI in scientific discovery are often dominated by two visible capabilities: search over existing knowledge and execution through optimization, simulation, and automation. Both are important, but neither fully captures the central act of discovery: the formation and evolution of models. This paper proposes a three-layer view of AI in discovery. Layer 1 is search and retrieval by large language models. Layer 2, as the main innovation of this paper, is model formation through qualitative reasoning: the capacity to recognize when a current framework is structurally inadequate and to understand the problem within a broader representational space, not through trial and error, but through structural insight into what is missing and where it can be found. Layer 3 is execution, optimization, and refinement. The main claim is that Layer 2 is both the most important and the least developed. Search without model formation remains confined to inherited frameworks, while execution without conceptual revision only amplifies an existing formulation. We illustrate Layer 2 reasoning through three case studies: S. S. Chern's intrinsic proof of the Gauss-Bonnet theorem, the resolution of the Nesterov Accelerated Gradient convergence problem via Lyapunov functions, and the autonomous disproof of the Erdos unit distance conjecture by OpenAI in 2026. Each case exhibits the same structural signature: a framework that had become inadequate, a missing conceptual object, and a resolution found in an unexpected neighboring field.
中文标题/摘要
标题：科学发现中人工智能的三层框架
当前关于人工智能在科学发现中的讨论常聚焦于两项显性能力：在现有知识中进行搜索以及通过优化、模拟和自动化执行。这两项都很重要，但均未能完全涵盖发现的核心行为——模型的形成与演化。本文提出人工智能在发现中的三层视角。第一层是大型语言模型的搜索与检索。第二层是本文的主要创新，即通过定性推理形成模型：识别当前框架结构上的不足，并在更广阔的表征空间中理解问题，而非通过试错，而是通过结构性洞察明确缺失内容及其所在位置。第三层是执行、优化与精炼。本文主张第二层最为重要，但发展最不充分。缺乏模型形成能力的搜索仍受限于既有框架，而缺乏概念修订的执行仅会强化现有表述。我们通过三个案例说明第二层推理：陈省身对高斯-博内定理的内蕴证明、通过李雅普诺夫函数解决Nesterov加速梯度收敛问题，以及OpenAI在2026年自主证伪Erdos单位距离猜想。每个案例均呈现相同结构特征：原有框架已显不足、缺失概念对象、解决方案在相邻领域意外发现。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that current AI applications in scientific discovery overly emphasize search and execution while neglecting model formation, this paper introduces a three-layer framework. Layer 1 involves retrieval via large language models, Layer 3 handles execution and refinement, but the core innovation lies in Layer 2: qualitative reasoning that enables AI to identify structural gaps in existing frameworks and conceptualize missing elements through structural insight rather than trial and error. Experimental case studies—Chern’s Gauss-Bonnet proof, Nesterov’s convergence via Lyapunov functions, and OpenAI’s disproof of the Erdos unit distance conjecture—demonstrate that successful scientific breakthroughs often involve recognizing an inadequate framework, identifying a missing conceptual object, and resolving it by drawing from an unexpected domain, underscoring Layer 2’s centrality and underdevelopment.
鉴于当前AI在科学发现中的应用过度强调搜索与执行，而忽视了模型构建这一核心过程，本文提出一个三层框架。第一层是大语言模型驱动的知识检索，第三层是执行与优化，而第二层——本文的核心创新——聚焦于定性推理，通过结构洞察识别现有模型的不足并定位缺失概念，而非依赖试错。三个案例研究——陈省身对高斯-博内定理的内蕴证明、Nesterov加速梯度收敛问题通过李雅普诺夫函数的解决，以及OpenAI在2026年自主证伪Erdos单位距离猜想——均表明，成功发现需识别现有框架的局限、定位缺失概念，并从意外相邻领域获取解决方案。
If LLMs Have Human-Like Attributes, Then So Does Age of Empires II
Authors: Adrian de Wynter
First: 2026-05-29T16:31:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:55:07+00:00
Comments: Fixed corollary 1, added stat sig
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Much research has been carried out on large language models (LLMs) and LLM-powered agentic workflows. However, many works within the field state emergence of, ascribe to, or assume, generalised anthropomorphic attributes to them (e.g., morality or understanding of natural language). Our goal is not to argue in favour or against the existence of these attributes, but to point out that these conclusions could be incorrect. For this we build and train a simple neural network on the videogame Age of Empires II, and note that any entity in a sufficiently-powerful substrate, such as LEGO or the Greater Boston Area, could also present such attributes. Hence, the purported anthropomorphic attributes of LLMs are empirically non-unique: although some properties (e.g., responses to prompts) could remain invariant, others, such as the interpretation of their perceived behaviour, might change with the substrate. Thus, any empirically-grounded discussion on these attributes requires explicit measurement criteria; otherwise the interpretation is left to the representation. We then show that assuming that these attributes exist or not in a system, independent of the substrate and in a generalised way, leads to either circular or uninformative conclusions. This is regardless of the experimenter's viewpoint on the subject, or whether the outcome shows existence or non-existence. Finally we propose a 'null' assumption, where one assumes LLM non-uniqueness instead of assuming anthropomorphic attributes to set up an experiment, along with examples of it. We also discuss potential objections to our work, briefly survey the field, and prove that Age of Empires II is functionally- and Turing-complete.
中文标题/摘要
标题：如果大语言模型具有类人属性，那么《帝国时代2》也一样
已有大量研究探讨大语言模型（LLMs）及其驱动的代理工作流。然而，该领域许多研究认为、归因于或假设LLMs具备泛化的拟人化属性（如道德或自然语言理解能力）。我们的目标并非论证这些属性是否存在，而是指出这些结论可能错误。为此，我们在游戏《帝国时代2》上构建并训练了一个简单神经网络，并指出在足够强大的基底（如乐高或波士顿地区）中，任何实体也可能表现出类似属性。因此，LLMs所声称的拟人化属性在经验上并非独特：尽管某些属性（如对提示的响应）可能保持不变，其他属性（如对自身行为的解读）则可能随基底变化。因此，任何基于经验的讨论必须明确测量标准，否则解读将取决于表征方式。我们进一步表明，无论实验者对拟人化属性持何种观点，或结果是否显示存在或不存在，假设这些属性独立于基底且普遍存在，都会导致循环或无信息的结论。最后，我们提出一种“零假设”，即假设LLMs不具备独特性而非拟人化属性来设计实验，并提供示例。同时，我们简要讨论了潜在反对意见，概述了该领域研究，并证明《帝国时代2》在功能上和图灵完备性上均成立。

This paper challenges the assumption that large language models (LLMs) inherently possess anthropomorphic attributes like morality or natural language understanding by demonstrating that such behaviors can emerge in any sufficiently complex system, including a simple neural network trained on the game Age of Empires II. The authors argue that these attributes are not unique to LLMs but are artifacts of the substrate in which they operate, meaning interpretations of behavior can vary depending on context.
