The paper proposes a 'null' assumption—treating LLMs as non-unique systems—to ground empirical analysis and offers examples of its application, while also proving that Age of Empires II is functionally and Turing-complete.
本文质疑将大型语言模型（LLM）视为天然具备人性特征（如道德或自然语言理解）的假设，指出此类特征可能并非LLM独有，而可能在任何足够复杂的系统（如训练于《帝国时代2》的神经网络、乐高积木或波士顿地区）中出现。作者表明，尽管某些行为在不同系统中可能保持不变，但对这些行为的解读会因系统基质而异，因此在缺乏明确测量标准的情况下，关于人性特征的实证讨论是模糊的。他们证明，无论实验者持何种立场，假设LLM普遍具有或不具备人性特征都会导致循环或无信息的结论。为此，他们提出“零假设”——将LLM视为非独特系统，并提供实验范例，同时证明《帝国时代2》在功能上和图灵完备性上均成立，强化了其论点的普适性。
How reliable are LLMs when it comes to playing dice?
Authors: Luca Avena, Gianmarco Bet, Bernardo Busoni
First: 2026-06-05T17:59:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:52:41+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We investigate the probabilistic reasoning capabilities of large language models through a controlled benchmarking study on discrete probability problems. We constructed two datasets, respectively a set of standard exercises and a set of counterintuitive exercises, designed to trigger heuristic reasoning, and evaluated 8 state-of-the-art models, each tested with and without Chain-of-Thought prompting. Models achieve an average accuracy of 0.96 on standard problems but only 0.59 on counterintuitive ones. We further provide empirical evidence of token bias: performance drops by over 20% when canonical formulations are replaced by disguised variants. Embedding misleading suggestions in the prompt reduces performance by up to 34%, with no model proving immune. Taken together, the reported findings suggest that current LLMs are not yet genuine probabilistic reasoners, despite their success in advanced mathematical problems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在掷骰子游戏中，大语言模型的可靠性如何？
我们通过一项受控基准测试，研究了大语言模型在离散概率问题上的概率推理能力。我们构建了两个数据集：一组标准练习题和一组反直觉练习题，旨在触发启发式推理，并评估了8个最先进的模型（每个模型均在有和无思维链提示的情况下测试）。模型在标准问题上的平均准确率为0.96，但在反直觉问题上仅为0.59。我们进一步提供了词元偏倚的实证证据：当标准表述被伪装变体替代时，性能下降超过20%。在提示中嵌入误导性建议可使性能下降高达34%，没有任何模型能完全免疫。综合来看，这些发现表明，尽管大语言模型在高级数学问题上表现优异，但它们目前尚未成为真正的概率推理者。
Summary / 总结
This study examines how well large language models handle probabilistic reasoning by testing them on discrete probability problems, using two datasets—one with standard exercises and another with counterintuitive ones designed to reveal heuristic biases. Eight state-of-the-art models were evaluated both with and without Chain-of-Thought prompting, revealing that while they scored 0.96 on standard problems, their accuracy dropped to 0.59 on counterintuitive ones. The research also found that performance degraded by over 20% when problems were disguised, and embedding misleading suggestions in prompts reduced accuracy by up to 34%, with no model immune. These results indicate that current LLMs lack genuine probabilistic reasoning despite excelling in advanced math.
本研究通过测试离散概率问题，评估大语言模型的随机推理能力，设计了包含标准题与反直觉题的两组数据，后者旨在诱发启发式错误。八款前沿模型在启用与禁用思维链提示下均被测试，标准题平均准确率0.96，反直觉题仅0.59。研究还发现模型对词元偏见敏感，当题目伪装或嵌入误导性提示时，准确率分别下降超20%和34%，无一模型免疫。结果表明，尽管在高等数学中表现优异，当前大模型尚未具备真正的概率推理能力。
A2D2: Fine-Tuning Any-Length Discrete Diffusion for Adaptive Decoding
Authors: Sophia Tang, Yuchen Zhu, Molei Tao, Pranam Chatterjee
First: 2026-06-11T16:50:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:50:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Discrete diffusion models offer a simple and stable likelihood-based framework for sequence generation, recently extended to any-length settings via token insertion. Principled reward-guided fine-tuning for any-length discrete diffusion, however, remains largely unexplored. We introduce Fine-Tuning Any-Length Discrete Diffusion for Adaptive Decoding (A2D2), a unified framework for reward-guided fine-tuning of any-length discrete diffusion models via joint optimization of the insertion and unmasking policies together with a quality-based inference schedule. We derive the Radon-Nikodym derivative for the joint insertion-unmasking path measures, enabling theoretically guaranteed convergence to the intractable reward-tilted sequence distribution without requiring target samples. Building on this, we establish unmasking and insertion quality as tractable approaches for minimizing decoding error and introduce the Adaptive Joint Decoding (AJD) loss, which provably yields the optimal path measure that generates the reward-tilted distribution. Empirically, A2D2 improves reward optimization while enhancing generation flexibility and accuracy over prior fixed-length fine-tuning and inference-time guidance methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：A2D2：面向自适应解码的任意长度离散扩散模型微调
离散扩散模型提供了一种简单且稳定的基于似然的序列生成框架，最近通过插入标记扩展至任意长度设置。然而，针对任意长度离散扩散模型的原理性奖励引导微调仍鲜有探索。我们提出了面向自适应解码的任意长度离散扩散模型微调（A2D2），这是一个统一框架，通过联合优化插入与解码掩码策略及基于质量的推理调度，实现奖励引导的微调。我们推导了联合插入-解码掩码路径测度的Radon-Nikodym导数，从而在无需目标样本的情况下，理论上保证收敛至不可计算的奖励倾斜序列分布。在此基础上，我们确立了解码掩码与插入质量作为降低解码误差的可行方法，并引入自适应联合解码（AJD）损失，该损失可证明地生成最优路径测度，以产生奖励倾斜分布。实验证明，A2D2在奖励优化方面优于先前的固定长度微调和推理时引导方法，同时提升了生成的灵活性与准确性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for principled reward-guided fine-tuning in any-length discrete diffusion models, the authors propose A2D2, a unified framework that jointly optimizes insertion and unmasking policies alongside a quality-based inference schedule. By deriving the Radon-Nikodym derivative for joint path measures, they enable convergence to the reward-tilted sequence distribution without target samples. The Adaptive Joint Decoding (AJD) loss is introduced to minimize decoding error and provably yield the optimal path measure. Experimentally, A2D2 outperforms prior fixed-length fine-tuning and inference-time guidance methods in reward optimization, generation flexibility, and accuracy.
为解决任意长度离散扩散模型中缺乏原理化奖励引导微调的问题，作者提出A2D2框架，联合优化插入与解码策略并结合质量导向的推理调度。通过推导联合路径测度的Radon-Nikodym导数，实现无需目标样本即可收敛至奖励倾斜序列分布。引入自适应联合解码（AJD）损失函数以最小化解码误差，并可证得最优路径测度。实验表明，A2D2在奖励优化、生成灵活性与准确性上均优于先前固定长度微调及推理时引导方法。
Transformer Field Theory: A Response-Theoretic Approach to Mechanistic Interpretability
Authors: David N. Olivieri, Antonio F. Pérez Rodríguez
First: 2026-05-24T19:26:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:48:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Mechanistic interpretability often studies Transformer behavior by intervening on internal activations through activation patching, causal tracing, path patching, and steering directions. This paper develops Transformer Field Theory: a response-theoretic framework in which the residual stream of a fixed forward pass is treated as a Transformer field over layer depth and token position. In this formulation, patching becomes a localized source insertion into the Transformer field, first-order sensitivity fields predict patch effects, Green functions describe downstream propagation, and patch selection is posed as an adjoint inverse problem. Empirically, we test the theory's forward response objects in GPT-2-style autoregressive Transformers. Localized Transformer-field interventions exhibit a bounded local linear regime; first-order sensitivities predict patch effects across layer-token sites; localized sources generate structured anisotropic Transformer-field propagation; high-sensitivity sites and sliced Green operators provide reduced response descriptions; and prompt-induced Transformer-field displacements partially transfer answer behavior. These results establish sensitivities, Transformer-field responses, and sliced Green operators as practical objects for organizing patching experiments, while providing the forward mathematical basis for patch-site inference and cross-scale response transfer.
中文标题/摘要
标题：变换器场理论：一种基于响应理论的机制可解释性方法
机制可解释性通常通过激活修补、因果追踪、路径修补和方向引导等方法干预变换器内部激活来研究其行为。本文提出了变换器场理论：一种响应理论框架，将固定前向传递中的残差流视为随层深度和标记位置变化的变换器场。在此框架下，修补操作转化为变换器场中的局部源插入，一阶敏感场可预测修补效应，格林函数描述下游传播，修补选择被表述为伴随逆问题。实证上，我们在GPT-2风格的自回归变换器中测试该理论的前向响应对象。局部变换器场干预表现出有界的局部线性区域；一阶敏感性可预测跨层标记位置的修补效应；局部源生成结构化的各向异性变换器场传播；高敏感性位置与切片格林算子提供简化响应描述；提示诱导的变换器场位移部分转移答案行为。这些结果确立了敏感性、变换器场响应与切片格林算子作为组织修补实验的实用对象，同时为修补位置推断与跨尺度响应迁移提供了前向数学基础。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to mechanistically interpret Transformer behavior through targeted interventions, this paper introduces Transformer Field Theory, a response-theoretic framework that models the residual stream as a field over layer depth and token position. The method treats activation patching as localized source insertion, uses first-order sensitivity fields to predict patch effects, and employs Green functions to describe downstream propagation, framing patch selection as an adjoint inverse problem. Experiments on GPT-2-style models show that localized interventions operate within a bounded linear regime, first-order sensitivities accurately predict patch outcomes across layer-token positions, sources generate anisotropic field propagation, and high-sensitivity sites with sliced Green operators enable compact response descriptions; prompt-induced field displacements also partially transfer answer behavior, validating the framework’s utility for organizing patching experiments and enabling cross-scale response inference.
为了解释Transformer内部机制，该文提出“Transformer场理论”，将固定前向传播中的残差流建模为层深度与token位置上的场。方法将激活修补视为局部源注入，用一阶敏感性场预测修补效果，用格林函数描述下游传播，并将修补选择建模为伴随逆问题。实验表明，局部干预处于有界线性区域，一阶敏感性可准确预测跨层token位置的修补效果，局部源引发各向异性场传播，高敏感性位置与切片格林算子可压缩响应描述，且提示诱导的场位移部分转移答案行为。
Contrast-Informed Augmentation and Domain-Adversarial Training for Adult-to-Neonatal MR Reconstruction Generalization
Authors: Stephen Moore, Lara Leijser, Richard Frayne, Roberto Souza
First: 2026-06-11T16:47:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:47:53+00:00
Comments: 24 pages, 1 table, 7 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Purpose: To investigate whether contrast-informed data augmentation and domain-adversarial training improve the adult-to-neonatal generalization of the E2E-VarNet. Methods: Three training regimes were investigated: (1) adult-only training with unaugmented adult data, (2) mixed training with paired unaugmented and neonatal-informed augmented adult data, and (3) mixed training with a domain-adversarial objective. Models were trained on retrospectively undersampled multi-coil adult T2-weighted brain MR data and evaluated on neonatal and adult test data at acceleration factors $R=4$ and $R=8$ using quantitative metrics and qualitative evaluation. Feature analyses assessed whether domain-adversarial training altered the latent representations of unaugmented adult, augmented adult, and neonatal test samples. Results: Mixed training (Mixed) and mixed domain-adversarial training (Mixed-DAT) outperformed unaugmented adult-only training (Unaug-Only) when evaluated on neonatal data. At R=4, Mixed-DAT achieved the best performance (SSIM = 0.924 +/- 0.027, PSNR = 33.98 +/- 1.15 dB). At R=8, Mixed-DAT performed best when measured using SSIM (0.848 +/- 0.031 vs. 0.766 +/- 0.037 for Unaug-Only and 0.814 +/- 0.035 for Mixed) and Mixed performed best when measured using PSNR (29.56 +/- 0.83 dB vs. 26.26 +/- 0.78 dB for Unaug-Only and 29.43 +/- 0.83 dB for Mixed-DAT). Qualitative assessment of t-SNE plots suggested that Mixed-DAT increased the overlap among the latent representations of the unaugmented adult, augmented adult, and neonatal test data. Conclusion: Contrast-informed augmentation and domain-adversarial training improved adult-to-neonatal generalization of deep learning-based MR reconstruction. These findings suggest that contrast-informed data augmentation combined with adversarial training may improve robustness to domain shift in undersampled neonatal MR reconstruction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于对比信息增强与域对抗训练的成人至新生儿MR重建泛化方法
目的：探究对比信息数据增强与域对抗训练是否能提升E2E-VarNet在成人至新生儿MR重建中的泛化能力。方法：比较三种训练策略：(1) 仅使用未增强的成人数据训练，(2) 混合训练（配对未增强成人数据与新生儿信息增强的成人数据），(3) 带域对抗目标的混合训练。模型在回顾性欠采样多线圈成人T2加权脑MR数据上训练，并在加速因子R=4和R=8下，使用定量指标和定性评估在新生儿和成人测试数据上进行评估。特征分析评估域对抗训练是否改变了未增强成人、增强成人及新生儿测试样本的潜在表示。结果：混合训练（Mixed）和混合域对抗训练（Mixed-DAT）在新生儿数据评估中优于未增强成人仅训练（Unaug-Only）。在R=4时，Mixed-DAT表现最佳（SSIM=0.924±0.027，PSNR=33.98±1.15 dB）。在R=8时，Mixed-DAT在SSIM指标上表现最佳（0.848±0.031 vs. Unaug-Only的0.766±0.037和Mixed的0.814±0.035），而Mixed在PSNR指标上表现最佳（29.56±0.83 dB vs. Unaug-Only的26.26±0.78 dB和Mixed-DAT的29.43±0.83 dB）。t-SNE图的定性评估表明，Mixed-DAT增加了未增强成人、增强成人及新生儿测试数据潜在表示之间的重叠。结论：对比信息增强与域对抗训练可提升基于深度学习的MR重建在成人至新生儿间的泛化能力。这些发现表明，结合对比信息数据增强与对抗训练可能提高欠采样新生儿MR重建对域偏移的鲁棒性。
Summary / 总结
This study aimed to enhance the generalization of end-to-end VarNet for reconstructing neonatal MR images from undersampled adult data by combining contrast-informed data augmentation with domain-adversarial training. Three training approaches were compared: training solely on adult data without augmentation, training with augmented adult data informed by neonatal contrasts, and training with domain-adversarial regularization. Models were evaluated on adult and neonatal test sets at acceleration factors R=4 and R=8 using SSIM and PSNR metrics. Results showed that the domain-adversarial augmented model (Mixed-DAT) outperformed both baseline and augmented-only models on neonatal data, achieving the highest SSIM at R=4 and best PSNR at R=8 for the augmented-only model. Qualitative analysis via t-SNE revealed that Mixed-DAT improved latent space alignment across adult and neonatal domains, indicating enhanced domain-invariant feature learning.
该研究旨在通过结合对比引导的数据增强与领域对抗训练，提升端到端VarNet模型从成人欠采样数据重建新生儿MR图像的泛化能力。研究比较了三种训练方式：仅使用未增强成人数据、使用新生儿对比引导增强的成人数据、以及加入领域对抗目标。在R=4和R=8加速因子下，领域对抗训练方法（Mixed-DAT）在新生儿测试数据上表现最优，SSIM与PSNR指标均领先，且t-SNE可视化显示其在不同域间潜在表示的重叠度更高。
Causal Inference with Generative Artificial Intelligence: Application to Texts as Treatments
Authors: Kosuke Imai, Kentaro Nakamura
First: 2024-10-01T17:46:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:46:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In this paper, we demonstrate how to enhance the validity of causal inference with unstructured high-dimensional treatments like texts, by leveraging the power of generative Artificial Intelligence (GenAI). Specifically, we propose to use a deep generative model such as large language models (LLMs) to efficiently generate treatments and use their internal representation for subsequent causal effect estimation. We show that the knowledge of this true internal representation helps disentangle the treatment features of interest, such as specific sentiments and certain topics, from other possibly unknown confounding features. Unlike existing methods, the proposed GenAI-Powered Inference (GPI) methodology eliminates the need to learn causal representation from the data, and hence produces more accurate and efficient estimates. We formally establish the conditions required for the nonparametric identification of the average treatment effect, propose an estimation strategy that avoids the violation of the overlap assumption, and derive the asymptotic properties of the proposed estimator through the application of double machine learning. Finally, using an instrumental variables approach, we extend the proposed GPI methodology to the settings in which the treatment feature is based on human perception. The GPI is also applicable to text reuse where an LLM is used to regenerate existing texts. We conduct simulation and empirical studies, using the generated text data from an open-source LLM, Llama 3, to illustrate the advantages of our estimator over state-of-the-art causal representation learning algorithms.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用生成式人工智能进行因果推断：文本作为处理的应用
本文展示了如何利用生成式人工智能（GenAI）的力量，提升对非结构化高维处理（如文本）进行因果推断的有效性。具体而言，我们提出使用深度生成模型（如大型语言模型LLMs）高效生成处理，并利用其内部表征进行后续因果效应估计。我们证明，对这种真实内部表征的知识有助于分离出感兴趣的处理特征（如特定情感和某些主题），并将其与可能未知的混杂特征区分开来。与现有方法不同，所提出的GenAI赋能推断（GPI）方法无需从数据中学习因果表征，因而能产生更准确、高效的估计结果。我们正式确立了非参数识别平均处理效应所需的条件，提出了一种避免违反重叠假设的估计策略，并通过双重机器学习推导了所提估计量的渐近性质。最后，我们利用工具变量方法，将GPI方法扩展至处理特征基于人类感知的情形。GPI方法同样适用于文本重用场景，其中LLM用于重新生成现有文本。我们通过使用开源LLM Llama 3生成的文本数据进行模拟和实证研究，展示了我们的估计量相较于当前最先进的因果表征学习算法的优势。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of estimating causal effects from unstructured, high-dimensional text treatments, this paper introduces GenAI-Powered Inference (GPI), a method that uses large language models to generate treatments and exploit their internal representations for causal effect estimation. Unlike traditional approaches that require learning causal representations from data, GPI leverages the inherent structure of LLMs to disentangle target treatment features—such as sentiment or topic—from confounding variables, thereby improving estimation accuracy and efficiency. The method formally establishes nonparametric identification conditions, avoids overlap assumption violations via double machine learning, and extends to human-perception-based treatments using instrumental variables. Empirical and simulation results using Llama 3-generated text demonstrate that GPI outperforms state-of-the-art causal representation learning methods in both accuracy and efficiency.
为解决从非结构化文本治疗中估计因果效应的难题，本文提出GenAI-Powered Inference（GPI）方法，利用大语言模型生成治疗变量并借助其内部表征进行因果效应估计。与传统方法需从数据中学习因果表征不同，GPI直接利用模型的潜在结构分离目标治疗特征（如情感或主题）与混杂因素，从而获得更准确高效的估计。该方法正式确立了识别条件，通过双重机器学习避免重叠假设的违反，并扩展至基于人类感知的治疗变量，采用工具变量法。实验结果表明，使用Llama 3生成的文本数据，GPI在模拟与实证研究中均优于当前最先进的因果表征学习方法。
Appearance-Invariant Detection of Suggestive Motion via Laban Movement Descriptors
Authors: Jaehoon Ahn, Jeonghan Kong, Moon-Ryul Jung
Venue: SIGGRAPH 2026 poster
First: 2026-05-23T09:22:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:42:25+00:00
Comments: 5 pages, 2 figures, 3 tables. Extended version of a poster accepted to SIGGRAPH 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Content moderation in online multiplayer 3D virtual environments is increasingly automated, yet detection has focused on images, video, and audio, leaving suggestive motion a blind spot. We present a motion-only classification pipeline that detects suggestive and explicit movement from SMPL skeleton trajectories using Laban Movement Analysis (LMA) descriptors. On a dataset spanning everyday, artistic, suggestive, and explicit movement (17+ hours of video), a logistic regression trained on 61-feature LMA descriptors reaches 68% binary SFW/NSFW accuracy (70% random forest) under a leak-free evaluation protocol. At this level, our descriptor performs comparably to a learned video model trained on the same motion re-rendered as appearance-free video, a gray figure with no clothing, skin, or scene. The indirectness (tortuosity) of each joint's trajectory, measured as the ratio of the joint's path length to its net displacement, peaks at the suggestive tier, showing that the Direct-to-Indirect polarity of Laban's Space factor provides an interpretable marker of the shift from functional to suggestive motion. Ultimately, Laban-based kinematic descriptors offer a lightweight, interpretable approach to suggestive-motion detection: every decision decomposes into named, theory-grounded features. Because the classifier operates on pose trajectories alone, moderation can run directly on avatar poses in virtual environments, with no appearance data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于拉班运动描述符的暗示性动作外观不变检测
在线多人3D虚拟环境中的内容审核日益自动化，但现有检测主要针对图像、视频和音频，暗示性动作仍被忽视。我们提出一种仅基于运动的分类流程，利用拉班运动分析（LMA）描述符从SMPL骨架轨迹中检测暗示性和显性动作。在涵盖日常、艺术、暗示及显性动作（共17小时视频）的数据集上，基于61维LMA特征的逻辑回归模型在无泄漏评估协议下达到68%的SFW/NSFW二分类准确率（随机森林为70%）。在此水平上，我们的描述符表现与在相同运动重渲染为无外观视频（灰色人形，无服装、皮肤或场景）上训练的视频模型相当。每个关节轨迹的间接性（曲折度），即路径长度与净位移之比，在暗示性层级达到峰值，表明拉班空间因素的“直接-间接”极性可作为从功能性动作向暗示性动作转变的可解释性标志。最终，基于拉班的运动学描述符提供了一种轻量且可解释的暗示性动作检测方法：每个决策均可分解为命名明确、理论支撑的特征。由于分类器仅基于姿态轨迹运行，审核可直接在虚拟环境中的角色姿态上执行，无需外观数据。

为解决3D虚拟环境中对暗示性动作缺乏自动化检测的问题，该研究提出一种仅基于运动轨迹的分类方法，利用Laban动作分析提取SMPL骨架轨迹的61维运动特征。通过逻辑回归与随机森林模型，在涵盖日常、艺术、暗示及露骨动作的17小时视频数据集上进行训练，采用无泄漏评估协议，模型达到68%（随机森林70%）的二分类准确率，与基于无外观视频的深度学习模型表现相当。研究发现关节轨迹的曲折度（路径长度与位移比）在暗示性动作阶段达到峰值，验证了Laban空间因素中“直接-间接”极性作为运动意图转变的可解释性指标，从而实现轻量、无外观依赖的虚拟环境中角色动作审核。
QueryOcc: Query-based Self-Supervision for 3D Semantic Occupancy
Authors: Adam Lilja, Ji Lan, Junsheng Fu, Lars Hammarstrand
First: 2025-11-21T13:05:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:41:57+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Learning 3D scene geometry and semantics from images is a core challenge in computer vision and a key capability for autonomous driving. Since large-scale 3D annotation is prohibitively expensive, recent work explores self-supervised learning directly from sensor data without manual labels. Existing approaches either rely on 2D rendering consistency, where 3D structure emerges only implicitly, or on discretized voxel grids from accumulated lidar point clouds, limiting spatial precision and scalability. We introduce QueryOcc, a query-based self-supervised framework that learns continuous 3D semantic occupancy directly through independent 4D spatio-temporal queries sampled across adjacent frames. The framework supports supervision from either pseudo-point clouds derived from vision foundation models or raw lidar data. To enable long-range supervision and reasoning under constant memory, we introduce a contractive scene representation that preserves near-field detail while smoothly compressing distant regions. QueryOcc surpasses previous camera-based methods by 26% in semantic RayIoU on the self-supervised Occ3D-nuScenes benchmark while running at 11.6 FPS, demonstrating that direct 4D query supervision enables strong self-supervised occupancy learning. https://research.zenseact.com/publications/queryocc/
中文标题/摘要
标题：QueryOcc：基于查询的自监督3D语义占据学习

为降低3D标注成本并克服现有自监督方法的局限，QueryOcc提出一种基于查询的框架，通过跨相邻帧采样的4D时空查询直接学习连续的3D语义占据。该框架支持由视觉基础模型生成的伪点云或原始激光雷达数据提供的监督，并引入收缩场景表示以保留近场细节同时压缩远场区域，实现长距离推理与低内存消耗。在Occ3D-nuScenes自监督基准上，QueryOcc在语义RayIoU指标上比现有相机方法提升26%，同时保持11.6 FPS的推理速度。
Edit the Bits, Diff the Codes: Bitwise Residual Editing for Visual Autoregressive Models
Authors: Shengqiang Zhang, Ruotong Liao, Volker Tresp, Barbara Plank, Hinrich Schütze
First: 2026-06-11T16:41:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:41:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Text-guided image editing with visual autoregressive (VAR) generators requires controlling both what the model samples and where the sampled change is written back into the image code. Existing VAR editors mainly operate on token streams, features, or flat next-token logits, leaving two native structures of bitwise-residual VAR models underused: the per-bit Bernoulli prediction head and the additive multi-scale residual code field from which the image is assembled. We propose BitResEdit, a training-free editor for bitwise-residual VAR generators such as Infinity. BitEdit performs source-negative guidance by tilting the post-CFG per-bit log-odds along a source--target contrast computed on a shared edited prefix, then projects each update into a closed-form Bernoulli-KL trust region around the clean CFG sampler. ResEdit converts the sampled bits into per-scale continuous-code residuals, gates them with a localization mask, and re-injects them through the generator's native sum-of-scales. Together they couple decision-time bit guidance with combination-time code composition, so masked-out latent features are preserved exactly by code arithmetic while localized, scale-aware edits are applied inside the target region. On PIE-Bench with Infinity-2B, BitResEdit attains the strongest text alignment among same-backbone VAR editors, improving CLIP on the edited region by +1.07 over the strongest prior editor while keeping background preservation competitive with it. Ablations show BitEdit and ResEdit play complementary roles in target alignment and background preservation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：编辑比特，差异编码：面向视觉自回归模型的比特残差编辑
基于文本引导的图像编辑，使用视觉自回归（VAR）生成器时，需同时控制模型采样内容及采样结果写回图像编码的位置。现有VAR编辑器主要操作于标记流、特征或扁平下一标记对数概率，而未充分利用比特残差VAR模型的两种原生结构：逐比特伯努利预测头与用于图像合成的加性多尺度残差码域。我们提出BitResEdit，一种面向比特残差VAR生成器（如Infinity）的无训练编辑器。BitEdit通过在共享编辑前缀上计算源-目标对比，倾斜后CFG阶段的逐比特对数似然比，再将每个更新投影至围绕干净CFG采样器的闭式伯努利-KL可信区域。ResEdit将采样比特转换为逐尺度连续码残差，通过定位掩码进行门控，并通过生成器原生的多尺度求和机制重新注入。二者协同实现决策时比特引导与组合时码域合成，使掩码外潜在特征通过码域运算精确保留，同时在目标区域内施加局部、尺度感知的编辑。在PIE-Bench上使用Infinity-2B时，BitResEdit在同架构VAR编辑器中实现最强文本对齐，编辑区域CLIP得分较最强前序编辑器提升+1.07，同时保持背景保留能力与之相当。消融实验表明BitEdit与ResEdit在目标对齐与背景保留中发挥互补作用。

Motivated by the need to precisely control both content and location in text-guided image editing using visual autoregressive models, the authors introduce BitResEdit, a training-free editor tailored for bitwise-residual VAR generators like Infinity. BitEdit guides edits by adjusting per-bit log-odds based on source-target contrast from a shared prefix, constrained within a Bernoulli-KL trust region to preserve clean sampling behavior. ResEdit translates sampled bits into continuous-scale residuals, applies localization via a mask, and re-injects them through the model’s native additive code structure. Together, these components enable localized, scale-aware edits while preserving masked latent features via code arithmetic. Evaluated on PIE-Bench with Infinity-2B, BitResEdit achieves the best text alignment among same-backbone editors, boosting CLIP score in edited regions by +1.07 over prior methods while maintaining competitive background preservation.
为实现对视觉自回归模型中内容与位置的精准控制，作者提出BitResEdit，一种针对位级残差VAR生成器（如Infinity）的无训练编辑器。BitEdit通过基于共享编辑前缀计算的源-目标对比，调整后CFG阶段的位级对数似然，约束在清洁CFG采样器的伯努利-KL信任区域内。ResEdit将采样位转换为多尺度连续残差，通过定位掩码进行门控后，通过模型原生的多尺度求和结构重新注入。二者协同实现决策时的位级引导与组合时的码级重构，确保掩码区域特征精确保留的同时，实现目标区域的局部、尺度感知编辑。在PIE-Bench上使用Infinity-2B时，BitResEdit在同架构编辑器中达到最佳文本对齐效果，CLIP得分较前序方法提升+1.07，同时背景保留能力保持竞争力。
InnoEval: On Research Idea Evaluation as a Knowledge-Grounded, Multi-Perspective Reasoning Problem
Authors: Shuofei Qiao, Yunxiang Wei, Xuehai Wang, Bin Wu, Boyang Xue, Ningyu Zhang, Hossein A. Rahmani, Yanshan Wang, Qiang Zhang, Keyan Ding, Jeff Z. Pan, Huajun Chen, Emine Yilmaz
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-02-16T00:40:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:39:44+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The rapid evolution of Large Language Models has catalyzed a surge in scientific idea production, yet this leap has not been accompanied by a matching advance in idea evaluation. The fundamental nature of scientific evaluation needs knowledgeable grounding, collective deliberation, and multi-criteria decision-making. However, existing idea evaluation methods often suffer from narrow knowledge horizons, flattened evaluation dimensions, and the inherent bias in LLM-as-a-Judge. To address these, we regard idea evaluation as a knowledge-grounded, multi-perspective reasoning problem and introduce InnoEval, a deep innovation evaluation framework designed to emulate human-level idea assessment. We apply a heterogeneous deep knowledge search engine that retrieves and grounds dynamic evidence from diverse online sources. We further achieve review consensus with an innovation review board containing reviewers with distinct academic backgrounds, enabling a multi-dimensional decoupled evaluation across multiple metrics. We construct comprehensive datasets derived from authoritative peer-reviewed submissions to benchmark InnoEval. Experiments demonstrate that InnoEval can consistently outperform baselines in point-wise, pair-wise, and group-wise evaluation tasks, exhibiting judgment patterns and consensus highly aligned with human experts.
中文标题/摘要
标题：InnoEval：将研究想法评估视为知识驱动的多视角推理问题
大语言模型的快速发展催生了大量科学想法的涌现，但这一跃进并未伴随评估能力的同步提升。科学评估的本质要求知识支撑、集体讨论与多准则决策。然而现有评估方法常受限于知识面狭窄、评价维度扁平化及LLM作为裁判的固有偏见。为此，我们将想法评估视为知识驱动的多视角推理问题，提出InnoEval框架，旨在模拟人类水平的评估能力。该框架采用异构深度知识搜索引擎，从多元在线资源中检索并锚定动态证据；并通过由不同学术背景评审员组成的创新评审委员会达成评审共识，实现多维度解耦评估。我们基于权威同行评审投稿构建全面数据集以评估InnoEval。实验表明，InnoEval在点对点、成对及组内评估任务中均显著优于基线方法，其判断模式与专家共识高度一致。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need to evaluate scientific ideas more rigorously as Large Language Models generate more research proposals, InnoEval frames idea evaluation as a knowledge-grounded, multi-perspective reasoning task. It employs a heterogeneous deep knowledge search engine to retrieve and ground evidence from diverse online sources, and integrates a multi-disciplinary review board to achieve consensus across multiple evaluation metrics. Experimental results on peer-reviewed datasets show that InnoEval consistently outperforms existing baselines in point-wise, pair-wise, and group-wise evaluation tasks, with judgment patterns and consensus closely matching those of human experts.

Is It You or Your Environment? A Bayesian Inference Framework for Genomically-Anchored Personalized Physiological Interpretation
Authors: Aruna Dey, Suraj Biswas
First: 2026-06-11T16:38:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:38:38+00:00
Comments: 24 pages, 8 figures, 3 tables. Conceptual framework paper
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Personalized health AI systems face a fundamental cold-start problem: machine learning models for physiological interpretation require weeks of individual behavioral data before they can distinguish constitutional variation from environmentally driven deviation. We propose a solution grounded in causal inference and Bayesian prior design. An individual's genomic profile serves as an exogenous genetic anchor -- a domain-informed, personalized prior that is fixed at conception, immune to reverse causation, and available before a single behavioral observation is collected. The anchor initializes a Bayesian belief state over an individual's physiological set point G-hat = mu + sum(beta_i * g_i), where beta_i are GWAS-derived effect sizes and g_i are risk-allele counts. Each incoming physiological measurement P produces a non-constitutional deviation delta = P - G-hat that separates the signal attributable to environment and state from the constitutionally fixed baseline. As behavioral data accrue, the prior decays according to G-hat_t = w(t)*G-hat_genomic + [1-w(t)]*P-bar_t, transitioning from genome-dominated to empirical-baseline-dominated inference. The same observed HRV of 55 ms generates a suppression hypothesis for a person whose prior predicts 80 ms, and an enhancement hypothesis for a person whose prior predicts 30 ms -- a reversal impossible without a personalized anchor. We develop this architecture across six physiological domains, grading genomic priors by evidence strength, distinguishing robustly replicated anchors (FTO, FADS1/2, FKBP5) from contested candidate genes (SLC6A4, MAOA, DRD2). We address the inference boundary between association, Mendelian randomization, and individual token causation, and define four constraints for deployment: evidence-graded priors, dynamic decay, ancestry-matched effect sizes, and attribution rather than deterministic output.
中文标题/摘要
标题：是你还是你的环境？一种基于基因组锚定的个性化生理解读贝叶斯推断框架
个性化健康AI系统面临根本性的冷启动问题：生理解读的机器学习模型需数周个体行为数据才能区分体质变异与环境驱动的偏离。我们提出一种基于因果推断和贝叶斯先验设计的解决方案。个体基因组特征作为外生遗传锚点——一种领域知识驱动、个性化且在受孕时即固定的先验，免疫反向因果，且在任何行为观测前即可获得。该锚点初始化个体生理设定点的贝叶斯信念状态 G-hat = mu + sum(beta_i * g_i)，其中 beta_i 为GWAS推导的效应大小，g_i 为风险等位基因计数。每项新生理测量 P 产生非体质性偏离 delta = P - G-hat，从而分离环境与状态信号与体质固定基线。随着行为数据积累，先验按 G-hat_t = w(t)*G-hat_genomic + [1-w(t)]*P-bar_t 递减，从基因主导过渡到经验基线主导的推断。相同55ms的HRV，对预测80ms者生成抑制假设，对预测30ms者生成增强假设——无个性化锚点则无法实现此反转。我们在六个生理领域构建该架构，按证据强度分级基因组先验，区分稳健复制的锚点基因（FTO、FADS1/2、FKBP5）与争议候选基因（SLC6A4、MAOA、DRD2）。我们厘清关联、孟德尔随机化与个体因果推断的边界，并定义四项部署约束：证据分级先验、动态衰减、祖先匹配效应大小、归因而非确定性输出。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the cold-start challenge in personalized health AI by introducing a Bayesian inference framework that uses an individual’s genomic profile as a fixed, domain-informed prior to distinguish constitutional from environmentally driven physiological deviations. The method initializes a belief state over an individual’s physiological set point using GWAS-derived effect sizes and risk allele counts, then updates this prior over time as behavioral data accumulate, blending genomic anchors with empirical measurements via a decay function. Experimental results across six physiological domains show that the same physiological measurement (e.g., HRV of 55 ms) can trigger opposing hypotheses (suppression vs. enhancement) depending on the individual’s genomic prior, demonstrating the framework’s ability to personalize interpretation without requiring weeks of behavioral data. The study also grades genomic anchors by evidence strength, identifying robust genes like FTO and contested ones like MAOA, and defines four deployment constraints to ensure causal validity and personalization.
该论文针对个性化健康AI的冷启动问题，提出一种基于贝叶斯推断的框架，利用个体基因组作为固定、领域相关的先验，区分生理基线与环境偏差。方法通过GWAS效应值和风险等位基因计数初始化个体生理设定点，随行为数据积累动态调整先验，融合基因组锚点与经验测量。在六个生理领域实验中，相同生理指标（如55ms HRV）可因个体基因组先验不同被解释为抑制或增强，证明框架无需数周行为数据即可实现个性化解读。框架还按证据强度分级基因锚点，识别如FTO等稳健基因与如MAOA等争议基因，并定义四项部署约束以确保因果有效性与个性化。
Differentiable Thermodynamic Phase-Equilibria for Machine Learning
Authors: Karim K. Ben Hicham, Moreno Ascani, Jan G. Rittig, Alexander Mitsos
First: 2026-03-11T19:14:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:31:43+00:00
Comments: 45 pages, 27 figures, 5 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Accurate prediction of phase equilibria remains a central challenge in chemical engineering. Physics-consistent machine learning methods that incorporate thermodynamic structure into neural networks have recently shown strong performance for activity-coefficient modeling. However, extending such approaches to equilibrium data arising from an extremum principle, such as liquid-liquid equilibria, remains difficult. Here we present DISCOMAX, a differentiable algorithm for phase-equilibrium calculation that guarantees thermodynamic consistency at both training and inference, only subject to a user-specified discretization. The method combines discrete enumeration of feasible phase states with masked softmax aggregation in the backward pass, with the propagation of the true equilibrium state in the forward pass, using a straight-through gradient estimator to enable physics-consistent end-to-end learning of neural \gls{gE}-models. We show that this approach bears analogy to statistical thermodynamics, and we evaluate it on binary liquid-liquid equilibrium data where it outperforms existing surrogate-based methods, while offering a general framework for learning from different kinds of equilibrium data.
中文标题/摘要

准确预测相平衡仍是化学工程的核心挑战。近期，将热力学结构融入神经网络的物理一致机器学习方法在活度系数建模中表现出色。然而，将其扩展到由极值原理产生的平衡数据（如液-液平衡）仍具挑战性。本文提出DISCOMAX，一种可微分的相平衡计算算法，该算法在训练和推理阶段均保证热力学一致性，仅受限于用户指定的离散化。该方法结合了可行相态的离散枚举与反向传播中的掩码softmax聚合，以及前向传播中真实平衡态的传播，利用直通梯度估计器实现对神经\gls{gE}-模型的物理一致端到端学习。我们表明该方法与统计热力学具有类比性，并在二元液-液平衡数据上评估其性能，结果优于现有代理模型方法，同时提供了一种适用于学习不同类型平衡数据的通用框架。

Motivated by the need for physics-consistent machine learning models to predict phase equilibria, this work introduces DISCOMAX, a differentiable algorithm that ensures thermodynamic consistency during both training and inference, limited only by user-defined discretization. The method combines discrete enumeration of feasible phase states with masked softmax aggregation in the backward pass and forward propagation of the true equilibrium state, enabled by a straight-through gradient estimator. Experimental results demonstrate that DISCOMAX outperforms existing surrogate-based methods on binary liquid-liquid equilibrium data, while providing a generalizable framework for learning from various equilibrium datasets.
为解决机器学习中预测相平衡尤其是受极值原理约束的液-液平衡问题，作者提出了DISCOMAX算法，该算法在训练和推理阶段均保证热力学一致性。方法结合可行相态的离散枚举、反向传播中的掩码softmax聚合，以及前向传播中真实平衡态的传递，利用直通梯度估计器实现神经gE模型的端到端物理一致学习。实验表明，该方法在二元液-液平衡数据上优于现有代理模型方法，同时提供了一种适用于多种平衡数据学习的通用框架。
Using Seismic Statistical Features and VQ-VAE to Improve Spatiotemporal Seismicity Predictability
Authors: Wei Quan, Denise Gorse
First: 2026-06-08T18:43:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:31:27+00:00
Comments: Title updated from "Spatiotemporal Seismic Hazard Assessment Using VQ-VAE and Seismic Statistical Features" to "Using Seismic Statistical Features and VQ-VAE to Improve Spatiotemporal Seismicity Predictability" in v2 to better reflect the focus of the paper. The content is unchanged apart from the title and minor copyediting
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In this paper we build upon a previous study in which we demonstrated, using XGBoost and earthquake catalogue data from Japan and Chile, that a set of 60 seismic statistical features (SSFs) had much greater predictive value than a set of 428 generic time series features from the tsfresh package. We here extend this previous work in two key ways, focusing on data from Japan as a large dataset is necessary in order to allow for the training of a deep learning (autoencoder) model. First, we move from whole-region prediction (considering, for each candidate event, the likelihood of an event M $\geq$ 5.0 anywhere in the region in the next 15 days) to localised predictions in which both the region of feature computation and the region of prediction are restricted to a circle of radius 24 km around the candidate event, and we show that performance remains excellent, similar to our previous whole-region study for the same area. Second, we here couple this proven set of SSFs, based on one-dimensional (catalogue) data, with a novel feature based on two-dimensional seismic maps, obtained by training a VQ-VAE model to reproduce such maps as output and identifying a measure of its error in doing so with a localised build-up of crustal stress. We show that while localised prediction based on SSFs can be effective alone, with test AUC values as high as those obtained in the case of Japan in our previous whole-region study, the inclusion of the new natively-spatial VQ-VAE-derived feature, top-ranked by SHAP analysis, can enhance performance and additionally appears to near-wholly replace the traditionally-computed $b$-value in terms of feature usage.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用地震统计特征与VQ-VAE提升时空地震活动预测能力
本文在先前研究基础上进一步拓展，先前研究已证明，使用XGBoost和日本、智利的地震目录数据，60个地震统计特征（SSFs）的预测价值远高于tsfresh包中的428个通用时序特征。本研究在两个关键方面扩展了前作：首先，聚焦日本数据（需大样本以训练深度学习自动编码器模型），将全区域预测（针对每个候选事件，预测未来15天内区域内任意位置发生M≥5.0地震的概率）转为局部预测，即特征计算与预测区域均限定在候选事件周围24公里半径圆内，结果表明性能仍保持优异，与先前全区域研究在相同区域的结果相当。其次，将已验证的基于一维（目录）数据的SSFs与一种基于二维地震地图的新特征结合——该特征通过训练VQ-VAE模型重建地震地图，并用其重建误差衡量局部地壳应力累积。结果显示，仅基于SSFs的局部预测已可取得高测试AUC值（与先前全区域研究在日本的结果相当），而新增的原生空间VQ-VAE衍生特征（SHAP分析排名最高）可进一步提升性能，并在特征使用上几乎完全替代传统计算的b值。

Motivated by the need to improve earthquake predictability using more spatially informed features, this study extends prior work by applying localized predictions within 24 km radius circles around candidate events, using seismic statistical features (SSFs) and a novel VQ-VAE model trained to reconstruct 2D seismic maps as a proxy for crustal stress buildup.
